# KAIROS COMPLETE BACKEND AUDIT & OPTIMIZATION REVIEW
Project: Kairos — Multi-Paper Literature Synthesis & Contradiction Mapping Engine (OPEN//BUILD Hackathon 2026)

Reviewer: Gemini Argon 4 (Google AI Studio, Thinking=High, Temperature 0.2)

## CONTEXT & OBJECTIVE
We have completed Milestone D5 on branch `feat/d5-orchestration`. The entire backend engine is implemented across all 5 milestones:
- D1: Contract-first Pydantic v2 schemas (extra="forbid"), error handlers, demo preset endpoints, fixtures.
- D2: High-throughput ingestion coordinator, PyMuPDF parser (sort=False, _MUPDF_LOCK), 5-format arXiv fetcher, semantic sectioner.
- D3: Open-weight LLM failover client (Groq -> HF -> Ollama), verbatim claim extraction, RapidFuzz-backed quote verifier anti-hallucination filter.
- D4: Dense vector embeddings (BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5), same-paper masked cosine similarity, pre-cap research gap detection, agglomerative clustering, strict batched NLI referee.
- D5: End-to-end 5-stage pipeline orchestrator, SSE progress streaming with late-subscriber replay and keep-alive pings, deterministic consensus scoring, Stage 4 LLM consensus writer with edge-ID validation, per-IP sliding-window rate limiting, and production Docker deployment.

Zero closed-source models are invoked at runtime (strictly meta-llama/Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, meta-llama/Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, and BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5).

## WHAT WE ARE ASKING YOU (ARGON 4) TO REVIEW:
Please conduct an extensive, end-to-end senior architectural and code-level critique across two major dimensions:

### PART 1: COMPREHENSIVE ARCHITECTURAL & CONTRACT AUDIT
Evaluate the complete codebase against:
1. API Contracts (docs/shared/02-api-contracts.md):
   - Invariant enforcement: strict ID formats (p1-c1, e-p1c1-p2c1, gap-p1-c1, cl-1), lower-numbered paper ordering in edges, cluster discard rules, StageLatency.total_ms summation, error response envelopes.
2. Open-Weight Runtime Model Integrity (docs/shared/03-open-weight-models.md):
   - Zero-closed-model verification, prompt templates fidelity, anti-hallucination quote verifier thresholds, token limits, temperature choices.
3. Robustness, Reliability & Fault Tolerance:
   - Failure isolation (e.g., corrupt PDFs, arXiv timeouts, provider 401/429/503 errors, rate-limit bursts).
   - Async concurrency patterns: race conditions in JobStore, SSE disconnection cleanup, thread safety around PyMuPDF ONNX runtimes.
   - Bounded memory and resources: job TTL cleanup, upload stream handling, task reference lifecycles.
4. Security & Production Readiness:
   - Secret scrubbing (redaction of API keys in logs/errors), CORS security, reverse-proxy IP spoofing defenses, Docker image caching & non-root permissions.

### PART 2: CONCRETE OPTIMIZATION & IMPROVEMENT RECOMMENDATIONS
Identify high-impact optimizations across:
1. Latency & Throughput:
   - Opportunities to parallelize, pipeline, or batch operations (e.g. LLM extraction, embedding generation, referee evaluation).
2. Memory & Scaling:
   - Large payload handling, garbage collection, ONNX model memory pooling.
3. Prompt & NLI Accuracy:
   - Improvements to extraction, referee, or consensus prompts to boost synthesis quality and reduce retry rates.
4. Resilience & Developer Experience:
   - Edge case handling, observability/telemetry, client reconnect behavior.

### REQUESTED OUTPUT FORMAT
1. EXECUTIVE VERDICT & SCORECARD:
   - Overall production readiness assessment.
   - Category scores (Contracts, Reliability, Performance, Open-Weight Adherence, Code Quality) out of 10.
2. FINDINGS TABLE (IF ANY DEFECTS/ISSUES REMAIN):
   - [ID | Severity (BLOCKER/MAJOR/MINOR/NIT) | Location (file:line) | Finding | Exact minimal fix]
3. PRIORITIZED OPTIMIZATION ROADMAP:
   - Concrete, actionable optimizations ranked by (Impact vs Effort), with code snippets or architectural diffs where applicable.
4. DEMO-DAY READINESS CHECKLIST:
   - Final audit points to guarantee zero glitches during live hackathon evaluation.

# 01 — System Architecture

End-to-end design of Kairos: ingestion, four model stages, graph construction, latency budget and failure handling. Field names refer to [`02-api-contracts.md`](02-api-contracts.md); prompts and model ids are in [`03-open-weight-models.md`](03-open-weight-models.md).

## 1. Dataflow

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    U["User: 2–10 PDFs and/or arXiv ids"] --> V["Validate (count, magic bytes %PDF-, size)"]
    V --> I1["PDF bytes"]
    V --> I2["arXiv fetch (httpx)"]
    I2 --> I1
    I1 --> P["PyMuPDF: per-page text with page numbers<br/>dehyphenate, strip references"]
    P --> S["Sectioner: ABSTRACT, METHODOLOGY, EXPERIMENTAL_SETUP,<br/>RESULTS, DISCUSSION, CONCLUSION, LIMITATIONS<br/>char budget per section"]
    S --> X["Stage 1 — Extraction (LLM, 1 call per paper, concurrent)<br/>metadata + 4–6 claims, JSON"]
    X --> Q["Quote verifier<br/>exact or fuzzy match to page text; drop failures"]
    Q --> E["Stage 2 — Embeddings (BGE-small, local)<br/>L2-normalised 384-d vectors"]
    E --> M["Cosine matrix (N×N), same-paper masked"]
    M --> G["Research gaps<br/>no neighbour ≥ threshold"]
    M --> K["Candidate pairs<br/>top-k per other paper, ≥ threshold, cap"]
    M --> C["Agglomerative clustering<br/>topic clusters"]
    K --> R["Stage 3 — NLI referee (LLM, batched pairs, concurrent)<br/>AGREE | CONTRADICT | METHODOLOGY_DELTA | UNRELATED"]
    R --> GB["Graph builder<br/>edges, ids, ordering"]
    C --> GB
    G --> CW["Stage 4 — Consensus writer (LLM, 1 call)<br/>+ deterministic score"]
    GB --> CW
    CW --> OUT["AnalysisResult (JSON) → REST / SSE → frontend"]
```

Presets bypass the whole pipeline: `GET /api/v1/demo/{preset_id}` returns a pre-baked `AnalysisResult` from `/fixtures`.

## 2. Ingestion

### 2.1 Validation
- Count: 2–10 papers after de-duplication (by arXiv id and by SHA-256 of file bytes).
- A file is a PDF only if its first 5 bytes are `%PDF-` (extension alone is not trusted).
- Size: ≤ `MAX_FILE_MB` per file, ≤ `MAX_TOTAL_MB` per request.

### 2.2 arXiv
Accepted forms: `2401.01234`, `2401.01234v2`, `arxiv.org/abs/2401.01234`, `arxiv.org/pdf/2401.01234v2(.pdf)`, old-style `cs.CL/0501001`. Normalise to the bare id (version kept if present). Download `https://arxiv.org/pdf/{id}` with `httpx.AsyncClient`, `User-Agent: Kairos/1.0 (OPEN//BUILD hackathon; contact via GitHub)`, timeout 30 s, `follow_redirects=True`, concurrency `ARXIV_CONCURRENCY` (default 3). Reject responses that do not start with `%PDF-`.

### 2.3 Text extraction (PyMuPDF)
- For each page: `page.get_text("text", sort=True)` (`sort=True` keeps two-column reading order reasonable).
- Remove soft hyphenation: `re.sub(r"-\n(?=[a-z])", "", text)`; collapse single newlines inside paragraphs to spaces but keep blank-line paragraph breaks.
- Keep a dict `page_texts: dict[int, str]` (1-indexed). **This cleaned text is the canonical source** for both the LLM input and quote verification.
- **Scanned PDF check:** if the mean extractable characters per page is < 200, raise `NO_TEXT_LAYER` for that paper.
- Metadata hints: PyMuPDF `doc.metadata` title/author are used only as hints; the LLM extraction is authoritative.

### 2.4 Section detection
Heading regex (line-anchored, case-insensitive), optional numbering such as `3.`, `3.1`, `III.`:

```
^\s*(?:\d+(?:\.\d+)*\.?|[IVX]+\.)?\s*(abstract|introduction|related work|background|method(?:s|ology)?|approach|materials and methods|experimental setup|experiments?|implementation details|results?|evaluation|discussion|conclusions?|limitations?|future work|references|bibliography|acknowledg(?:e)?ments?)\s*$
```

Mapping to `SectionName`:

| Heading(s) | SectionName |
| :--- | :--- |
| abstract | `ABSTRACT` |
| method, methods, methodology, approach, materials and methods | `METHODOLOGY` |
| experimental setup, experiment(s), implementation details | `EXPERIMENTAL_SETUP` |
| result(s), evaluation | `RESULTS` |
| discussion | `DISCUSSION` |
| conclusion(s), future work | `CONCLUSION` |
| limitation(s) | `LIMITATIONS` |
| introduction, related work, background | `OTHER` (kept only as overflow, lowest priority) |
| references, bibliography, acknowledgements | **stop**: discard everything from here on |

Text before the first heading on page 1 is treated as `ABSTRACT` (up to 3000 chars).

### 2.5 Character budget per paper (`MAX_PAPER_CHARS = 24000` ≈ 6k tokens)

| Group | Budget | Truncation |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `ABSTRACT` | 3000 | keep head |
| `METHODOLOGY` + `EXPERIMENTAL_SETUP` | 7000 | split evenly, keep head of each |
| `RESULTS` | 8000 | keep head |
| `DISCUSSION` + `CONCLUSION` + `LIMITATIONS` | 6000 | split evenly, keep head of each |

If no headings are detected, fall back to head/tail: first 6000 chars (labelled `ABSTRACT`) and last 18000 chars before references (labelled `OTHER`), and omit those sections from `sections_found` except those truly detected.

### 2.6 Model-ready text format
The extractor receives text with explicit markers so page numbers and sections can be cited exactly:

```
[[SECTION: ABSTRACT]]
[[PAGE 1]]
...text...
[[SECTION: RESULTS]]
[[PAGE 6]]
...text...
```
A `[[PAGE n]]` marker is inserted at the start of every page's text inside a section. Markers are never part of a valid verbatim quote.

## 3. Stage 1 — Structured extraction
One LLM call per paper, run concurrently (bounded by `LLM_MAX_CONCURRENCY`). Output is validated with Pydantic, then each claim passes the **quote verifier** (`03-open-weight-models.md` §6). After verification a paper must retain at least 2 claims; otherwise a warning is added, and with 0 claims the paper is excluded. If fewer than 2 papers remain, the job fails with `TOO_FEW_PAPERS`.

## 4. Stage 2 — Embeddings and similarity math

Let `N` be the number of verified claims across all papers and `E ∈ R^{N×384}` the L2-normalised embedding matrix (BGE vectors are normalised before use, so cosine similarity equals the dot product):

```
S = E · Eᵀ                      # N×N cosine similarity matrix, computed with NumPy in < 50 ms for N ≤ 60
S[i, j] = -1  if paper(i) == paper(j)    # mask same-paper pairs
```

**Pair counts for 8 papers × 5 claims (N = 40):**

| Quantity | Value |
| :--- | :--- |
| All unordered claim pairs | C(40, 2) = 780 |
| Same-paper pairs (not compared) | 8 × C(5, 2) = 80 |
| **Cross-paper pairs (naive LLM calls)** | **700** |
| Upper bound after top-k=2 per other paper | 40 × 7 × 2 / 2 = 280 |
| Expected after `SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD` | a small fraction; the *actual* value is measured and returned in `stats` |
| Hard cap sent to the referee | `MAX_REFEREE_PAIRS` (default 60), highest cosine first |

**Candidate selection:** for each claim `i` and each *other* paper `p`, take the top `TOP_K_PER_PAPER` claims of `p` by `S[i, ·]`; keep those with `S ≥ SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD`; de-duplicate unordered pairs; sort by similarity descending; truncate to `MAX_REFEREE_PAIRS` (adding a warning if truncated).

**Research gaps:** claim `i` is a gap iff `max_j S[i, j] < SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD`, computed **before** top-k and cap truncation.

**Clustering:** `AgglomerativeClustering(n_clusters=None, metric="cosine", linkage="average", distance_threshold=CLUSTER_DISTANCE_THRESHOLD)` on `E`. Clusters containing fewer than 2 claims **or fewer than 2 distinct papers** are discarded (their claims get `cluster_id = None`). Cluster `label` = `topic_hint` of the claim closest to the cluster centroid. `mean_similarity` = mean pairwise cosine within the cluster.

`SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD` (default 0.65) is a **starting value**; D4 must tune it on real fixtures (see `03-open-weight-models.md` §4).

## 5. Stage 3 — Batched NLI referee

- Pairs are packed `REFEREE_PAIRS_PER_CALL` (default 4) per request. Each pair includes both claims, their quotes, and each paper's `dataset` and `methodology` (needed to recognise `METHODOLOGY_DELTA`).
- Calls run concurrently under the same semaphore as extraction. Output: `{"results":[{pair_id, relation, confidence, explanation}]}`.
- `UNRELATED` results are discarded (not in the public enum). Results with a missing `pair_id`, unknown relation or invalid confidence are retried once as single-pair calls, then dropped.
- Edge direction: source = claim from the lower-numbered paper.
- Calls needed = `ceil(pairs / REFEREE_PAIRS_PER_CALL)`; for the cap of 60 that is 15 calls.

## 6. Stage 4 — Consensus
- `consensus_score` is computed deterministically (`02-api-contracts.md` §6).
- One LLM call writes `executive_summary`, `agreed_findings`, `top_contradictions[].headline` and `recommended_next_steps` from the structured edges and gaps only (never from raw PDFs). Returned ids are validated against real edges/gaps.
- If this call fails, a template-based fallback summary is produced from the data (e.g. "N papers analysed, A agreements, C contradictions…") so the pipeline still completes.

## 7. Concurrency and job model

- FastAPI + `asyncio`. CPU-bound steps (PyMuPDF parsing, embedding, clustering) run via `asyncio.to_thread`.
- `POST /analyze` creates a `Job` in an in-memory dict (TTL `JOB_TTL_MINUTES`) and schedules `run_pipeline` with `asyncio.create_task`. Progress events are pushed to an `asyncio.Queue` per subscriber; SSE endpoints consume it and replay the last event for late subscribers.
- Hard job timeout `JOB_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` (default 90) → `failed` event with `INTERNAL_ERROR` and a message suggesting presets.
- Single-process deployment (one uvicorn worker) so the in-memory store is coherent.

## 8. Latency budget (8 papers, live)

Targets assume an unthrottled provider tier. Free tiers have request/token rate limits, so wall time can be longer; the UI shows staged progress and presets are the safety net.

| Stage | Budget |
| :--- | :--- |
| Ingestion (validate, parse, section) | ≤ 2.0 s |
| Stage 1 extraction (8 calls, concurrency 6) | ≤ 6.0 s |
| Stage 2 embeddings + similarity | ≤ 0.6 s (model preloaded at startup) |
| Clustering | ≤ 0.1 s |
| Stage 3 referee (≤ 15 calls, concurrency 6) | ≤ 4.0 s |
| Stage 4 consensus | ≤ 1.5 s |
| **Total live target** | **< 15 s** (hard timeout 90 s) |
| Cached preset (`/demo/{id}`) | **< 100 ms** |

Rate-limit handling: the LLM client honours `Retry-After` once, then fails over to the next provider in `LLM_PROVIDER_CHAIN`. Optional `LLM_MAX_RPM` enables a client-side limiter.

## 9. Failure modes and mitigations

| Failure | Mitigation |
| :--- | :--- |
| Provider 429/5xx/timeout | Retry once, then next provider (Groq → HF → Ollama) |
| Invalid JSON | One corrective retry with the parse error appended, then next provider |
| Hallucinated quote | Quote verifier drops the claim; counts logged to `warnings` |
| Scanned PDF | Skip paper, warn; fail only if < 2 papers remain |
| arXiv failure | Skip that id, warn |
| Too many referee pairs | Cap and warn |
| Cold-start hosting | Presets served from disk; warm the service before Demo Day |
| Everything down | Frontend falls back to bundled preset fixtures |

## 10. Security and privacy
- Uploaded files are processed in memory/temp storage and deleted when the job ends; they are never logged.
- No API keys in the repo; keys only in `.env` / hosting secrets.
- Rate limit `ANALYZE_RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE` per IP on `POST /analyze`.
- CORS restricted to `CORS_ALLOW_ORIGINS`.
- Error messages to clients are generic for `INTERNAL_ERROR`; details only in server logs.

================================================================================
FILE: docs/shared/02-api-contracts.md (1055 lines)
================================================================================
# 02 — API Contracts (Single Source of Truth)

> **Owner:** DuroT (backend). **Consumers:** Peace (frontend) and every coding agent.
> **Schema version:** `1.0.0`. Any change requires an entry in [`../requests.md`](../requests.md), approval from **both** developers, and a `schema_version` bump in `backend/app/schemas.py` **and** `frontend/src/lib/types.ts` in the same PR cycle.

## 0. Conventions (read before anything else)

1. **JSON keys are `snake_case` everywhere** (Python, wire format, and TypeScript). The frontend MUST NOT convert to camelCase.
2. **Enums are `UPPER_SNAKE_CASE` strings.** They are identical in Python and TypeScript. Never invent new values on one side only.
3. **Every claim and every edge carries a `Provenance`** object (`paper_id`, `paper_title`, `section`, `page_number`, `verbatim_quote`). No exceptions. The backend drops any claim whose quote cannot be verified against the source PDF text (see `03-open-weight-models.md` §6).
4. **Pydantic models use `extra="forbid"`.** Unknown keys are validation errors. Fixtures must match exactly.
5. **Four semantic signals** are bound to enums and must stay visually distinct in the UI: `AGREE`, `CONTRADICT`, `METHODOLOGY_DELTA` (edge relations) and `RESEARCH_GAP` (the `signal` field of a `ResearchGap`).
6. **Fixtures are honest.** Every `AnalysisResult` has `fixture_provenance`:
   - `SYNTHETIC_PLACEHOLDER` — invented data used only to unblock development. The UI MUST show a visible "Synthetic placeholder data" banner. Never shown at Demo Day.
   - `REAL_PIPELINE_OUTPUT` — produced by running the real pipeline on real, openly licensed papers, with quotes verified. Only these may be used at Demo Day.
   - `NOT_APPLICABLE` — live analysis of user-supplied papers.
7. **Limits:** min 2 papers, max 10 papers, max 20 MB per PDF, max 80 MB per request. Demo presets contain 6–8 papers.

---

## 1. Pydantic v2 models (Python 3.11+)

Copy this block verbatim into `backend/app/schemas.py` (Milestone D1). A validation script in the repo (`backend/tests/test_contract.py`) loads `fixtures/contract_sample.json` through `AnalysisResult` on every test run.

```python
# backend/app/schemas.py
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum
from typing import Literal, Optional

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field

SCHEMA_VERSION = "1.0.0"


class ContractModel(BaseModel):
    """Base class: unknown keys are rejected so fixtures and API never drift."""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", use_enum_values=False)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Enums
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class RelationType(str, Enum):
    AGREE = "AGREE"
    CONTRADICT = "CONTRADICT"
    METHODOLOGY_DELTA = "METHODOLOGY_DELTA"


class SectionName(str, Enum):
    ABSTRACT = "ABSTRACT"
    METHODOLOGY = "METHODOLOGY"
    EXPERIMENTAL_SETUP = "EXPERIMENTAL_SETUP"
    RESULTS = "RESULTS"
    DISCUSSION = "DISCUSSION"
    CONCLUSION = "CONCLUSION"
    LIMITATIONS = "LIMITATIONS"
    OTHER = "OTHER"


class PaperSource(str, Enum):
    UPLOAD = "UPLOAD"
    ARXIV = "ARXIV"
    DEMO = "DEMO"


class ClaimType(str, Enum):
    EMPIRICAL_RESULT = "EMPIRICAL_RESULT"
    METHOD = "METHOD"
    LIMITATION = "LIMITATION"
    CONCLUSION = "CONCLUSION"


class AnalysisMode(str, Enum):
    LIVE = "LIVE"
    DEMO = "DEMO"


class FixtureProvenance(str, Enum):
    SYNTHETIC_PLACEHOLDER = "SYNTHETIC_PLACEHOLDER"
    REAL_PIPELINE_OUTPUT = "REAL_PIPELINE_OUTPUT"
    NOT_APPLICABLE = "NOT_APPLICABLE"


class ModelRole(str, Enum):
    EXTRACTION = "EXTRACTION"
    EMBEDDING = "EMBEDDING"
    REFEREE = "REFEREE"
    CONSENSUS_WRITER = "CONSENSUS_WRITER"


class PipelineStage(str, Enum):
    INGESTION = "INGESTION"
    EXTRACTION = "EXTRACTION"
    EMBEDDING = "EMBEDDING"
    CLUSTERING = "CLUSTERING"
    REFEREE = "REFEREE"
    CONSENSUS = "CONSENSUS"
    DONE = "DONE"


class JobStatus(str, Enum):
    QUEUED = "QUEUED"
    RUNNING = "RUNNING"
    COMPLETED = "COMPLETED"
    FAILED = "FAILED"


class ErrorCode(str, Enum):
    INVALID_FILE_TYPE = "INVALID_FILE_TYPE"
    FILE_TOO_LARGE = "FILE_TOO_LARGE"
    TOO_FEW_PAPERS = "TOO_FEW_PAPERS"
    TOO_MANY_PAPERS = "TOO_MANY_PAPERS"
    INVALID_ARXIV_ID = "INVALID_ARXIV_ID"
    ARXIV_FETCH_FAILED = "ARXIV_FETCH_FAILED"
    PDF_PARSE_FAILED = "PDF_PARSE_FAILED"
    NO_TEXT_LAYER = "NO_TEXT_LAYER"
    LLM_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE = "LLM_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE"
    LLM_INVALID_JSON = "LLM_INVALID_JSON"
    ANALYSIS_NOT_FOUND = "ANALYSIS_NOT_FOUND"
    PRESET_NOT_FOUND = "PRESET_NOT_FOUND"
    RATE_LIMITED = "RATE_LIMITED"
    INTERNAL_ERROR = "INTERNAL_ERROR"


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Core data models
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class Provenance(ContractModel):
    paper_id: str
    paper_title: str
    section: SectionName
    page_number: int = Field(ge=1)
    verbatim_quote: str = Field(min_length=10, max_length=600)


class PaperMetadata(ContractModel):
    paper_id: str = Field(pattern=r"^p[0-9]{1,2}$")  # "p1".."p10", stable order of upload
    short_label: str = Field(pattern=r"^P[0-9]{1,2}$")  # "P1".."P10"
    citation_label: str  # e.g. "Smith et al. (2024)"; "Example A (2024)" in placeholders
    palette_index: int = Field(ge=0, le=9)  # index into the 10-colour paper palette
    source: PaperSource
    arxiv_id: Optional[str] = None
    filename: Optional[str] = None
    title: str
    authors: list[str]
    year: Optional[int] = Field(default=None, ge=1900, le=2100)
    page_count: int = Field(ge=1)
    sections_found: list[SectionName]
    dataset: str  # "Not reported" when absent
    sample_size: Optional[str] = None  # free text, e.g. "n=1,200 scans"
    methodology: str
    primary_metric: str
    limitations: str
    core_conclusion: str
    extraction_model: str


class ExtractedClaim(ContractModel):
    claim_id: str  # "{paper_id}-c{n}", e.g. "p3-c2"
    paper_id: str
    claim_type: ClaimType
    topic_hint: str = Field(max_length=80)  # short topic phrase from the extractor
    statement: str = Field(min_length=10, max_length=300)
    metric_value: Optional[str] = None  # e.g. "94.2% accuracy"
    cluster_id: Optional[str] = None  # None when unclustered
    is_research_gap: bool = False
    provenance: Provenance


class TopicCluster(ContractModel):
    cluster_id: str  # "cl-1", "cl-2", ...
    label: str  # topic_hint of the claim closest to the cluster centroid
    claim_ids: list[str] = Field(min_length=2)
    paper_ids: list[str] = Field(min_length=2)
    mean_similarity: float = Field(ge=0.0, le=1.0)


class ClaimComparisonEdge(ContractModel):
    edge_id: str  # "e-{source_claim_id without dash}-{target_claim_id without dash}"
    source_claim_id: str  # claim from the lower-numbered paper
    target_claim_id: str
    source: Provenance
    target: Provenance
    relation: RelationType
    confidence: float = Field(ge=0.0, le=1.0)
    cosine_similarity: float = Field(ge=0.0, le=1.0)
    explanation: str = Field(min_length=10, max_length=500)
    cluster_id: Optional[str] = None
    referee_model: str


class ResearchGap(ContractModel):
    signal: Literal["RESEARCH_GAP"] = "RESEARCH_GAP"
    gap_id: str  # "gap-{claim_id}"
    claim_id: str
    paper_id: str
    statement: str
    provenance: Provenance
    best_match_similarity: float = Field(ge=0.0, le=1.0)
    reason: str


class TopContradiction(ContractModel):
    edge_id: str
    headline: str = Field(max_length=200)


class ConsensusReport(ContractModel):
    consensus_score: int = Field(ge=0, le=100)  # computed deterministically, see §6
    executive_summary: str = Field(max_length=1200)
    agreed_findings: list[str]
    top_contradictions: list[TopContradiction]
    gap_ids: list[str]
    recommended_next_steps: list[str]


class StageLatency(ContractModel):
    ingestion_ms: int = Field(ge=0)
    extraction_ms: int = Field(ge=0)
    embedding_ms: int = Field(ge=0)
    clustering_ms: int = Field(ge=0)
    referee_ms: int = Field(ge=0)
    consensus_ms: int = Field(ge=0)
    total_ms: int = Field(ge=0)


class AnalysisStats(ContractModel):
    paper_count: int = Field(ge=0)
    claim_count: int = Field(ge=0)
    cross_paper_pairs_total: int = Field(ge=0)  # claim pairs from different papers
    candidate_pairs: int = Field(ge=0)  # pairs surviving the cosine pre-filter
    referee_pairs_evaluated: int = Field(ge=0)  # after MAX_REFEREE_PAIRS cap
    referee_calls: int = Field(ge=0)  # LLM calls actually made (pairs are batched)
    pair_reduction_pct: float = Field(ge=0.0, le=100.0)
    stage_latency_ms: StageLatency


class ModelDisclosure(ContractModel):
    role: ModelRole
    model_id: str
    provider: str  # "groq" | "huggingface" | "ollama" | "fastembed-local" | "hf-inference-api"
    license: str
    license_url: str
    parameter_count: str


class AnalysisResult(ContractModel):
    schema_version: str = SCHEMA_VERSION
    analysis_id: str
    mode: AnalysisMode
    preset_id: Optional[str] = None
    fixture_provenance: FixtureProvenance
    generated_at: datetime
    papers: list[PaperMetadata] = Field(min_length=2, max_length=10)
    claims: list[ExtractedClaim]
    clusters: list[TopicCluster]
    edges: list[ClaimComparisonEdge]
    gaps: list[ResearchGap]
    consensus: ConsensusReport
    stats: AnalysisStats
    models: list[ModelDisclosure]
    warnings: list[str] = []


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Job / transport models
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class ErrorBody(ContractModel):
    code: ErrorCode
    message: str
    details: Optional[dict] = None


class ErrorResponse(ContractModel):
    error: ErrorBody


class ProgressEvent(ContractModel):
    analysis_id: str
    stage: PipelineStage
    status: JobStatus
    progress_pct: int = Field(ge=0, le=100)
    message: str
    timestamp: datetime


class JobCreated(ContractModel):
    analysis_id: str
    status: JobStatus
    events_url: str  # "/api/v1/analyze/{analysis_id}/events"
    result_url: str  # "/api/v1/analyze/{analysis_id}"


class JobState(ContractModel):
    analysis_id: str
    status: JobStatus
    stage: PipelineStage
    progress_pct: int = Field(ge=0, le=100)
    error: Optional[ErrorBody] = None
    result: Optional[AnalysisResult] = None  # populated only when status == COMPLETED


class DemoPresetSummary(ContractModel):
    preset_id: str  # "radiology" | "llm_quantization" | "microgrids" | "contract_sample"
    title: str
    description: str
    domain: str
    paper_count: int
    fixture_provenance: FixtureProvenance


class ProviderHealth(ContractModel):
    configured: bool
    model_id: Optional[str] = None


class HealthResponse(ContractModel):
    status: Literal["ok", "degraded"]
    version: str
    schema_version: str
    providers: dict[str, ProviderHealth]  # keys: groq, huggingface, ollama
    embedding_backend: str
    presets_loaded: list[str]
```

## 2. TypeScript interfaces

Copy verbatim into `frontend/src/lib/types.ts` (Milestone P1). Field names, optionality and enum values mirror §1 exactly.

```ts
// frontend/src/lib/types.ts
export const SCHEMA_VERSION = "1.0.0" as const;

export type RelationType = "AGREE" | "CONTRADICT" | "METHODOLOGY_DELTA";
export type SemanticSignal = RelationType | "RESEARCH_GAP";

export type SectionName =
  | "ABSTRACT"
  | "METHODOLOGY"
  | "EXPERIMENTAL_SETUP"
  | "RESULTS"
  | "DISCUSSION"
  | "CONCLUSION"
  | "LIMITATIONS"
  | "OTHER";

export type PaperSource = "UPLOAD" | "ARXIV" | "DEMO";
export type ClaimType = "EMPIRICAL_RESULT" | "METHOD" | "LIMITATION" | "CONCLUSION";
export type AnalysisMode = "LIVE" | "DEMO";
export type FixtureProvenance = "SYNTHETIC_PLACEHOLDER" | "REAL_PIPELINE_OUTPUT" | "NOT_APPLICABLE";
export type ModelRole = "EXTRACTION" | "EMBEDDING" | "REFEREE" | "CONSENSUS_WRITER";
export type PipelineStage =
  | "INGESTION"
  | "EXTRACTION"
  | "EMBEDDING"
  | "CLUSTERING"
  | "REFEREE"
  | "CONSENSUS"
  | "DONE";
export type JobStatus = "QUEUED" | "RUNNING" | "COMPLETED" | "FAILED";
export type ErrorCode =
  | "INVALID_FILE_TYPE"
  | "FILE_TOO_LARGE"
  | "TOO_FEW_PAPERS"
  | "TOO_MANY_PAPERS"
  | "INVALID_ARXIV_ID"
  | "ARXIV_FETCH_FAILED"
  | "PDF_PARSE_FAILED"
  | "NO_TEXT_LAYER"
  | "LLM_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE"
  | "LLM_INVALID_JSON"
  | "ANALYSIS_NOT_FOUND"
  | "PRESET_NOT_FOUND"
  | "RATE_LIMITED"
  | "INTERNAL_ERROR";

export interface Provenance {
  paper_id: string;
  paper_title: string;
  section: SectionName;
  page_number: number; // >= 1
  verbatim_quote: string; // 10..600 chars
}

export interface PaperMetadata {
  paper_id: string; // "p1".."p10"
  short_label: string; // "P1".."P10"
  citation_label: string;
  palette_index: number; // 0..9
  source: PaperSource;
  arxiv_id: string | null;
  filename: string | null;
  title: string;
  authors: string[];
  year: number | null;
  page_count: number;
  sections_found: SectionName[];
  dataset: string;
  sample_size: string | null;
  methodology: string;
  primary_metric: string;
  limitations: string;
  core_conclusion: string;
  extraction_model: string;
}

export interface ExtractedClaim {
  claim_id: string; // "p3-c2"
  paper_id: string;
  claim_type: ClaimType;
  topic_hint: string;
  statement: string;
  metric_value: string | null;
  cluster_id: string | null;
  is_research_gap: boolean;
  provenance: Provenance;
}

export interface TopicCluster {
  cluster_id: string; // "cl-1"
  label: string;
  claim_ids: string[];
  paper_ids: string[];
  mean_similarity: number; // 0..1
}

export interface ClaimComparisonEdge {
  edge_id: string;
  source_claim_id: string;
  target_claim_id: string;
  source: Provenance;
  target: Provenance;
  relation: RelationType;
  confidence: number; // 0..1
  cosine_similarity: number; // 0..1
  explanation: string;
  cluster_id: string | null;
  referee_model: string;
}

export interface ResearchGap {
  signal: "RESEARCH_GAP";
  gap_id: string;
  claim_id: string;
  paper_id: string;
  statement: string;
  provenance: Provenance;
  best_match_similarity: number;
  reason: string;
}

export interface TopContradiction {
  edge_id: string;
  headline: string;
}

export interface ConsensusReport {
  consensus_score: number; // 0..100
  executive_summary: string;
  agreed_findings: string[];
  top_contradictions: TopContradiction[];
  gap_ids: string[];
  recommended_next_steps: string[];
}

export interface StageLatency {
  ingestion_ms: number;
  extraction_ms: number;
  embedding_ms: number;
  clustering_ms: number;
  referee_ms: number;
  consensus_ms: number;
  total_ms: number;
}

export interface AnalysisStats {
  paper_count: number;
  claim_count: number;
  cross_paper_pairs_total: number;
  candidate_pairs: number;
  referee_pairs_evaluated: number;
  referee_calls: number;
  pair_reduction_pct: number;
  stage_latency_ms: StageLatency;
}

export interface ModelDisclosure {
  role: ModelRole;
  model_id: string;
  provider: string;
  license: string;
  license_url: string;
  parameter_count: string;
}

export interface AnalysisResult {
  schema_version: string;
  analysis_id: string;
  mode: AnalysisMode;
  preset_id: string | null;
  fixture_provenance: FixtureProvenance;
  generated_at: string; // ISO-8601 UTC
  papers: PaperMetadata[]; // 2..10
  claims: ExtractedClaim[];
  clusters: TopicCluster[];
  edges: ClaimComparisonEdge[];
  gaps: ResearchGap[];
  consensus: ConsensusReport;
  stats: AnalysisStats;
  models: ModelDisclosure[];
  warnings: string[];
}

export interface ErrorBody {
  code: ErrorCode;
  message: string;
  details: Record<string, unknown> | null;
}

export interface ErrorResponse {
  error: ErrorBody;
}

export interface ProgressEvent {
  analysis_id: string;
  stage: PipelineStage;
  status: JobStatus;
  progress_pct: number; // 0..100
  message: string;
  timestamp: string; // ISO-8601 UTC
}

export interface JobCreated {
  analysis_id: string;
  status: JobStatus;
  events_url: string;
  result_url: string;
}

export interface JobState {
  analysis_id: string;
  status: JobStatus;
  stage: PipelineStage;
  progress_pct: number;
  error: ErrorBody | null;
  result: AnalysisResult | null;
}

export interface DemoPresetSummary {
  preset_id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  domain: string;
  paper_count: number;
  fixture_provenance: FixtureProvenance;
}

export interface ProviderHealth {
  configured: boolean;
  model_id: string | null;
}

export interface HealthResponse {
  status: "ok" | "degraded";
  version: string;
  schema_version: string;
  providers: Record<string, ProviderHealth>;
  embedding_backend: string;
  presets_loaded: string[];
}
```

> **Serialization note (backend):** FastAPI must serialize with `response_model_exclude_none=False` so optional fields appear as `null` (the TypeScript types above declare `T | null`, not `T | undefined`). Datetimes serialize as ISO-8601 UTC with a trailing `Z`.

---

## 3. REST endpoints

Base path: `/api/v1`. All responses are `application/json` unless stated. CORS allows the origins listed in `CORS_ALLOW_ORIGINS`.

### 3.1 `GET /api/v1/health`
- **200** → `HealthResponse`. `status` is `"degraded"` when no LLM provider is configured **and** Ollama is unreachable; presets still work in that state.

### 3.2 `GET /api/v1/demo`
- **200** → `DemoPresetSummary[]`. Always includes `contract_sample` until real presets replace it; at Demo Day the list contains exactly `radiology`, `llm_quantization`, `microgrids` (all `REAL_PIPELINE_OUTPUT`).

### 3.3 `GET /api/v1/demo/{preset_id}`
- **200** → `AnalysisResult` read from `fixtures/{preset_id}.json` (no network calls, target < 100 ms).
- **404** → `ErrorResponse` with `PRESET_NOT_FOUND`.

### 3.4 `POST /api/v1/analyze`
Starts an analysis job. `Content-Type: multipart/form-data`.

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `files` | file[] (repeatable) | no | PDFs only (`application/pdf`, `.pdf`). |
| `arxiv_ids` | string (JSON array encoded as a string) | no | e.g. `["2401.01234","https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.14314v2"]`. Accepts bare ids, `abs`/`pdf` URLs, old-style ids (`cs.CL/0501001`). |

- Total papers (`files` + `arxiv_ids`, after de-duplication) MUST be 2–10.
- **202** → `JobCreated`.
- **400** → `ErrorResponse`: `INVALID_FILE_TYPE`, `TOO_FEW_PAPERS`, `TOO_MANY_PAPERS`, `INVALID_ARXIV_ID`.
- **413** → `ErrorResponse`: `FILE_TOO_LARGE`.
- **429** → `ErrorResponse`: `RATE_LIMITED` (per-IP limit `ANALYZE_RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE`, default 6).

### 3.5 `GET /api/v1/analyze/{analysis_id}/events`
Server-Sent Events (`Content-Type: text/event-stream`). Each event:

```
event: progress
data: {"analysis_id":"a1b2","stage":"EXTRACTION","status":"RUNNING","progress_pct":35,"message":"Extracting claims from paper 3 of 8","timestamp":"2026-10-28T10:15:03Z"}

```

Event names: `progress` (payload `ProgressEvent`), `complete` (payload `ProgressEvent` with `stage="DONE"`, `status="COMPLETED"`, `progress_pct=100`), `failed` (payload `ErrorResponse`). The stream closes after `complete` or `failed`. A `: keep-alive` comment line is sent every 15 s. If the job already finished when the client connects, the server immediately emits the terminal event.

Stage → progress ranges: `INGESTION` 0–15, `EXTRACTION` 15–55, `EMBEDDING` 55–62, `CLUSTERING` 62–68, `REFEREE` 68–90, `CONSENSUS` 90–99, `DONE` 100.

### 3.6 `GET /api/v1/analyze/{analysis_id}`
- **200** → `JobState` (`result` populated only when `status == "COMPLETED"`).
- **404** → `ErrorResponse` with `ANALYSIS_NOT_FOUND`.
- Jobs are kept in memory for `JOB_TTL_MINUTES` (default 60).

### 3.7 Error semantics
All non-2xx bodies are `ErrorResponse`. Failures that happen *inside* a running job are delivered via the SSE `failed` event and `JobState.error`, never as HTTP errors.

| Code | HTTP | Meaning |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `INVALID_FILE_TYPE` | 400 | Non-PDF upload (checked by magic bytes `%PDF-`, not only extension). |
| `FILE_TOO_LARGE` | 413 | A file exceeds `MAX_FILE_MB` or the request exceeds `MAX_TOTAL_MB`. |
| `TOO_FEW_PAPERS` | 400 | Fewer than 2 papers after de-duplication. |
| `TOO_MANY_PAPERS` | 400 | More than 10 papers. |
| `INVALID_ARXIV_ID` | 400 | An `arxiv_ids` entry cannot be normalised. |
| `ARXIV_FETCH_FAILED` | job | arXiv PDF download failed (timeout, 404, non-PDF response). |
| `PDF_PARSE_FAILED` | job | PyMuPDF could not open the file. |
| `NO_TEXT_LAYER` | job | PDF is scanned (fewer than 200 extractable characters per page on average). OCR is out of scope. |
| `LLM_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE` | job | Every provider in `LLM_PROVIDER_CHAIN` failed. |
| `LLM_INVALID_JSON` | job | Model output was not valid after `LLM_MAX_RETRIES` retries. |
| `ANALYSIS_NOT_FOUND` | 404 | Unknown or expired `analysis_id`. |
| `PRESET_NOT_FOUND` | 404 | Unknown `preset_id`. |
| `RATE_LIMITED` | 429 | Too many analyze requests. |
| `INTERNAL_ERROR` | 500 | Unhandled exception (message is generic; details only in server logs). |

A paper that fails individually (e.g. `NO_TEXT_LAYER`) does **not** fail the whole job if at least 2 papers remain: it is skipped and an explanatory string is appended to `AnalysisResult.warnings`.

---

## 4. Identifier and ordering rules

| Item | Rule |
| :--- | :--- |
| `paper_id` | `p{n}` in submission order (uploads first in the order received, then arXiv ids). `short_label = P{n}`; `palette_index = n-1`. |
| `claim_id` | `{paper_id}-c{k}`, `k` starting at 1 in extraction order, e.g. `p3-c2`. |
| `cluster_id` | `cl-{k}`, `k` starting at 1, ordered by descending `len(claim_ids)` then ascending first `claim_id`. |
| `edge_id` | `e-` + `source_claim_id` + `-` + `target_claim_id` with dashes removed inside each claim id, e.g. `e-p1c2-p2c2`. The source is always the claim from the lower-numbered paper. |
| `gap_id` | `gap-{claim_id}`. |
| Edge ordering | Descending `confidence`, with `CONTRADICT` before `METHODOLOGY_DELTA` before `AGREE` on ties. |

---

## 5. Pre-filter and gap semantics (shared understanding)

- `cross_paper_pairs_total` = number of claim pairs whose `paper_id` values differ.
- `candidate_pairs` = pairs with cosine similarity `>= SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD` after keeping at most `TOP_K_PER_PAPER` neighbours per claim per other paper.
- `referee_pairs_evaluated` = `min(candidate_pairs, MAX_REFEREE_PAIRS)`; truncation keeps the highest-similarity pairs and adds a string to `warnings`.
- `pair_reduction_pct = round(100 * (1 - candidate_pairs / cross_paper_pairs_total), 1)` (0.0 when the denominator is 0).
- A claim is a **research gap** when it has **no** cross-paper neighbour with cosine similarity `>= SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD`, evaluated **before** the `TOP_K` and `MAX_REFEREE_PAIRS` caps. `best_match_similarity` is its highest cross-paper similarity.

## 6. Consensus score (deterministic, not LLM-generated)

Let `A`, `C`, `D` be the counts of `AGREE`, `CONTRADICT`, `METHODOLOGY_DELTA` edges.

```
consensus_score = 0                      if A + C + D == 0
consensus_score = round(100 * (A + 0.5 * D) / (A + C + D))   otherwise
```

The LLM writes only the prose fields (`executive_summary`, `agreed_findings`, `top_contradictions[].headline`, `recommended_next_steps`). Every `edge_id` / `gap_id` it returns is validated against the real lists; unknown ids are discarded.

---

================================================================================
FILE: docs/shared/03-open-weight-models.md (238 lines)
================================================================================
# 03 — Open-Weight Model Strategy

Everything the models do in Kairos, with exact ids, licences, prompts and known limits. **Runtime code may only call the models in this file.**

## 1. Provider fallback chain

`LLM_PROVIDER_CHAIN=groq,huggingface,ollama` (comma separated, tried in order). All three expose an **OpenAI-compatible** chat-completions API, so one client class (the `openai` Python SDK with a custom `base_url`) serves them all. This is only a transport choice: the models are open-weight.

| Order | Provider | Base URL (env) | Key env | Model env (default) |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| 1 | Groq | `GROQ_BASE_URL=https://api.groq.com/openai/v1` | `GROQ_API_KEY` | `GROQ_MODEL=llama-3.1-8b-instant` |
| 2 | Hugging Face Inference | `HF_BASE_URL=https://router.huggingface.co/v1` | `HF_TOKEN` | `HF_MODEL=meta-llama/Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct` |
| 3 | Local Ollama | `OLLAMA_BASE_URL=http://localhost:11434/v1` | none (any string, e.g. `ollama`) | `OLLAMA_MODEL=llama3.2:3b` |

A provider is **skipped** when its key env is empty (Groq, HF) or when it is unreachable (Ollama). Fail-over triggers: HTTP 429 (after honouring `Retry-After` once), HTTP ≥ 500, timeout, connection error, or invalid JSON after the corrective retry. HTTP 401/403 disables that provider for the process lifetime and logs a clear error (never the key).

**Request parameters**

| Stage | `temperature` | `max_tokens` | `response_format` |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| 1 Extraction | 0.1 | 1800 | `{"type":"json_object"}` |
| 3 Referee | 0.0 | 900 | `{"type":"json_object"}` |
| 4 Consensus | 0.3 | 700 | `{"type":"json_object"}` |

If a provider rejects `response_format`, retry once without it (the prompts already demand JSON-only output) and rely on the JSON parser and Pydantic validation.

## 2. Model registry and licence compliance

| Role | Model id | Params | Licence | Licence URL |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Primary LLM | `meta-llama/Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct` | 8B | Llama 3.1 Community License | https://www.llama.com/llama3_1/license/ |
| Alternative LLM | `Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct` | 7B | Apache-2.0 | https://huggingface.co/Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct |
| Offline fallback LLM | `meta-llama/Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct` | 3B | Llama 3.2 Community License | https://www.llama.com/llama3_2/license/ |
| Embeddings | `BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5` | 33M, 384-d | MIT | https://huggingface.co/BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5 |

**Obligations we meet:**
1. Show **"Built with Llama"** in the UI footer and README (Llama licences require attribution when distributing a product built on them).
2. Include the licence names and links in the in-app model table (`AnalysisResult.models[]`) and README.
3. We **do not redistribute model weights**. Weights are fetched by providers or by the user's own Ollama/FastEmbed install.
4. Meta models on Hugging Face are gated: the person using an HF token must have accepted the licence on the model page.
5. Follow each licence's acceptable-use policy. Kairos only analyses documents the user supplies.
6. "Open weights" ≠ "open source": the README says so explicitly.

## 3. Stage 1 — Structured extraction

### 3.1 System prompt (use verbatim)

```
You are a meticulous scientific information extraction engine. You read excerpts from ONE academic paper and return a single JSON object. You never chat, never explain, and never use markdown.

HARD RULES
1. Output ONLY one JSON object that matches the SCHEMA below. No code fences, no commentary.
2. Use ONLY information present in the PAPER TEXT. If a field is not stated, use the string "Not reported" (or null where the schema allows null). Never guess and never use outside knowledge.
3. Every claim MUST include "verbatim_quote": an EXACT contiguous substring copied character-for-character from the PAPER TEXT, between 10 and 400 characters. Do not paraphrase, correct typos, merge sentences, or add ellipses. Never include [[PAGE n]] or [[SECTION: X]] markers inside a quote.
4. "page_number" is the integer n of the nearest preceding [[PAGE n]] marker before the quote.
5. "section" is the name in the nearest preceding [[SECTION: NAME]] marker and must be one of: ABSTRACT, METHODOLOGY, EXPERIMENTAL_SETUP, RESULTS, DISCUSSION, CONCLUSION, LIMITATIONS, OTHER.
6. Extract between 4 and 6 atomic claims. Prefer claims that contain numbers, comparisons, named datasets or models, or explicit recommendations. Each "statement" must be a single self-contained sentence of at most 280 characters that names its subject (no bare "it", "this method", "our model").
7. Include at least one claim of type CONCLUSION. If the paper states limitations, include one claim of type LIMITATION.
8. "claim_type" is one of: EMPIRICAL_RESULT, METHOD, LIMITATION, CONCLUSION.
9. "topic_hint" is a 2-6 word noun phrase naming what the claim is about (e.g. "long-context accuracy"). Claims about the same question in different papers should use similar wording.
10. "year" is the 4-digit publication year if printed in the text, otherwise null.

SCHEMA
{
  "title": string,
  "authors": [string],
  "year": integer | null,
  "dataset": string,
  "sample_size": string | null,
  "methodology": string,
  "primary_metric": string,
  "limitations": string,
  "core_conclusion": string,
  "claims": [
    {
      "claim_type": "EMPIRICAL_RESULT" | "METHOD" | "LIMITATION" | "CONCLUSION",
      "topic_hint": string,
      "statement": string,
      "metric_value": string | null,
      "section": "ABSTRACT" | "METHODOLOGY" | "EXPERIMENTAL_SETUP" | "RESULTS" | "DISCUSSION" | "CONCLUSION" | "LIMITATIONS" | "OTHER",
      "page_number": integer,
      "verbatim_quote": string
    }
  ]
}
```

### 3.2 User prompt template

```
PAPER FILENAME: {filename}
PAPER TEXT (sectioned, with page markers):
<<<
{sectioned_text}
>>>
Return the JSON object now.
```

### 3.3 Corrective retry (appended as a second user message when parsing/validation fails)

```
Your previous reply was rejected: {error_message}.
Reply again with ONLY the corrected JSON object that satisfies the schema. Do not apologise or explain.
```

### 3.4 After the call
Backend maps the model's flat claim fields into `ExtractedClaim` + `Provenance` (adding `paper_id`, `paper_title`, ids), then runs the quote verifier (§6). The `Provenance.paper_title` is the extracted `title`.

## 4. Stage 2 — Embeddings (`BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5`)

- **Backend:** `EMBEDDING_BACKEND=fastembed` (default; ONNX, no PyTorch, CPU-friendly). Alternative: `hf_api` (Hugging Face Inference feature-extraction endpoint) when the host cannot run ONNX.
- **Input text per claim:** `"{topic_hint}: {statement}"` (no instruction prefix is used because both sides of every comparison are symmetric claim texts).
- **Post-processing:** L2-normalise every vector, so cosine similarity = dot product.
- **Preload** the model at application startup (and bake it into the Docker image) so the first request is not delayed.

### 4.1 Threshold tuning procedure (done in D4, results recorded here)

BGE similarity scores are compressed into a high range, so a default cannot be trusted blindly.
1. Build `backend/tests/data/tuning_pairs.json`: at least 30 claim pairs written/selected by the humans from real papers, each labelled `related` or `unrelated`.
2. Run `python backend/scripts/tune_threshold.py` to print precision/recall for thresholds 0.50–0.85 in 0.05 steps.
3. Choose the highest threshold with recall ≥ 0.85 on `related` pairs. Update `SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD` default and fill in this table:

| Date | Pairs | Chosen threshold | Precision | Recall | Notes |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| 2026-10-27 | 36 | 0.70 | 1.000 | 0.950 | BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5 on 20 related / 16 unrelated pairs across LLM, radiology, microgrids. Highest threshold with recall >= 0.85 (F1=0.974). |

Until then the working default is **0.65**.

## 5. Stage 3 — Strict NLI referee

### 5.1 System prompt (use verbatim)

```
You are a rigorous academic referee performing Natural Language Inference between pairs of research claims taken from different papers. For each pair, decide how the two papers relate. You never chat and never use markdown. Output ONLY one JSON object.

LABELS
- AGREE: Under comparable conditions, both claims support the same finding or point in the same direction.
- CONTRADICT: The claims make incompatible assertions about the SAME question under COMPARABLE conditions (same task, population, dataset type and setting), so both cannot be true at once.
- METHODOLOGY_DELTA: The results or conclusions differ, but the difference is plausibly explained by different datasets, sample sizes, models, hardware, metrics, time horizons or boundary conditions that are visible in the claims or the paper context. This is NOT a true conflict.
- UNRELATED: The claims address different questions or variables.

DECISION RULES
1. If both statements could be true at the same time, the label is NOT CONTRADICT.
2. If the conditions differ in a way that explains the difference, choose METHODOLOGY_DELTA, not CONTRADICT.
3. Use only the information given. Do not use outside knowledge about the papers.
4. Judge each pair independently.

CONFIDENCE
A number from 0.0 to 1.0: 0.9+ means the quotes alone make the label unambiguous; 0.7-0.89 means likely; below 0.7 means uncertain.

EXPLANATION
One or two sentences, at most 60 words. Name BOTH papers using their citation labels. State the deciding factor (for example, benchmark, context length, sample size, dataset cleanliness, or opposite recommendations). Do not quote more than 10 words.

OUTPUT SCHEMA
{"results":[{"pair_id": string, "relation": "AGREE"|"CONTRADICT"|"METHODOLOGY_DELTA"|"UNRELATED", "confidence": number, "explanation": string}]}
The "results" array MUST contain exactly one entry per input pair, using the same "pair_id".
```

### 5.2 User prompt template

```
Evaluate each of the following pairs.

PAIRS (JSON):
{pairs_json}

Return the JSON object now.
```

`pairs_json` is a JSON array of:

```json
{
  "pair_id": "e-p1c2-p2c2",
  "paper_a": {"citation_label": "Example A (2024)", "dataset": "...", "methodology": "..."},
  "claim_a": {"statement": "...", "verbatim_quote": "..."},
  "paper_b": {"citation_label": "Example B (2025)", "dataset": "...", "methodology": "..."},
  "claim_b": {"statement": "...", "verbatim_quote": "..."}
}
```

### 5.3 Validation and post-processing
- `relation` must be one of the four labels; `UNRELATED` pairs are dropped.
- `confidence` is clamped to [0, 1]; non-numeric → retry that pair alone.
- `explanation` truncated to 500 chars (contract limit).
- Pairs missing from `results` are retried once individually.
- `cosine_similarity` is copied from the similarity matrix (not from the model).

### 5.4 Stage 4 — Consensus writer (system prompt, verbatim)

```
You write the executive summary of a multi-paper literature comparison. You are given structured data only: paper metadata, verified relations between claims (with ids), and research gaps. You never chat and never use markdown. Output ONLY one JSON object.

RULES
1. Use ONLY the supplied data. Do not add facts, numbers or papers that are not present.
2. Refer to papers by their citation labels.
3. "executive_summary": at most 120 words, neutral academic tone, covering overall agreement, the main contradictions, and notable gaps.
4. "agreed_findings": up to 5 short bullet strings, each supported by at least one AGREE edge.
5. "top_contradictions": up to 3 objects {"edge_id", "headline"} using ONLY edge_ids with relation CONTRADICT from the input; "headline" is one question-style sentence of at most 25 words. If there are no CONTRADICT edges return an empty list.
6. "recommended_next_steps": 2 to 4 concrete suggestions that follow from the contradictions, methodology differences and gaps.

OUTPUT SCHEMA
{"executive_summary": string, "agreed_findings": [string], "top_contradictions": [{"edge_id": string, "headline": string}], "recommended_next_steps": [string]}
```

User message: a JSON object `{"papers": [...citation_label,title,dataset,methodology,primary_metric], "edges": [...edge_id,relation,confidence,explanation,source_claim,target_claim], "gaps": [...gap_id,paper,statement]}`.

## 6. Quote verification (anti-hallucination, mandatory)

Applies to every claim from Stage 1, before embedding.

1. **Normalise** both quote and page text with the same function: Unicode NFKC, map curly quotes/dashes to ASCII, collapse all whitespace to single spaces, `casefold()`. While normalising, keep an `index_map` from each normalised character back to the index in the original page text.
2. **Exact pass:** search the normalised quote in the normalised text of the claimed page, then the pages ±1, then outward by distance.
3. **Fuzzy pass:** if no exact match, use `rapidfuzz.fuzz.partial_ratio_alignment`; accept the best alignment with score ≥ **92**.
4. On success, **replace the stored quote with the true substring** of the original page text (via `index_map`) and **correct `page_number`** to the page where it was found. The stored quote is therefore guaranteed verbatim.
5. Reject (drop the claim) if no match, if the final quote is shorter than 10 or longer than 600 characters, or if it contains `[[PAGE` / `[[SECTION`.
6. Record counts (`proposed`, `kept`, `dropped`) per paper. If `kept < 2`, add a warning; if `kept == 0`, exclude the paper.

## 7. Known limitations (shown to users and judges)

- **Small models err.** An 8B (or 3B) model can mislabel relations or miss nuance. Mitigations: strict prompts, `temperature` 0–0.1, verbatim quotes shown next to every verdict, confidence scores, and `METHODOLOGY_DELTA` to avoid false contradictions.
- **Quote verification proves a quote exists, not that the claim is correct or that the paper is right.**
- **Layout loss:** tables, figures, equations and footnotes are not parsed; two-column PDFs can reorder text; scanned PDFs are rejected.
- **Language:** English only (the embedding model is English-specific).
- **Truncation:** only a character budget of each paper is read (`MAX_PAPER_CHARS`), so claims in omitted text are not found.
- **Threshold sensitivity:** the similarity threshold trades recall (missed comparisons) against cost.
- **Consensus score** is a simple ratio of edge types, not a meta-analysis.
- **Provider limits:** free tiers are rate limited; response times vary.
- **Licences:** Llama terms apply (attribution, acceptable use). Open weights are not necessarily open source.

## 8. Model-id verification checklist (start of D3, and again before submission)

Provider catalogues change. Before relying on defaults:
1. Open the Groq models page and confirm `llama-3.1-8b-instant` is available; note its free-tier rate limits.
2. Confirm `meta-llama/Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct` is served by the Hugging Face router for your account (and that the licence is accepted).
3. Run `ollama pull llama3.2:3b`.
4. If a default id has changed, update `.env.example`, this file's §1 table and `AnalysisResult.models` generation in the same PR, **keeping the model open-weight**. Choose `Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct` (Apache-2.0) as the first substitute.
5. Record the verified date and provider limits in `docs/requests.md` (row type `INFO`).

FILE: backend/README.md (149 lines)
================================================================================
# Kairos Backend Engine

FastAPI backend engine for **Kairos** — Multi-Paper Literature Synthesis & Contradiction Mapping Engine. Built for the OPEN//BUILD Hackathon 2026.

Kairos synthesizes 2–10 scientific research papers (via direct PDF uploads or arXiv IDs), extracts empirical claims with verbatim text provenance, maps research gaps, resolves semantic contradictions with open-weight Natural Language Inference (NLI), and streams real-time progress via Server-Sent Events (SSE).

> **Built with Llama**  
> Runtime language models: `meta-llama/Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct` (primary), `meta-llama/Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct` (fallback), and `Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct` (alternative). Runtime embeddings: `BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5` (33M parameters, 384-dimensional dense vectors). **Zero closed models are invoked in runtime application code.**

---

## Architecture Overview

The backend executes a 5-stage synthesis pipeline:

1. **Stage 0: Ingestion & Parsing (`/api/v1/analyze`)**
   - Concurrently parses PDFs using PyMuPDF (`fitz`) and fetches arXiv papers with graceful failure isolation.
   - Computes SHA-256 digests to eliminate duplicates across upload and arXiv streams.
   - Injects section markers (`[[SECTION: X]]`) and page indicators (`[[PAGE n]]`).
2. **Stage 1: LLM Claim Extraction & Quote Verification**
   - Extracts 3–7 structured findings, methodologies, and limitations per paper.
   - Enforces the **Quote Verifier Anti-Hallucination Filter**: extracts must contain a 10–400 character verbatim substring from the source text, normalized with Unicode NFKC and RapidFuzz partial ratio alignment. Hallucinated or drifting quotes are discarded.
3. **Stage 2: Embeddings, Cosine Matrix, Research Gaps & Clustering**
   - Embeds claim texts using `BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5` via FastEmbed ONNX runtime.
   - Vectorized cosine similarity matrix with strict same-paper pair masking.
   - Evaluates **Research Gaps** *before* top-k pair caps: identifies isolated claims where cross-paper similarity < 0.70.
   - Agglomerative hierarchical clustering partitions claims into shared topic themes.
4. **Stage 3: Strict Academic NLI Referee**
   - Pairs surviving similarity threshold are evaluated in batches by an open-weight LLM referee.
   - Classifies relationships as `AGREE`, `CONTRADICT`, or `METHODOLOGY_DELTA`. Unrelated pairs are pruned.
5. **Stage 4: Deterministic Consensus & Graph Assembly**
   - Computes deterministic consensus score $round(100 \times (A + 0.5D)/(A+C+D))$.
   - Synthesizes executive summary, agreed findings, and top contradiction highlights with strict ID validation.
   - Assembles contract-compliant `AnalysisResult` JSON.

---

## Quickstart

### Prerequisites
- Python 3.11+ (Python 3.11 or 3.12 recommended)
- `pip` or `uv`
- Optional: Groq API key (for fast cloud inference with Llama 3.1 8B), Hugging Face token, or local Ollama instance.

### Installation

```bash
# Clone the repository
git clone https://github.com/taiwodurotoluwa-svg/kairos.git
cd kairos/backend

# Create virtual environment
python -m venv .venv
source .venv/bin/activate  # On Windows: .venv\Scripts\activate

# Install dependencies
pip install -r requirements.txt
```

### Configuration

Copy `.env.example` to `.env` and configure your LLM providers:

```bash
cp .env.example .env
```

Key environment variables:

| Variable | Description | Default |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `GROQ_API_KEY` | API key for Groq Cloud inference | `""` |
| `GROQ_MODEL` | Primary open-weight model on Groq | `llama-3.1-8b-instant` |
| `HF_TOKEN` | Secondary failover API key | `""` |
| `OLLAMA_BASE_URL` | Local offline Ollama server URL | `http://localhost:11434/v1` |
| `EMBEDDING_BACKEND` | Embedding backend engine | `fastembed` |
| `SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD` | Empirical cosine similarity cutoff | `0.70` |
| `JOB_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` | Pipeline execution timeout | `90` |
| `ANALYZE_RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE` | Per-IP rate limit | `6` |

---

## API Endpoints

### 1. Health & Readiness
`GET /api/v1/health`  
Returns provider health (`ok` or `degraded`), active LLM provider chain, embedding model readiness, and loaded demo presets.

### 2. Demo Presets (Zero API Key Mode)
- `GET /api/v1/demo`: Lists curated multi-paper presets.
- `GET /api/v1/demo/{preset_id}`: Retrieves pre-computed contract-compliant synthesis fixture in < 100 ms.
  - `radiology`: 8 chest X-ray deep learning papers.
  - `llm_quantization`: 6 post-training quantization papers (AWQ vs GPTQ vs SmoothQuant).
  - `microgrids`: 6 islanded microgrid energy management papers (RL vs MPC).

### 3. Multi-Paper Synthesis
- `POST /api/v1/analyze`: Submit 2 to 10 PDFs (multipart/form-data) and/or arXiv IDs. Returns `202 Accepted` with `analysis_id`, `events_url`, and `result_url`.
- `GET /api/v1/analyze/{analysis_id}/events`: Server-Sent Events (SSE) live progress stream (`progress`, `complete`, `failed`) with 15-second keep-alive comments.
- `GET /api/v1/analyze/{analysis_id}`: Polls active job state (`QUEUED`, `RUNNING`) or returns final completed `AnalysisResult`.

---

## Running the Application

### Development Server
```bash
uvicorn app.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000 --reload
```

Interactive API documentation is available at:
- Swagger UI: `http://localhost:8000/docs`
- ReDoc: `http://localhost:8000/redoc`

### Docker Deployment

Build and run using the production Dockerfile:

```bash
# Build from repository root
docker build -f backend/Dockerfile -t kairos-backend .

# Run container (presets run out-of-the-box with zero keys)
docker run -p 8000:8000 --env-file backend/.env kairos-backend
```

The Docker image pre-bakes `BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5` so cold starts serve requests immediately.

---

## Testing

Run the full automated test suite (contract tests, quote verification, NLI referee, similarity, and integration tests):

```bash
python -m pytest backend/tests -v
```

All 97 unit and integration tests execute in < 8 seconds.

---

## Troubleshooting

1. **`LLM_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE` on live analyze:**
   Ensure at least one valid provider key (`GROQ_API_KEY` or `HF_TOKEN`) is set in `.env`, or that local Ollama is running (`ollama run llama3.2:3b`). Demo presets (`GET /api/v1/demo/{id}`) require zero keys.
2. **`RATE_LIMITED` (HTTP 429):**
   The live analyze endpoint enforces a default sliding window of 6 requests per minute per IP. Adjust `ANALYZE_RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE` in `.env` if necessary.
3. **`PDF_PARSE_FAILED` or `NO_TEXT_LAYER`:**
   Kairos processes native, machine-readable scientific PDFs. Scanned image-only PDFs without an OCR text layer will return a descriptive error and warning.

from functools import lru_cache
from pathlib import Path
from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict


def _resolve_default_fixtures_dir() -> str:
    # Look relative to repo root (when running from repo root or backend/)
    backend_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent
    candidate = backend_root / "fixtures"
    if candidate.exists():
        return str(candidate)
    return "../fixtures"


class Settings(BaseSettings):
    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_file=".env", extra="ignore")

    app_env: str = "development"
    app_version: str = "1.0.0"
    cors_allow_origins: str = "http://localhost:3000,http://localhost:5500,http://127.0.0.1:5500,http://localhost:5173"
    fixtures_dir: str = _resolve_default_fixtures_dir()

    min_papers: int = 2
    max_papers: int = 10
    max_file_mb: int = 20
    max_total_mb: int = 80
    max_paper_chars: int = 24000
    analyze_rate_limit_per_minute: int = 6
    job_ttl_minutes: int = 60
    job_timeout_seconds: int = 90
    arxiv_concurrency: int = 3

    llm_provider_chain: str = "groq,huggingface,ollama"
    groq_api_key: str = ""
    groq_model: str = "llama-3.1-8b-instant"
    groq_base_url: str = "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1"
    hf_token: str = ""
    hf_model: str = "meta-llama/Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct"
    hf_base_url: str = "https://router.huggingface.co/v1"
    ollama_base_url: str = ""  # Default empty so /health is not falsely green when Ollama is not configured
    ollama_model: str = "llama3.2:3b"
    llm_timeout_seconds: float = 45.0
    llm_max_retries: int = 2
    llm_max_concurrency: int = 6
    llm_max_rpm: int = 0

    embedding_backend: str = "fastembed"
    embedding_model: str = "BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5"

    similarity_threshold: float = 0.70
    top_k_per_paper: int = 2
    max_referee_pairs: int = 60
    referee_pairs_per_call: int = 4
    cluster_distance_threshold: float = 0.35

    @property
    def cors_origins(self) -> list[str]:
        return [o.strip() for o in self.cors_allow_origins.split(",") if o.strip()]


@lru_cache
def get_settings() -> Settings:
    return Settings()

FILE: backend/app/schemas.py (318 lines)

from __future__ import annotations
import re
# backend/app/schemas.py

from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum
from typing import Literal, Optional, Self

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, model_validator

SCHEMA_VERSION = "1.0.0"


class ContractModel(BaseModel):
    """Base class: unknown keys are rejected so fixtures and API never drift."""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", use_enum_values=False)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Enums
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class RelationType(str, Enum):
    AGREE = "AGREE"
    CONTRADICT = "CONTRADICT"
    METHODOLOGY_DELTA = "METHODOLOGY_DELTA"


class SectionName(str, Enum):
    ABSTRACT = "ABSTRACT"
    METHODOLOGY = "METHODOLOGY"
    EXPERIMENTAL_SETUP = "EXPERIMENTAL_SETUP"
    RESULTS = "RESULTS"
    DISCUSSION = "DISCUSSION"
    CONCLUSION = "CONCLUSION"
    LIMITATIONS = "LIMITATIONS"
    OTHER = "OTHER"


class PaperSource(str, Enum):
    UPLOAD = "UPLOAD"
    ARXIV = "ARXIV"
    DEMO = "DEMO"


class ClaimType(str, Enum):
    EMPIRICAL_RESULT = "EMPIRICAL_RESULT"
    METHOD = "METHOD"
    LIMITATION = "LIMITATION"
    CONCLUSION = "CONCLUSION"


class AnalysisMode(str, Enum):
    LIVE = "LIVE"
    DEMO = "DEMO"


class FixtureProvenance(str, Enum):
    SYNTHETIC_PLACEHOLDER = "SYNTHETIC_PLACEHOLDER"
    REAL_PIPELINE_OUTPUT = "REAL_PIPELINE_OUTPUT"
    NOT_APPLICABLE = "NOT_APPLICABLE"


class ModelRole(str, Enum):
    EXTRACTION = "EXTRACTION"
    EMBEDDING = "EMBEDDING"
    REFEREE = "REFEREE"
    CONSENSUS_WRITER = "CONSENSUS_WRITER"


class PipelineStage(str, Enum):
    INGESTION = "INGESTION"
    EXTRACTION = "EXTRACTION"
    EMBEDDING = "EMBEDDING"
    CLUSTERING = "CLUSTERING"
    REFEREE = "REFEREE"
    CONSENSUS = "CONSENSUS"
    DONE = "DONE"


class JobStatus(str, Enum):
    QUEUED = "QUEUED"
    RUNNING = "RUNNING"
    COMPLETED = "COMPLETED"
    FAILED = "FAILED"


class ErrorCode(str, Enum):
    INVALID_FILE_TYPE = "INVALID_FILE_TYPE"
    FILE_TOO_LARGE = "FILE_TOO_LARGE"
    TOO_FEW_PAPERS = "TOO_FEW_PAPERS"
    TOO_MANY_PAPERS = "TOO_MANY_PAPERS"

    ARXIV_FETCH_FAILED = "ARXIV_FETCH_FAILED"

    LLM_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE = "LLM_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE"

    RATE_LIMITED = "RATE_LIMITED"
    INTERNAL_ERROR = "INTERNAL_ERROR"


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Core data models
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class Provenance(ContractModel):
    paper_id: str
    paper_title: str
    section: SectionName
    page_number: int = Field(ge=1)
    verbatim_quote: str = Field(min_length=10, max_length=600)


class PaperMetadata(ContractModel):
    paper_id: str = Field(pattern=r"^p([1-9]|10)$")  # "p1".."p10", stable order of upload
    short_label: str = Field(pattern=r"^P([1-9]|10)$")  # "P1".."P10"
    citation_label: str  # e.g. "Smith et al. (2024)"; "Example A (2024)" in placeholders
    palette_index: int = Field(ge=0, le=9)  # index into the 10-colour paper palette
    source: PaperSource
    arxiv_id: Optional[str] = None
    filename: Optional[str] = None
    title: str
    authors: list[str]
    year: Optional[int] = Field(default=None, ge=1900, le=2100)
    page_count: int = Field(ge=1)
    sections_found: list[SectionName]
    dataset: str  # "Not reported" when absent
    sample_size: Optional[str] = None  # free text, e.g. "n=1,200 scans"
    methodology: str
    primary_metric: str
    limitations: str
    core_conclusion: str
    extraction_model: str


class ExtractedClaim(ContractModel):
    claim_id: str  # "{paper_id}-c{n}", e.g. "p3-c2"
    paper_id: str
    claim_type: ClaimType
    topic_hint: str = Field(max_length=80)  # short topic phrase from the extractor
    statement: str = Field(min_length=10, max_length=300)
    metric_value: Optional[str] = None  # e.g. "94.2% accuracy"
    cluster_id: Optional[str] = None  # None when unclustered
    is_research_gap: bool = False
    provenance: Provenance


class TopicCluster(ContractModel):
    cluster_id: str  # "cl-1", "cl-2", ...
    label: str  # topic_hint of the claim closest to the cluster centroid
    claim_ids: list[str] = Field(min_length=2)
    paper_ids: list[str] = Field(min_length=2)
    mean_similarity: float = Field(ge=0.0, le=1.0)


class ClaimComparisonEdge(ContractModel):
    edge_id: str  # "e-{source_claim_id without dash}-{target_claim_id without dash}"
    source_claim_id: str  # claim from the lower-numbered paper
    target_claim_id: str
    source: Provenance
    target: Provenance
    relation: RelationType
    confidence: float = Field(ge=0.0, le=1.0)
    cosine_similarity: float = Field(ge=0.0, le=1.0)
    explanation: str = Field(min_length=10, max_length=500)
    cluster_id: Optional[str] = None
    referee_model: str

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_edge_direction_and_no_self_loop(self) -> Self:
        if self.source_claim_id == self.target_claim_id:
            raise ValueError(f"Self-loop edge is forbidden: {self.source_claim_id}")
        s_match = re.match(r"^p([0-9]+)", self.source_claim_id)
        t_match = re.match(r"^p([0-9]+)", self.target_claim_id)
        if s_match and t_match:
            s_num, t_num = int(s_match.group(1)), int(t_match.group(1))
            if s_num >= t_num:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"source_claim_id must belong to a lower-numbered paper than target_claim_id: "
                    f"{self.source_claim_id} (p{s_num}) vs {self.target_claim_id} (p{t_num})"
                )
        return self


class ResearchGap(ContractModel):
    signal: Literal["RESEARCH_GAP"] = "RESEARCH_GAP"
    gap_id: str  # "gap-{claim_id}"
    claim_id: str
    paper_id: str
    statement: str
    provenance: Provenance
    best_match_similarity: float = Field(ge=0.0, le=1.0)
    reason: str


class TopContradiction(ContractModel):
    edge_id: str
    headline: str = Field(max_length=200)


class ConsensusReport(ContractModel):
    consensus_score: int = Field(ge=0, le=100)  # computed deterministically, see §6
    executive_summary: str = Field(max_length=1200)
    agreed_findings: list[str]
    top_contradictions: list[TopContradiction]
    gap_ids: list[str]
    recommended_next_steps: list[str]


class StageLatency(ContractModel):
    ingestion_ms: int = Field(ge=0)
    extraction_ms: int = Field(ge=0)
    embedding_ms: int = Field(ge=0)
    clustering_ms: int = Field(ge=0)
    referee_ms: int = Field(ge=0)
    consensus_ms: int = Field(ge=0)
    total_ms: int = Field(ge=0)


class AnalysisStats(ContractModel):
    paper_count: int = Field(ge=0)
    claim_count: int = Field(ge=0)
    cross_paper_pairs_total: int = Field(ge=0)  # claim pairs from different papers
    candidate_pairs: int = Field(ge=0)  # pairs surviving the cosine pre-filter
    referee_pairs_evaluated: int = Field(ge=0)  # after MAX_REFEREE_PAIRS cap
    referee_calls: int = Field(ge=0)  # LLM calls actually made (pairs are batched)
    pair_reduction_pct: float = Field(ge=0.0, le=100.0)
    stage_latency_ms: StageLatency


class ModelDisclosure(ContractModel):
    role: ModelRole
    model_id: str
    provider: str  # "groq" | "huggingface" | "ollama" | "fastembed-local" | "hf-inference-api"
    license: str
    license_url: str
    parameter_count: str


class AnalysisResult(ContractModel):
    schema_version: str = SCHEMA_VERSION
    analysis_id: str
    mode: AnalysisMode
    preset_id: Optional[str] = None
    fixture_provenance: FixtureProvenance
    generated_at: datetime
    papers: list[PaperMetadata] = Field(min_length=2, max_length=10)
    claims: list[ExtractedClaim]
    clusters: list[TopicCluster]
    edges: list[ClaimComparisonEdge]
    gaps: list[ResearchGap]
    consensus: ConsensusReport
    stats: AnalysisStats
    models: list[ModelDisclosure]
    warnings: list[str] = []


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Job / transport models
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class ErrorBody(ContractModel):
    code: ErrorCode
    message: str
    details: Optional[dict] = None


class ErrorResponse(ContractModel):
    error: ErrorBody


class ProgressEvent(ContractModel):
    analysis_id: str
    stage: PipelineStage
    status: JobStatus
    progress_pct: int = Field(ge=0, le=100)
    message: str
    timestamp: datetime


class JobCreated(ContractModel):
    analysis_id: str
    status: JobStatus
    events_url: str = Field(pattern=r"^/api/v1/analyze/.*")
    result_url: str = Field(pattern=r"^/api/v1/analyze/.*")


class JobState(ContractModel):
    analysis_id: str
    status: JobStatus
    stage: PipelineStage
    progress_pct: int = Field(ge=0, le=100)
    error: Optional[ErrorBody] = None
    result: Optional[AnalysisResult] = None  # populated only when status == COMPLETED


class DemoPresetSummary(ContractModel):
    preset_id: str  # "radiology" | "llm_quantization" | "microgrids" | "contract_sample"
    title: str
    description: str
    domain: str
    paper_count: int
    fixture_provenance: FixtureProvenance


class ProviderHealth(ContractModel):
    configured: bool
    model_id: Optional[str] = None


class HealthResponse(ContractModel):
    status: Literal["ok", "degraded"]
    version: str
    schema_version: str
    providers: dict[str, ProviderHealth]  # keys: groq, huggingface, ollama
    embedding_backend: str
    presets_loaded: list[str]

from fastapi import FastAPI, Request
from fastapi.exceptions import RequestValidationError
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
from starlette.exceptions import HTTPException as StarletteHTTPException

from app.schemas import ErrorBody, ErrorCode, ErrorResponse


class KairosError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, code: ErrorCode, message: str, status_code: int = 400, details: dict | None = None):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code, self.message, self.status_code, self.details = code, message, status_code, details

    def to_body(self) -> ErrorBody:
        return ErrorBody(code=self.code, message=self.message, details=self.details)


def install_error_handlers(app: FastAPI) -> None:
    @app.exception_handler(KairosError)
    async def _handle_kairos(_: Request, exc: KairosError) -> JSONResponse:
        body = ErrorResponse(error=exc.to_body()).model_dump(mode="json")
        return JSONResponse(status_code=exc.status_code, content=body)

    @app.exception_handler(RequestValidationError)
    async def _handle_validation(_: Request, exc: RequestValidationError) -> JSONResponse:
        body = ErrorResponse(
            error=ErrorBody(
                code=ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR,
                message="Request validation error",
                details={"errors": exc.errors()},
            )
        ).model_dump(mode="json")
        return JSONResponse(status_code=422, content=body)

    @app.exception_handler(StarletteHTTPException)
    async def _handle_http(_: Request, exc: StarletteHTTPException) -> JSONResponse:
        code = ErrorCode.PRESET_NOT_FOUND if exc.status_code == 404 else ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR
        body = ErrorResponse(
            error=ErrorBody(
                code=code,
                message=str(exc.detail),
                details=None,
            )
        ).model_dump(mode="json")
        return JSONResponse(status_code=exc.status_code, content=body)

    @app.exception_handler(Exception)
    async def _handle_unhandled(_: Request, exc: Exception) -> JSONResponse:
        body = ErrorResponse(
            error=ErrorBody(
                code=ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR,
                message="An unexpected internal error occurred.",
                details={"type": exc.__class__.__name__, "detail": str(exc)},
            )
        ).model_dump(mode="json")
        return JSONResponse(status_code=500, content=body)

from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware

from app.api import analyze, demo, health
from app.config import get_settings
from app.errors import install_error_handlers


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    demo.warm_demo_cache()
    settings = get_settings()
    if settings.embedding_backend == "fastembed":
        try:
            from app.services.embeddings import get_embedder

            get_embedder(settings)
        except Exception:
            pass
    yield


settings = get_settings()
app = FastAPI(title="Kairos API", version=settings.app_version, lifespan=lifespan)
app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=settings.cors_origins,
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["GET", "POST", "OPTIONS"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)
install_error_handlers(app)
app.include_router(health.router, prefix="/api/v1")
app.include_router(demo.router, prefix="/api/v1")
app.include_router(analyze.router, prefix="/api/v1")

from fastapi import APIRouter
from app.config import get_settings
from app.schemas import HealthResponse, ProviderHealth, SCHEMA_VERSION
from app.api.demo import PRESETS, _resolve_fixture_path

router = APIRouter(tags=["health"])


@router.get("/health", response_model=HealthResponse)
def health_check() -> HealthResponse:
    settings = get_settings()

    provider_chain = [p.strip() for p in settings.llm_provider_chain.split(",") if p.strip()]

    groq_conf = "groq" in provider_chain and bool(settings.groq_api_key.strip())
    hf_conf = "huggingface" in provider_chain and bool(settings.hf_token.strip())
    ollama_conf = "ollama" in provider_chain and bool(settings.ollama_base_url.strip())

    providers = {
        "groq": ProviderHealth(configured=groq_conf, model_id=settings.groq_model if groq_conf else None),
        "huggingface": ProviderHealth(configured=hf_conf, model_id=settings.hf_model if hf_conf else None),
        "ollama": ProviderHealth(configured=ollama_conf, model_id=settings.ollama_model if ollama_conf else None),
    }

    # Canonical preset IDs resolved safely via _resolve_fixture_path
    loaded_presets = [pid for pid, (fn, *_) in PRESETS.items() if _resolve_fixture_path(fn).exists()]

    has_llm = groq_conf or hf_conf or ollama_conf
    status = "ok" if has_llm else "degraded"

    return HealthResponse(
        status=status,
        version=settings.app_version,
        schema_version=SCHEMA_VERSION,
        providers=providers,
        embedding_backend=settings.embedding_backend,
        presets_loaded=sorted(loaded_presets)
    )
