from functools import lru_cache
from pathlib import Path
from fastapi import APIRouter
from app.config import get_settings
from app.errors import KairosError
from app.schemas import AnalysisResult, DemoPresetSummary, ErrorCode

router = APIRouter(tags=["demo"])

# preset_id -> (file name, title, description, domain)
PRESETS: dict[str, tuple[str, str, str, str]] = {
    "radiology": ("demo_radiology_8papers.json", "Deep learning in chest radiography", "8 papers on lab vs real-world performance", "Medical imaging"),
    "llm_quantization": ("demo_llm_quantization_6papers.json", "LLM quantization trade-offs", "6 papers on accuracy vs efficiency", "Machine learning"),
    "microgrids": ("demo_microgrids_6papers.json", "Solar micro-grid reliability", "6 papers on storage and stability", "Energy systems"),
    "contract_sample": ("contract_sample.json", "Contract sample (3 papers)", "Synthetic payload that illustrates the API contract", "Contract"),
}


def _resolve_fixture_path(filename: str) -> Path:
    settings = get_settings()
    candidate1 = Path(settings.fixtures_dir) / filename
    if candidate1.exists():
        return candidate1
    candidate2 = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent.parent / "fixtures" / filename
    if candidate2.exists():
        return candidate2
    return candidate1


@lru_cache(maxsize=16)
def _load(preset_id: str) -> AnalysisResult:
    if preset_id not in PRESETS:
        raise KairosError(ErrorCode.PRESET_NOT_FOUND, f"Unknown preset '{preset_id}'.", 404)
    path = _resolve_fixture_path(PRESETS[preset_id][0])
    if not path.exists():
        raise KairosError(ErrorCode.PRESET_NOT_FOUND, f"Preset file for '{preset_id}' is missing.", 404)
    try:
        return AnalysisResult.model_validate_json(path.read_bytes())
    except KairosError:
        raise
    except Exception as exc:
        raise KairosError(ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR, f"Invalid fixture '{preset_id}': {exc}", 500) from exc


def warm_demo_cache() -> None:
    """Pre-warm cached demo presets so Demo Day playback has sub-millisecond response."""
    for pid in PRESETS:
        try:
            _load(pid)
        except Exception:
            pass


@router.get("/demo", response_model=list[DemoPresetSummary])
def list_presets() -> list[DemoPresetSummary]:
    out = []
    for pid, (_, title, desc, domain) in PRESETS.items():
        try:
            r = _load(pid)
        except Exception:
            continue
        out.append(DemoPresetSummary(preset_id=pid, title=title, description=desc, domain=domain,
                                     paper_count=len(r.papers), fixture_provenance=r.fixture_provenance))
    return out


@router.get("/demo/{preset_id}", response_model=AnalysisResult)
def get_preset(preset_id: str) -> AnalysisResult:
    return _load(preset_id)

FILE: backend/app/api/analyze.py (214 lines)

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import logging
import re
from typing import AsyncGenerator, Optional
from fastapi import APIRouter, File, Form, Request, Response, UploadFile, status
from sse_starlette.sse import EventSourceResponse

from app.config import get_settings
from app.errors import KairosError
from app.schemas import (
    ErrorCode,
    JobCreated,
    JobStatus,
)
from app.services.ingestion.validators import validate_inputs
from app.services.jobs import get_job_store
from app.services.pipeline import run_pipeline
from app.services.rate_limit import get_rate_limiter

logger = logging.getLogger("kairos.api.analyze")
router = APIRouter(tags=["analyze"])

_BACKGROUND_TASKS: set[asyncio.Task] = set()


@router.post(
    "/analyze",
    response_model=JobCreated,
    status_code=status.HTTP_202_ACCEPTED,
    summary="Submit PDFs and arXiv IDs for multi-paper synthesis",
)
async def submit_analysis(
    request: Request,
    files: list[UploadFile] = File(default=[]),
    arxiv_ids: Optional[str] = Form(default=None),
) -> JobCreated:
    """Submit 2 to 10 research papers via direct PDF uploads or arXiv IDs.

    Returns 202 Accepted with analysis_id and polling / SSE event stream URLs.
    """
    settings = get_settings()

    # Per-IP rate limiting (contract §3.7) with reverse-proxy X-Forwarded-For support
    forwarded = request.headers.get("x-forwarded-for")
    if forwarded:
        client_ip = forwarded.split(",")[0].strip()
    elif request.client:
        client_ip = request.client.host
    else:
        client_ip = "unknown"
    get_rate_limiter().check_rate_limit(client_ip)

    # Read uploaded file contents
    raw_files: list[tuple[str, bytes]] = []
    for f in files:
        if f.filename:
            content = await f.read()
            raw_files.append((f.filename, content))

    # Parse arXiv IDs (comma, space, or newline separated)
    parsed_arxiv_ids: list[str] = []
    if arxiv_ids:
        for item in re.split(r"[,\s\n]+", arxiv_ids.strip()):
            clean = item.strip()
            if clean:
                parsed_arxiv_ids.append(clean)

    # Validate paper limits, file sizes, and PDF magic bytes before starting job
    validate_inputs(files=raw_files, arxiv_ids=parsed_arxiv_ids, settings=settings)

    # Bounded memory: cleanup expired jobs before allocating a new one
    job_store = get_job_store()
    job_store.cleanup_expired(settings.job_ttl_minutes)

    analysis_id = job_store.generate_analysis_id()
    job_store.create_job(analysis_id)

    # Launch pipeline asynchronously with strong reference and unhandled exception guard
    async def _run_pipeline_background() -> None:
        try:
            await run_pipeline(
                analysis_id=analysis_id,
                raw_files=raw_files,
                arxiv_ids=parsed_arxiv_ids,
                job_store=job_store,
                settings=settings,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.info("Background analysis %s finished with exception: %s", analysis_id, exc)

    task = asyncio.create_task(_run_pipeline_background())
    _BACKGROUND_TASKS.add(task)
    task.add_done_callback(_BACKGROUND_TASKS.discard)

    return JobCreated(
        analysis_id=analysis_id,
        status=JobStatus.QUEUED,
        events_url=f"/api/v1/analyze/{analysis_id}/events",
        result_url=f"/api/v1/analyze/{analysis_id}",
    )


@router.get(
    "/analyze/{analysis_id}/events",
    summary="Server-Sent Events progress stream for an active synthesis job",
)
async def stream_analysis_events(
    analysis_id: str,
    request: Request,
) -> EventSourceResponse:
    """Stream live Server-Sent Events (progress, complete, failed) with 15s keep-alive pings."""
    job_store = get_job_store()
    queue = job_store.subscribe(analysis_id)
    if queue is None:
        raise KairosError(
            ErrorCode.ANALYSIS_NOT_FOUND,
            f"Analysis job {analysis_id} not found or expired.",
            status_code=404,
        )

    async def event_generator() -> AsyncGenerator[dict[str, str], None]:
        try:
            while True:
                # Check for client disconnect
                if await request.is_disconnected():
                    break

                try:
                    event = await asyncio.wait_for(queue.get(), timeout=15.0)
                except asyncio.TimeoutError:
                    # Keep-alive ping comment
                    yield {"comment": "keep-alive"}
                    continue

                if event is None:
                    # Stream finished (complete or failed)
                    break

                # Determine SSE event type
                if event.status == JobStatus.COMPLETED:
                    event_name = "complete"
                elif event.status == JobStatus.FAILED:
                    event_name = "failed"
                else:
                    event_name = "progress"

                yield {
                    "event": event_name,
                    "data": event.model_dump_json(),
                }

                if event.status in (JobStatus.COMPLETED, JobStatus.FAILED):
                    break

        finally:
            job_store.unsubscribe(analysis_id, queue)

    return EventSourceResponse(event_generator(), ping=15)


@router.get(
    "/analyze/{analysis_id}",
    summary="Retrieve final synthesis result or current job status",
)
async def get_analysis_result(analysis_id: str) -> Response:
    """Retrieve the completed AnalysisResult or the active JobState."""
    job_store = get_job_store()
    job = job_store.get_job(analysis_id)
    if job is None:
        raise KairosError(
            ErrorCode.ANALYSIS_NOT_FOUND,
            f"Analysis job {analysis_id} not found or expired.",
            status_code=404,
        )

    if job.state.status == JobStatus.COMPLETED and job.state.result is not None:
        # Return full AnalysisResult
        return Response(
            content=job.state.result.model_dump_json(),
            media_type="application/json",
            status_code=200,
        )

    if job.state.status == JobStatus.FAILED:
        err = job.state.error
        code = err.code if err else ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR
        message = err.message if err else "Analysis job failed."
        status_map = {
            ErrorCode.LLM_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE: 503,
            ErrorCode.TOO_FEW_PAPERS: 400,
            ErrorCode.TOO_MANY_PAPERS: 400,
            ErrorCode.INVALID_FILE_TYPE: 400,
            ErrorCode.FILE_TOO_LARGE: 400,
            ErrorCode.INVALID_ARXIV_ID: 400,
            ErrorCode.ARXIV_FETCH_FAILED: 502,
            ErrorCode.PDF_PARSE_FAILED: 422,
            ErrorCode.NO_TEXT_LAYER: 422,
            ErrorCode.RATE_LIMITED: 429,
        }
        status_code = status_map.get(code, 500)
        raise KairosError(
            code=code,
            message=message,
            status_code=status_code,
        )

    # Return intermediate JobState while queued or running
    return Response(
        content=job.state.model_dump_json(),
        media_type="application/json",
        status_code=200,
    )

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Optional
from app.schemas import SectionName, PaperSource


@dataclass
class ParsedPdf:
    page_texts: dict[int, str]  # 1-indexed: 1 -> text
    page_count: int
    meta_title: Optional[str] = None
    char_count: int = 0


@dataclass
class SectionedPaper:
    paper_id: str  # e.g. "p1", "p2", ...
    title: str
    text_for_llm: str
    sections_found: list[SectionName]
    page_texts: dict[int, str]
    page_count: int
    source: PaperSource
    arxiv_id: Optional[str] = None
    filename: Optional[str] = None


@dataclass
class IngestedBatch:
    papers: list[SectionedPaper]
    warnings: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

FILE: backend/app/services/ingestion/validators.py (91 lines)

import hashlib
from app.config import Settings
from app.errors import KairosError
from app.schemas import ErrorCode
from app.services.ingestion.arxiv_fetcher import normalize_arxiv_id


def validate_inputs(
    files: list[tuple[str, bytes]],
    arxiv_ids: list[str],
    settings: Settings
) -> tuple[list[tuple[str, bytes]], list[str]]:
    """
    Validates uploaded PDF files and arXiv ID list:
    1. Magic bytes check: every file must contain b"%PDF-" in first 1024 bytes.
    2. File size bounds: per-file <= max_file_mb, total <= max_total_mb.
    3. De-duplication: files by SHA-256 hash, arXiv IDs by canonical normalized ID.
    4. Paper count bounds: min_papers <= total <= max_papers.
    """
    max_file_bytes = settings.max_file_mb * 1024 * 1024
    max_total_bytes = settings.max_total_mb * 1024 * 1024

    total_bytes = 0
    seen_hashes: set[str] = set()
    deduped_files: list[tuple[str, bytes]] = []

    for filename, data in files:
        # Check individual file size
        file_len = len(data)
        if file_len > max_file_bytes:
            raise KairosError(
                ErrorCode.FILE_TOO_LARGE,
                f"File '{filename}' exceeds {settings.max_file_mb}MB limit ({file_len / (1024 * 1024):.1f}MB).",
                400
            )

        total_bytes += file_len
        if total_bytes > max_total_bytes:
            raise KairosError(
                ErrorCode.FILE_TOO_LARGE,
                f"Total file size exceeds {settings.max_total_mb}MB limit ({total_bytes / (1024 * 1024):.1f}MB).",
                400
            )

        # Check magic bytes (%PDF-)
        header = data[:1024]
        if b"%PDF-" not in header:
            raise KairosError(
                ErrorCode.INVALID_FILE_TYPE,
                f"File '{filename}' is not a valid PDF document (missing %PDF- header).",
                400
            )

        # SHA-256 de-duplication
        h = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
        if h in seen_hashes:
            continue
        seen_hashes.add(h)
        deduped_files.append((filename, data))

    # De-duplicate arXiv IDs using canonical normalization
    seen_arxiv: set[str] = set()
    deduped_arxiv: list[str] = []
    for aid in arxiv_ids:
        raw_aid = aid.strip()
        if not raw_aid:
            continue
        # Canonicalize format (e.g. 'https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.12345v1' -> '2309.12345')
        canonical_id = normalize_arxiv_id(raw_aid)
        if canonical_id in seen_arxiv:
            continue
        seen_arxiv.add(canonical_id)
        deduped_arxiv.append(canonical_id)

    total_count = len(deduped_files) + len(deduped_arxiv)

    if total_count < settings.min_papers:
        raise KairosError(
            ErrorCode.TOO_FEW_PAPERS,
            f"At least {settings.min_papers} unique papers are required for synthesis (received {total_count}).",
            400
        )

    if total_count > settings.max_papers:
        raise KairosError(
            ErrorCode.TOO_MANY_PAPERS,
            f"At most {settings.max_papers} papers are allowed per analysis (received {total_count}).",
            400
        )

    return deduped_files, deduped_arxiv

================================================================================
FILE: backend/app/services/ingestion/pdf_parser.py (88 lines)
================================================================================
import re
import threading
import pymupdf
from app.errors import KairosError
from app.schemas import ErrorCode
from app.services.ingestion.models import ParsedPdf

_MUPDF_LOCK = threading.Lock()


def _dehyphenate(text: str) -> str:
    """Rejoins words split across line-breaks with hyphens: 'multi-\\npaper' -> 'multipaper'."""
    return re.sub(r"(\w+)-\n(\w+)", r"\1\2", text)


def _clean_text(text: str) -> str:
    """Normalises paragraph breaks and removes non-printable control characters."""
    text = _dehyphenate(text)
    # Remove control characters except standard whitespace
    text = re.sub(r"[\x00-\x08\x0b\x0c\x0e-\x1f\x7f-\x9f]", "", text)
    # Normalise 3+ newlines to double newline
    text = re.sub(r"\n{3,}", "\n\n", text)
    return text.strip()


def parse_pdf(data: bytes, filename: str = "paper.pdf") -> ParsedPdf:
    """
    Parses a PDF from bytes using PyMuPDF.
    - Protected by module-level lock (_MUPDF_LOCK) for C-library thread safety.
    - Extracts text per page (1-indexed) in document stream order (sort=False) to preserve two-column layouts.
    - Checks for sufficient text density (>= 200 mean chars/page).
    - Extracts metadata title if available.
    - Raises KairosError(NO_TEXT_LAYER) or KairosError(PDF_PARSE_FAILED).
    """
    with _MUPDF_LOCK:
        try:
            doc = pymupdf.open(stream=data, filetype="pdf")
        except Exception as exc:
            raise KairosError(
                ErrorCode.PDF_PARSE_FAILED,
                f"Failed to open or parse PDF '{filename}': {exc}",
                400
            ) from exc

        try:
            page_count = len(doc)
            if page_count == 0:
                raise KairosError(
                    ErrorCode.PDF_PARSE_FAILED,
                    f"PDF '{filename}' contains 0 pages.",
                    400
                )

            page_texts: dict[int, str] = {}
            total_chars = 0

            for page_idx, page in enumerate(doc, start=1):
                # Use sort=False (stream/reading order) to prevent column interleaving in multi-column layouts
                raw_text = page.get_text("text", sort=False)
                cleaned = _clean_text(raw_text)
                page_texts[page_idx] = cleaned
                total_chars += len(cleaned)

            # Scanned PDF check: mean chars per page must be >= 200
            mean_chars = total_chars / page_count
            if mean_chars < 200:
                raise KairosError(
                    ErrorCode.NO_TEXT_LAYER,
                    f"PDF '{filename}' appears to be scanned or lacks an extractable text layer "
                    f"(mean {mean_chars:.0f} chars/page < 200).",
                    400
                )

            # Meta title extraction
            meta_title = None
            if doc.metadata:
                raw_title = doc.metadata.get("title", "")
                if raw_title and len(raw_title.strip()) > 4 and not raw_title.lower().startswith("untitled"):
                    meta_title = raw_title.strip()

            return ParsedPdf(
                page_texts=page_texts,
                page_count=page_count,
                meta_title=meta_title,
                char_count=total_chars
            )
        finally:
            doc.close()

FILE: backend/app/services/ingestion/sectioner.py (289 lines)

import re
from app.config import Settings
from app.schemas import SectionName, PaperSource
from app.services.ingestion.models import ParsedPdf, SectionedPaper

HEADING_RE = re.compile(
    r"^\s*(?:\d+(?:\.\d+)*\.?|[IVX]+\.)?\s*(abstract|introduction|related work|background|method(?:s|ology)?|approach|"
    r"materials and methods|experimental setup|experiments?|implementation details|results?|evaluation|discussion|"
    r"conclusions?|limitations?|future work|references|bibliography|acknowledg(?:e)?ments?)(?:\s*[:\.\-]?\s*$)",
    re.IGNORECASE | re.MULTILINE,
)

STOP_HEADINGS = {
    "references",
    "bibliography",
}

SECTION_MAP: dict[str, SectionName] = {
    "abstract": SectionName.ABSTRACT,
    "method": SectionName.METHODOLOGY,
    "methods": SectionName.METHODOLOGY,
    "methodology": SectionName.METHODOLOGY,
    "approach": SectionName.METHODOLOGY,
    "materials and methods": SectionName.METHODOLOGY,
    "experimental setup": SectionName.EXPERIMENTAL_SETUP,
    "experiments": SectionName.EXPERIMENTAL_SETUP,
    "implementation details": SectionName.EXPERIMENTAL_SETUP,
    "results": SectionName.RESULTS,
    "evaluation": SectionName.RESULTS,
    "discussion": SectionName.DISCUSSION,
    "conclusion": SectionName.CONCLUSION,
    "conclusions": SectionName.CONCLUSION,
    "limitation": SectionName.LIMITATIONS,
    "limitations": SectionName.LIMITATIONS,
    "introduction": SectionName.OTHER,
    "related work": SectionName.OTHER,
    "background": SectionName.OTHER,
    "future work": SectionName.OTHER,
    "acknowledgement": SectionName.OTHER,
    "acknowledgements": SectionName.OTHER,
    "acknowledgment": SectionName.OTHER,
    "acknowledgments": SectionName.OTHER,
}


def _classify_heading(raw_match: str) -> str | SectionName:
    norm = raw_match.strip().lower()
    if norm in STOP_HEADINGS:
        return "STOP"
    return SECTION_MAP.get(norm, SectionName.OTHER)


def section_paper(
    parsed: ParsedPdf,
    paper_id: str,
    title: str,
    source: PaperSource,
    arxiv_id: str | None = None,
    filename: str | None = None,
    settings: Settings | None = None,
) -> SectionedPaper:
    """
    Splits ParsedPdf page_texts into semantic sections with character budgeting and markers:
    [[SECTION: X]]
    [[PAGE n]]
    """
    max_chars = settings.max_paper_chars if settings else 24000

    # Stream of tuples: (page_num, section_name, text_segment)
    sections_found_set: set[SectionName] = set()
    raw_segments: list[tuple[int, SectionName, str]] = []

    current_section = SectionName.ABSTRACT
    stopped = False

    for page_num in sorted(parsed.page_texts.keys()):
        if stopped:
            break
        text = parsed.page_texts[page_num]
        if not text:
            continue

        # Find heading matches on this page
        matches = list(HEADING_RE.finditer(text))
        if not matches:
            # Entire page belongs to current section
            raw_segments.append((page_num, current_section, text))
            sections_found_set.add(current_section)
            continue

        last_pos = 0
        for m in matches:
            heading_word = m.group(1).lower().strip()
            heading_type = _classify_heading(heading_word)

            # Preceding text belongs to previous section
            pre_text = text[last_pos:m.start()].strip()
            if pre_text:
                raw_segments.append((page_num, current_section, pre_text))
                sections_found_set.add(current_section)

            if heading_type == "STOP":
                stopped = True
                break

            current_section = heading_type
            sections_found_set.add(current_section)
            last_pos = m.end()

        if not stopped and last_pos < len(text):
            trailing_text = text[last_pos:].strip()
            if trailing_text:
                raw_segments.append((page_num, current_section, trailing_text))
                sections_found_set.add(current_section)

    # 2. Check if meaningful headings were detected
    has_meaningful_headings = any(s != SectionName.ABSTRACT and s != SectionName.OTHER for s in sections_found_set)

    if not has_meaningful_headings:
        # Fallback to head/tail strategy: 6k head (ABSTRACT), up to 17k tail (OTHER) leaving 1k marker headroom
        total_avail = sum(len(t) for t in parsed.page_texts.values())
        if total_avail <= 6000:
            head_budget = max(200, total_avail // 2)
        else:
            head_budget = 6000

        tail_budget = max(0, max_chars - head_budget - 1000)

        head_segments: list[tuple[int, SectionName, str]] = []
        tail_candidates: list[tuple[int, str]] = []
        chars_left_head = head_budget

        for p in sorted(parsed.page_texts.keys()):
            t = parsed.page_texts[p]
            if not t:
                continue
            if chars_left_head > 0:
                if len(t) <= chars_left_head:
                    head_segments.append((p, SectionName.ABSTRACT, t))
                    chars_left_head -= len(t)
                else:
                    head_segments.append((p, SectionName.ABSTRACT, t[:chars_left_head]))
                    tail_candidates.append((p, t[chars_left_head:]))
                    chars_left_head = 0
            else:
                tail_candidates.append((p, t))

        # Build tail segments from remaining text
        total_tail_avail = sum(len(t) for _, t in tail_candidates)
        tail_segments: list[tuple[int, SectionName, str]] = []

        if total_tail_avail <= tail_budget:
            for p, t in tail_candidates:
                if t.strip():
                    tail_segments.append((p, SectionName.OTHER, t))
        else:
            # Take the final tail_budget characters from the end of the unsectioned paper
            chars_to_skip = total_tail_avail - tail_budget
            curr_skipped = 0
            for p, t in tail_candidates:
                if curr_skipped + len(t) <= chars_to_skip:
                    curr_skipped += len(t)
                    continue
                elif curr_skipped < chars_to_skip:
                    offset = chars_to_skip - curr_skipped
                    curr_skipped = chars_to_skip
                    tail_segments.append((p, SectionName.OTHER, t[offset:]))
                else:
                    tail_segments.append((p, SectionName.OTHER, t))

        output_parts: list[str] = ["[[SECTION: ABSTRACT]]"]
        for p, _, chunk in head_segments:
            if chunk.strip():
                output_parts.append(f"[[PAGE {p}]]\n{chunk}")

        if tail_segments:
            output_parts.append("[[SECTION: OTHER]]")
            for p, _, chunk in tail_segments:
                if chunk.strip():
                    output_parts.append(f"[[PAGE {p}]]\n{chunk}")

        text_for_llm = "\n\n".join(output_parts)
        if len(text_for_llm) > max_chars:
            text_for_llm = text_for_llm[:max_chars]

        sections_found = [SectionName.ABSTRACT]
        if tail_segments:
            sections_found.append(SectionName.OTHER)

        return SectionedPaper(
            paper_id=paper_id,
            title=title,
            text_for_llm=text_for_llm,
            sections_found=sections_found,
            page_texts=parsed.page_texts,
            page_count=parsed.page_count,
            source=source,
            arxiv_id=arxiv_id,
            filename=filename,
        )

    # 3. Apply Group Character Budgets:
    # 2500 + 6500 + 7500 + 5500 + 1000 = 23,000 chars total, leaving ~1,000 chars headroom for markers
    group_budgets = {
        "abstract": 2500,
        "methods": 6500,
        "results": 7500,
        "discussion": 5500,
    }

    group_mapping = {
        SectionName.ABSTRACT: "abstract",
        SectionName.METHODOLOGY: "methods",
        SectionName.EXPERIMENTAL_SETUP: "methods",
        SectionName.RESULTS: "results",
        SectionName.DISCUSSION: "discussion",
        SectionName.CONCLUSION: "discussion",
        SectionName.LIMITATIONS: "discussion",
        SectionName.OTHER: "other",
    }

    grouped_segments: dict[str, list[tuple[int, SectionName, str]]] = {
        "abstract": [],
        "methods": [],
        "results": [],
        "discussion": [],
        "other": [],
    }

    for p, sec, text in raw_segments:
        grp = group_mapping.get(sec, "other")
        grouped_segments[grp].append((p, sec, text))

    budgeted_output_by_group: dict[str, list[tuple[int, SectionName, str]]] = {}
    for grp, segs in grouped_segments.items():
        budget = group_budgets.get(grp, 1000)
        curr_chars = 0
        kept_segs = []
        for p, sec, t in segs:
            rem = budget - curr_chars
            if rem <= 0:
                break
            if len(t) <= rem:
                kept_segs.append((p, sec, t))
                curr_chars += len(t)
            else:
                kept_segs.append((p, sec, t[:rem]))
                curr_chars += rem
                break
        budgeted_output_by_group[grp] = kept_segs

    # 4. Construct model-ready text with markers
    ordered_groups = ["abstract", "methods", "results", "discussion", "other"]
    output_sections: list[str] = []
    final_sections_found: list[SectionName] = []

    for grp in ordered_groups:
        segs = budgeted_output_by_group.get(grp, [])
        if not segs:
            continue

        curr_sec = None
        for p, sec, chunk in segs:
            if not chunk.strip():
                continue
            if sec not in final_sections_found:
                final_sections_found.append(sec)

            if sec != curr_sec:
                output_sections.append(f"[[SECTION: {sec.value}]]")
                curr_sec = sec

            output_sections.append(f"[[PAGE {p}]]\n{chunk}")

    text_for_llm = "\n\n".join(output_sections)
    if len(text_for_llm) > max_chars:
        text_for_llm = text_for_llm[:max_chars]

    return SectionedPaper(
        paper_id=paper_id,
        title=title,
        text_for_llm=text_for_llm,
        sections_found=final_sections_found,
        page_texts=parsed.page_texts,
        page_count=parsed.page_count,
        source=source,
        arxiv_id=arxiv_id,
        filename=filename,
    )

================================================================================
FILE: backend/app/services/ingestion/arxiv_fetcher.py (152 lines)
================================================================================
import asyncio
import re
import httpx
from app.config import Settings
from app.errors import KairosError
from app.schemas import ErrorCode

ARXIV_NEW_RE = re.compile(r"^(\d{4}\.\d{4,5})(?:v\d+)?$")
ARXIV_OLD_RE = re.compile(r"^([a-z\-]+/\d{7})(?:v\d+)?$", re.IGNORECASE)


def normalize_arxiv_id(raw: str) -> str:
    """
    Normalises all standard arXiv input formats to canonical identifier:
    - 2309.12345
    - 2309.12345v2 -> 2309.12345
    - arxiv:2309.12345 -> 2309.12345
    - https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.12345 -> 2309.12345
    - https://arxiv.org/pdf/2309.12345.pdf -> 2309.12345
    - hep-th/9901001 -> hep-th/9901001
    """
    text = raw.strip()

    # Strip URLs
    text = re.sub(r"^https?://arxiv\.org/(?:abs|pdf)/", "", text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
    text = re.sub(r"\.pdf$", "", text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)

    # Strip arxiv: prefix
    text = re.sub(r"^arxiv:\s*", "", text, flags=re.IGNORECASE).strip()

    # Check new format
    m_new = ARXIV_NEW_RE.match(text)
    if m_new:
        return m_new.group(1)

    # Check old format
    m_old = ARXIV_OLD_RE.match(text)
    if m_old:
        return m_old.group(1).lower()

    raise KairosError(
        ErrorCode.INVALID_ARXIV_ID,
        f"Invalid arXiv identifier format: '{raw}'. Supported forms include '2309.12345', 'arxiv:2309.12345', or 'https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.12345'.",
        400
    )


async def fetch_arxiv_pdf(
    arxiv_id: str,
    client: httpx.AsyncClient,
    semaphore: asyncio.Semaphore,
    max_file_mb: int = 20,
) -> tuple[str, bytes]:
    """
    Fetches a single paper PDF from arXiv with timeout, validation, and retry backoff.
    """
    clean_id = normalize_arxiv_id(arxiv_id)
    url = f"https://arxiv.org/pdf/{clean_id}.pdf"
    headers = {"User-Agent": "Kairos-Paper-Synthesizer/1.0 (Research Assistant; mailto:team@kairos.dev)"}

    res: httpx.Response | None = None
    async with semaphore:
        for attempt in range(2):
            try:
                res = await client.get(url, headers=headers, follow_redirects=True, timeout=30.0)
                if res.status_code in (429, 503) and attempt == 0:
                    await asyncio.sleep(1.5)
                    continue
                break
            except Exception as exc:
                if attempt == 0:
                    await asyncio.sleep(1.0)
                    continue
                raise KairosError(
                    ErrorCode.ARXIV_FETCH_FAILED,
                    f"Network error downloading arXiv paper '{clean_id}': {exc}",
                    400
                ) from exc

    if res is None or res.status_code != 200:
        code = res.status_code if res is not None else "unknown"
        raise KairosError(
            ErrorCode.ARXIV_FETCH_FAILED,
            f"arXiv returned HTTP {code} for paper '{clean_id}'.",
            400
        )

    content = res.content
    max_file_bytes = max_file_mb * 1024 * 1024
    if len(content) > max_file_bytes:
        raise KairosError(
            ErrorCode.FILE_TOO_LARGE,
            f"Downloaded arXiv paper '{clean_id}' exceeds {max_file_mb}MB limit ({len(content) / (1024 * 1024):.1f}MB).",
            400
        )

    if b"%PDF-" not in content[:1024]:
        raise KairosError(
            ErrorCode.ARXIV_FETCH_FAILED,
            f"arXiv response for paper '{clean_id}' did not contain valid PDF data.",
            400
        )

    filename = f"arxiv_{clean_id.replace('/', '_')}.pdf"
    return filename, content


async def fetch_all_arxiv(
    arxiv_ids: list[str],
    settings: Settings,
    client: httpx.AsyncClient | None = None
) -> tuple[list[tuple[str, str, bytes]], list[str]]:
    """
    Concurrently fetches multiple arXiv papers.
    Returns:
    - successful_papers: list of (canonical_arxiv_id, filename, pdf_bytes)
    - warnings: list of error warning strings for failed downloads
    """
    if not arxiv_ids:
        return [], []

    sem = asyncio.Semaphore(settings.arxiv_concurrency)
    close_client = False
    if client is None:
        client = httpx.AsyncClient()
        close_client = True

    try:
        results: list[tuple[str, str, bytes]] = []
        warnings: list[str] = []

        async def _fetch_one(aid: str):
            try:
                clean_id = normalize_arxiv_id(aid)
                filename, data = await fetch_arxiv_pdf(clean_id, client, sem, max_file_mb=settings.max_file_mb)
                return ("OK", clean_id, filename, data)
            except Exception as exc:
                return ("ERR", aid, str(exc), b"")

        tasks = [_fetch_one(aid) for aid in arxiv_ids]
        raw_responses = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)

        for status, ident, fn_or_err, data in raw_responses:
            if status == "OK":
                results.append((ident, fn_or_err, data))
            else:
                warnings.append(f"Failed to fetch arXiv paper '{ident}': {fn_or_err}")

        return results, warnings
    finally:
        if close_client:
            await client.aclose()

================================================================================
FILE: backend/app/services/ingestion/coordinator.py (114 lines)
================================================================================
import asyncio
import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
from app.config import Settings
from app.errors import KairosError
from app.schemas import ErrorCode, PaperSource
from app.services.ingestion.models import IngestedBatch, SectionedPaper, ParsedPdf
from app.services.ingestion.validators import validate_inputs
from app.services.ingestion.pdf_parser import parse_pdf
from app.services.ingestion.sectioner import section_paper
from app.services.ingestion.arxiv_fetcher import fetch_all_arxiv


async def ingest_all(
    files: list[tuple[str, bytes]],
    arxiv_ids: list[str],
    settings: Settings,
) -> IngestedBatch:
    """
    Unified end-to-end multi-paper ingestion pipeline:
    1. Validates file counts, sizes, magic bytes, and deduplicates inputs.
    2. Concurrently fetches arXiv papers (with graceful failure isolation).
    3. Cross-deduplicates arXiv downloaded papers against uploaded PDFs by SHA-256.
    4. Concurrently parses PDFs in thread pools using PyMuPDF.
    5. Handles scanned / broken PDFs with warnings.
    6. Sections text, budgets characters, and assigns stable paper IDs (p1..p10).
    """
    # 1. Input validation & deduplication
    valid_files, valid_arxiv = validate_inputs(files, arxiv_ids, settings)

    all_warnings: list[str] = []

    # 2. Fetch arXiv papers
    arxiv_papers, arxiv_warnings = await fetch_all_arxiv(valid_arxiv, settings)
    all_warnings.extend(arxiv_warnings)

    # 3. Assemble full raw paper list: (filename, data, source, arxiv_id)
    # Cross-deduplicate fetched arXiv PDFs against uploaded PDFs by SHA-256
    seen_hashes: set[str] = set()
    raw_papers: list[tuple[str, bytes, PaperSource, str | None]] = []

    for fn, data in valid_files:
        h = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
        seen_hashes.add(h)
        raw_papers.append((fn, data, PaperSource.UPLOAD, None))

    for aid, fn, data in arxiv_papers:
        h = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
        if h in seen_hashes:
            all_warnings.append(f"arXiv paper '{aid}' is identical to an uploaded PDF (SHA-256 duplicate); skipped.")
            continue
        seen_hashes.add(h)
        raw_papers.append((fn, data, PaperSource.ARXIV, aid))

    if not raw_papers:
        raise KairosError(
            ErrorCode.TOO_FEW_PAPERS,
            "No valid PDF papers were provided or could be retrieved.",
            400,
            details={"warnings": all_warnings}
        )

    # 4. Concurrently parse PDFs in thread pool
    async def _parse_single(item):
        fn, data, src, aid = item
        try:
            parsed = await asyncio.to_thread(parse_pdf, data, fn)
            return ("OK", fn, parsed, src, aid, "")
        except Exception as exc:
            return ("ERR", fn, None, src, aid, str(exc))

    parse_tasks = [_parse_single(item) for item in raw_papers]
    parse_results = await asyncio.gather(*parse_tasks)

    surviving: list[tuple[str, ParsedPdf, PaperSource, str | None]] = []
    for status, fn, parsed, src, aid, err_msg in parse_results:
        if status == "OK" and parsed is not None:
            surviving.append((fn, parsed, src, aid))
        else:
            all_warnings.append(f"Excluded '{fn}': {err_msg}")

    # Check minimum surviving threshold
    if len(surviving) < settings.min_papers:
        raise KairosError(
            ErrorCode.TOO_FEW_PAPERS,
            f"Only {len(surviving)} paper(s) could be successfully parsed. At least {settings.min_papers} are required.",
            400,
            details={"warnings": all_warnings}
        )

    # 5. Section and budget each surviving paper
    sectioned_papers: list[SectionedPaper] = []
    for idx, (fn, parsed, src, aid) in enumerate(surviving, start=1):
        paper_id = f"p{idx}"

        # Clean title
        if parsed.meta_title and len(parsed.meta_title.strip()) > 5:
            title = parsed.meta_title.strip()
        else:
            base_name = Path(fn).stem.replace("_", " ").replace("-", " ")
            title = base_name.strip().title()

        sec_paper = section_paper(
            parsed=parsed,
            paper_id=paper_id,
            title=title,
            source=src,
            arxiv_id=aid,
            filename=fn,
            settings=settings,
        )
        sectioned_papers.append(sec_paper)

    return IngestedBatch(papers=sectioned_papers, warnings=all_warnings)

from dataclasses import dataclass
from app.config import Settings


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Provider:
    """Represents an open-weight LLM inference provider endpoint."""
    name: str
    base_url: str
    api_key: str
    model: str
    license_name: str
    license_url: str


def build_provider_chain(settings: Settings) -> list[Provider]:
    """
    Builds the active provider fallback chain based on settings.llm_provider_chain.
    Providers with missing credentials or unconfigured URLs are skipped.
    """
    chain_names = [p.strip().lower() for p in settings.llm_provider_chain.split(",") if p.strip()]
    active_providers: list[Provider] = []

    for name in chain_names:
        if name == "groq":
            key = settings.groq_api_key.strip()
            if key:
                active_providers.append(
                    Provider(
                        name="groq",
                        base_url=settings.groq_base_url.strip(),
                        api_key=key,
                        model=settings.groq_model.strip() or "llama-3.1-8b-instant",
                        license_name="Llama 3.1 Community License",
                        license_url="https://www.llama.com/llama3_1/license/",
                    )
                )
        elif name == "huggingface":
            token = settings.hf_token.strip()
            if token:
                active_providers.append(
                    Provider(
                        name="huggingface",
                        base_url=settings.hf_base_url.strip(),
                        api_key=token,
                        model=settings.hf_model.strip() or "meta-llama/Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct",
                        license_name="Llama 3.1 Community License",
                        license_url="https://www.llama.com/llama3_1/license/",
                    )
                )
        elif name == "ollama":
            url = settings.ollama_base_url.strip()
            if url:
                active_providers.append(
                    Provider(
                        name="ollama",
                        base_url=url,
                        api_key="ollama",
                        model=settings.ollama_model.strip() or "llama3.2:3b",
                        license_name="Llama 3.2 Community License",
                        license_url="https://www.llama.com/llama3_2/license/",
                    )
                )

    return active_providers

FILE: backend/app/services/llm/json_utils.py (44 lines)

import json
import re
from typing import Any


class InvalidJSON(ValueError):
    """Raised when an LLM response cannot be parsed into a valid JSON object."""
    pass


def parse_json_object(text: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Parses a JSON object from raw LLM output:
    1. Strips markdown code fences (```json ... ``` or ``` ... ```).
    2. Locates the outermost JSON object bounds ({ ... }).
    3. Decodes JSON using raw_decode to gracefully ignore trailing commentary or extra notes.
    4. Ensures the decoded result is a dictionary.
    5. Raises InvalidJSON with diagnostic context if invalid.
    """
    if not text or not isinstance(text, str):
        raise InvalidJSON("Response is empty or not a string.")

    cleaned = text.strip()

    # Strip markdown code fences if present at start/end
    cleaned = re.sub(r"^```(?:json)?\s*\n?", "", cleaned, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
    cleaned = re.sub(r"\n?\s*```\s*$", "", cleaned)
    cleaned = cleaned.strip()

    # Locate outermost starting {
    start = cleaned.find("{")
    if start == -1:
        preview = cleaned[:80].replace("\n", " ")
        raise InvalidJSON(f"No JSON object delimiter found in response: '{preview}'")

    try:
        data, _ = json.JSONDecoder().raw_decode(cleaned[start:])
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise InvalidJSON(f"JSON decode syntax error: {exc}") from exc

    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        raise InvalidJSON(f"Expected JSON object (dict), but got {type(data).__name__}.")

    return data

FILE: backend/app/services/llm/client.py (175 lines)

import asyncio
import random
import re
from typing import Any, Callable, TypeVar
from openai import (
    AsyncOpenAI,
    RateLimitError,
    APITimeoutError,
    APIConnectionError,
    APIStatusError,
    AuthenticationError,
    BadRequestError,
)
from app.config import Settings
from app.errors import KairosError
from app.schemas import ErrorCode
from app.services.llm.json_utils import parse_json_object, InvalidJSON
from app.services.llm.providers import Provider, build_provider_chain
from app.services.prompts.extraction import CORRECTIVE_RETRY_TEMPLATE

T = TypeVar("T")


def _retry_after(exc: RateLimitError) -> float:
    """Extracts Retry-After delay from response headers if available, capped at 5.0s with jitter."""
    delay = 1.5
    try:
        if hasattr(exc, "response") and exc.response is not None:
            header = exc.response.headers.get("retry-after")
            if header:
                delay = min(max(0.5, float(header)), 5.0)
    except Exception:
        pass
    # Add random jitter (0.1s - 0.6s) to prevent concurrent synchronized thundering herd
    return delay + random.uniform(0.1, 0.6)


class LLMClient:
    """
    OpenAI-compatible client managing open-weight LLM inference across a fallback chain:
    - Provider fallback: Groq -> Hugging Face -> local Ollama.
    - Concurrency semaphore (default: 6 concurrent requests).
    - Automatic JSON decoding and schema validation.
    - Single corrective retry upon invalid JSON output.
    - 401/403 provider deactivation without logging secret keys.
    - Rate limit retry once with Retry-After backoff before fail-over.
    - API key scrubbing on unexpected exceptions and error responses.
    """

    def __init__(self, settings: Settings, providers: list[Provider] | None = None):
        self.settings = settings
        self.providers = providers if providers is not None else build_provider_chain(settings)
        self.sem = asyncio.Semaphore(settings.llm_max_concurrency)
        self.disabled: set[str] = set()
        self.last_used_model: str = ""
        self.last_used_provider: str = ""
        self.call_count: int = 0

    async def complete_json(
        self,
        *,
        system: str,
        user: str,
        max_tokens: int,
        temperature: float,
        validate: Callable[[dict[str, Any]], T],
    ) -> tuple[T, str, str]:
        """
        Executes an LLM JSON completion with validation and provider fail-over.
        Returns:
            (validated_result, provider_name, model_id)
        Raises:
            KairosError(LLM_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE): if all configured providers fail.
        """
        last_error = "no provider configured"

        for p in self.providers:
            if p.name in self.disabled:
                continue

            # Safely manage client lifecycle to prevent connection/socket leaks
            async with AsyncOpenAI(
                base_url=p.base_url,
                api_key=p.api_key or "none",
                timeout=self.settings.llm_timeout_seconds,
            ) as client:
                messages = [
                    {"role": "system", "content": system},
                    {"role": "user", "content": user},
                ]

                use_response_format = True

                for attempt in range(max(1, self.settings.llm_max_retries)):
                    text = ""
                    try:
                        async with self.sem:
                            kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {
                                "model": p.model,
                                "messages": messages,
                                "temperature": temperature,
                                "max_tokens": max_tokens,
                            }
                            if use_response_format:
                                kwargs["response_format"] = {"type": "json_object"}

                            try:
                                resp = await client.chat.completions.create(**kwargs)
                            except BadRequestError as bad_req:
                                # Some local models or endpoints reject response_format parameter
                                if use_response_format and "response_format" in str(bad_req).lower():
                                    use_response_format = False
                                    kwargs.pop("response_format", None)
                                    resp = await client.chat.completions.create(**kwargs)
                                else:
                                    raise

                        text = resp.choices[0].message.content or ""
                        parsed = parse_json_object(text)
                        validated = validate(parsed)
                        self.last_used_model = p.model
                        self.last_used_provider = p.name
                        self.call_count += 1
                        return validated, p.name, p.model

                    except (InvalidJSON, ValueError) as err:
                        # Catch JSON parsing error or Pydantic ValidationError (subclass of ValueError)
                        last_error = f"{p.name}: invalid output ({err})"
                        if attempt + 1 < self.settings.llm_max_retries:
                            # Append corrective retry dialog formatted from template
                            messages = list(messages) + [
                                {"role": "assistant", "content": text},
                                {
                                    "role": "user",
                                    "content": CORRECTIVE_RETRY_TEMPLATE.format(error_message=str(err)),
                                },
                            ]
                            continue
                        break

                    except AuthenticationError:
                        self.disabled.add(p.name)
                        last_error = f"{p.name}: authentication failed"
                        break

                    except RateLimitError as rle:
                        last_error = f"{p.name}: rate limited"
                        if attempt == 0:
                            await asyncio.sleep(_retry_after(rle))
                            continue
                        break

                    except (APITimeoutError, APIConnectionError) as net_err:
                        last_error = f"{p.name}: connection error ({net_err.__class__.__name__})"
                        break

                    except APIStatusError as status_err:
                        last_error = f"{p.name}: HTTP {status_err.status_code}"
                        break

                    except Exception as exc:
                        last_error = f"{p.name}: unexpected error ({exc})"
                        break

        # Scrub any provider API keys before raising
        for p in self.providers:
            if p.api_key and len(p.api_key) > 4:
                last_error = last_error.replace(p.api_key, "[REDACTED]")

        raise KairosError(
            ErrorCode.LLM_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE,
            f"All LLM providers failed ({last_error}). Use a demo preset or check LLM credentials.",
            503,
            details={"last_error": last_error},
        )

HARD RULES
1. Output ONLY one JSON object that matches the SCHEMA below. No code fences, no commentary.

      "section": "ABSTRACT" | "METHODOLOGY" | "EXPERIMENTAL_SETUP" | "RESULTS" | "DISCUSSION" | "CONCLUSION" | "LIMITATIONS" | "OTHER",
      "page_number": integer,
      "verbatim_quote": string
    }
  ]
}"""

"""Prompts for Stage 3 — Strict Natural Language Inference (NLI) referee.

Copied verbatim from docs/shared/03-open-weight-models.md §5.
"""

REFEREE_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """You are a rigorous academic referee performing Natural Language Inference between pairs of research claims taken from different papers. For each pair, decide how the two papers relate. You never chat and never use markdown. Output ONLY one JSON object.

LABELS
- AGREE: Under comparable conditions, both claims support the same finding or point in the same direction.
- CONTRADICT: The claims make incompatible assertions about the SAME question under COMPARABLE conditions (same task, population, dataset type and setting), so both cannot be true at once.
- METHODOLOGY_DELTA: The results or conclusions differ, but the difference is plausibly explained by different datasets, sample sizes, models, hardware, metrics, time horizons or boundary conditions that are visible in the claims or the paper context. This is NOT a true conflict.
- UNRELATED: The claims address different questions or variables.

DECISION RULES
1. If both statements could be true at the same time, the label is NOT CONTRADICT.
2. If the conditions differ in a way that explains the difference, choose METHODOLOGY_DELTA, not CONTRADICT.
3. Use only the information given. Do not use outside knowledge about the papers.
4. Judge each pair independently.

CONFIDENCE
A number from 0.0 to 1.0: 0.9+ means the quotes alone make the label unambiguous; 0.7-0.89 means likely; below 0.7 means uncertain.

EXPLANATION
One or two sentences, at most 60 words. Name BOTH papers using their citation labels. State the deciding factor (for example, benchmark, context length, sample size, dataset cleanliness, or opposite recommendations). Do not quote more than 10 words.

OUTPUT SCHEMA
{"results":[{"pair_id": string, "relation": "AGREE"|"CONTRADICT"|"METHODOLOGY_DELTA"|"UNRELATED", "confidence": number, "explanation": string}]}
The "results" array MUST contain exactly one entry per input pair, using the same "pair_id"."""

REFEREE_USER_PROMPT_TEMPLATE = """Evaluate each of the following pairs.

PAIRS (JSON):
{pairs_json}

Return the JSON object now."""

FILE: backend/app/services/quote_verifier.py (128 lines)

import unicodedata
from rapidfuzz import fuzz

_MAP = {ord(c): "-" for c in "\u2010\u2011\u2012\u2013\u2014\u2212"} | {
    0x2018: "'",
    0x2019: "'",
    0x201C: '"',
    0x201D: '"',
}


def normalize_with_map(text: str) -> tuple[str, list[int]]:
    """
    Normalises string into standard matching form while maintaining character index mapping:
    - NFKC normalisation.
    - Curly quotes mapped to ASCII single/double quotes.
    - Unicode dashes mapped to ASCII hyphen.
    - Collapses consecutive whitespace to a single space.
    - casefold() for case-insensitive matching.
    Returns:
        (normalised_string, list_of_original_indices)
    """
    out: list[str] = []
    idx: list[int] = []
    prev_space = True

    for i, ch in enumerate(text):
        for c in unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", ch).translate(_MAP):
            if c.isspace():
                if prev_space:
                    continue
                c, prev_space = " ", True
            else:
                prev_space = False

            for cc in c.casefold():
                out.append(cc)
                idx.append(i)

    return "".join(out), idx


def _slice(original: str, idx: list[int], start: int, end: int) -> str | None:
    """Extracts the true verbatim substring from the original text and validates length (10-600 chars)."""
    if not idx or start >= len(idx) or end <= 0 or start >= end:
        return None

    orig_start = idx[start]
    orig_end = idx[min(end - 1, len(idx) - 1)] + 1
    text = original[orig_start:orig_end].strip()

    if 10 <= len(text) <= 600:
        return text
    return None


def verify_quote(
    quote: str,
    pages: dict[int, str],
    claimed_page: int,
    fuzzy_min: int = 92
) -> tuple[str, int] | None:
    """
    Verifies that a quote is an authentic substring of the paper text.
    - Rejects quotes shorter than 10 or longer than 600 chars.
    - Rejects quotes containing model prompt markers [[PAGE or [[SECTION.
    - First attempts exact normalized match on claimed page and adjacent pages.
    - Falls back to fuzzy partial ratio alignment with span-length parity and short-page guards.
    - If verified, returns (verbatim_true_substring, verified_page_number).
    - If unverified (hallucination or low confidence), returns None.
    """
    if not quote or not isinstance(quote, str):
        return None

    # Anti-hallucination marker guard
    lower_q = quote.lower()
    if "[[page" in lower_q or "[[section" in lower_q:
        return None

    nq, _ = normalize_with_map(quote)
    nq = nq.strip()
    if len(nq) < 10 or len(nq) > 600:
        return None

    if not pages:
        return None

    # Search claimed page first, then adjacent pages, then outward
    order = sorted(pages.keys(), key=lambda p: (abs(p - claimed_page), p))
    cache = {p: normalize_with_map(pages[p]) for p in order}

    # 1. Exact pass
    for p in order:
        nt, im = cache[p]
        pos = nt.find(nq)
        if pos >= 0:
            sl = _slice(pages[p], im, pos, pos + len(nq))
            if sl is not None:
                return sl, p

    # 2. Fuzzy pass (aligning minor OCR typos, line break wraps, or omitted punctuation)
    # Guards against short-page false positives where nt is shorter than nq
    best = None
    for p in order:
        nt, im = cache[p]
        if len(nt) < len(nq):
            # Cannot align a longer quote inside a shorter page text
            continue

        al = fuzz.partial_ratio_alignment(nq, nt)
        req_min = 97 if len(nq) < 40 else fuzzy_min
        span_len = (al.dest_end - al.dest_start) if al else 0

        # Require high score and span length parity within 20% of quote length
        if (
            al
            and al.score >= req_min
            and (0.8 * len(nq) <= span_len <= 1.2 * len(nq))
            and (best is None or al.score > best[0])
        ):
            best = (al.score, p, im, al.dest_start, al.dest_end)

    if best is not None:
        sl = _slice(pages[best[1]], best[2], best[3], best[4])
        if sl is not None:
            return sl, best[1]

    return None

FILE: backend/app/services/extraction.py (217 lines)
================================================================================
import re
from typing import Any
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, field_validator
from app.config import Settings
from app.schemas import (
    ClaimType,
    ExtractedClaim,
    PaperMetadata,
    PaperSource,
    Provenance,
    SectionName,
)
from app.services.ingestion.models import SectionedPaper
from app.services.llm.client import LLMClient
from app.services.prompts.extraction import (
    EXTRACTION_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
    EXTRACTION_USER_PROMPT_TEMPLATE,
)
from app.services.quote_verifier import verify_quote


class RawClaim(BaseModel):
    """Raw extracted claim from LLM response before quote verification."""
    claim_type: ClaimType
    topic_hint: str
    statement: str
    metric_value: str | None = None
    section: SectionName
    page_number: int
    verbatim_quote: str

    @field_validator("topic_hint")
    @classmethod
    def clamp_topic_hint(cls, v: str) -> str:
        return v.strip()[:80]

    @field_validator("statement")
    @classmethod
    def clamp_statement(cls, v: str) -> str:
        cleaned = v.strip()
        if len(cleaned) < 10:
            raise ValueError(f"Claim statement too short ({len(cleaned)} chars < 10): '{cleaned}'")
        return cleaned[:280]

    @field_validator("page_number")
    @classmethod
    def check_page_number(cls, v: int) -> int:
        return max(1, v)


class RawExtraction(BaseModel):
    """Raw structured extraction response matching 03-open-weight-models.md SCHEMA."""
    title: str = "Untitled Paper"
    authors: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    year: int | None = None
    dataset: str | None = "Not reported"
    sample_size: str | None = None
    methodology: str | None = "Not reported"
    primary_metric: str | None = "Not reported"
    limitations: str | None = "Not reported"
    core_conclusion: str | None = "Not reported"
    claims: list[RawClaim] = Field(default_factory=list)

    @field_validator("year")
    @classmethod
    def valid_year(cls, v: int | None) -> int | None:
        """Validates that publication year falls within reasonable historical/modern bounds (1900-2100)."""
        return v if (v is not None and 1900 <= v <= 2100) else None

    @field_validator("dataset", "methodology", "primary_metric", "limitations", "core_conclusion", mode="before")
    @classmethod
    def coerce_none_strings(cls, v: Any) -> str:
        """Coerces null/empty string metadata values to 'Not reported'."""
        if v is None or not str(v).strip():
            return "Not reported"
        return str(v).strip()


def _generate_citation_label(authors: list[str], year: int | None, title: str) -> str:
    """Generates an academic citation label like 'Vaswani et al. (2017)' or 'Attention Is All... (2017)'."""
    year_str = str(year) if year else "n.d."

    if authors:
        first = authors[0].strip()
        last_name = first.split()[-1] if first else "Anonymous"
        if len(authors) == 1:
            return f"{last_name} ({year_str})"
        elif len(authors) == 2:
            second_last = authors[1].split()[-1] if authors[1].strip() else "Co-author"
            return f"{last_name} & {second_last} ({year_str})"
        else:
            return f"{last_name} et al. ({year_str})"

    clean_title = re.sub(r"[^\w\s]", "", title).strip()
    words = clean_title.split()[:3]
    prefix = " ".join(words) if words else "Study"
    return f"{prefix} ({year_str})"


async def extract_claims_from_paper(
    sec_paper: SectionedPaper,
    llm_client: LLMClient,
    settings: Settings | None = None,
) -> tuple[PaperMetadata, list[ExtractedClaim], list[str]]:
    """
    Runs Stage 1 extraction on a sectioned paper:
    1. Submits sectioned text with [[SECTION: X]] and [[PAGE n]] markers to open-weight LLM.
    2. Validates output with RawExtraction schema and executes corrective retry if needed.
    3. Verifies every claim's quote against raw page text (anti-hallucination filter).
    4. Drops unverified/hallucinated quotes and corrects page numbers if displaced.
    5. Assembles PaperMetadata and ExtractedClaim list with exact Provenance.
    Returns:
        (PaperMetadata, list[ExtractedClaim], list[warning_strings])
    """
    # Escape literal >>> in paper text to prevent breaking prompt boundary
    safe_sectioned_text = sec_paper.text_for_llm.replace(">>>", "> > >")

    user_prompt = EXTRACTION_USER_PROMPT_TEMPLATE.format(
        filename=sec_paper.filename or f"{sec_paper.paper_id}.pdf",
        sectioned_text=safe_sectioned_text,
    )

    raw_data, provider_name, model_id = await llm_client.complete_json(
        system=EXTRACTION_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
        user=user_prompt,
        max_tokens=1800,
        temperature=0.1,
        validate=RawExtraction.model_validate,
    )

    # Resolve title
    final_title = sec_paper.title
    if raw_data.title and len(raw_data.title.strip()) > 5 and not raw_data.title.lower().startswith("untitled"):
        final_title = raw_data.title.strip()

    citation_label = _generate_citation_label(raw_data.authors, raw_data.year, final_title)

    num_match = re.search(r"\d+", sec_paper.paper_id)
    paper_num = int(num_match.group(0)) if num_match else 1
    short_label = f"P{paper_num}"
    palette_index = (paper_num - 1) % 10

    paper_meta = PaperMetadata(
        paper_id=sec_paper.paper_id,
        short_label=short_label,
        citation_label=citation_label,
        palette_index=palette_index,
        source=sec_paper.source,
        arxiv_id=sec_paper.arxiv_id,
        filename=sec_paper.filename,
        title=final_title,
        authors=raw_data.authors,
        year=raw_data.year,
        page_count=sec_paper.page_count,
        sections_found=sec_paper.sections_found,
        dataset=raw_data.dataset or "Not reported",
        sample_size=raw_data.sample_size,
        methodology=raw_data.methodology or "Not reported",
        primary_metric=raw_data.primary_metric or "Not reported",
        limitations=raw_data.limitations or "Not reported",
        core_conclusion=raw_data.core_conclusion or "Not reported",
        extraction_model=model_id,
    )

    warnings: list[str] = []
    kept_claims: list[ExtractedClaim] = []
    dropped_count = 0

    claim_counter = 1
    for raw_claim in raw_data.claims:
        # Verify quote against raw page texts
        verified = verify_quote(
            quote=raw_claim.verbatim_quote,
            pages=sec_paper.page_texts,
            claimed_page=raw_claim.page_number,
        )

        if verified is None:
            dropped_count += 1
            continue

        true_quote, verified_page = verified

        prov = Provenance(
            paper_id=sec_paper.paper_id,
            paper_title=final_title,
            section=raw_claim.section,
            page_number=verified_page,
            verbatim_quote=true_quote,
        )

        # Enforce contract hyphen format: {paper_id}-c{n} (e.g. p1-c1)
        claim_id = f"{sec_paper.paper_id}-c{claim_counter}"
        claim_counter += 1

        extracted_claim = ExtractedClaim(
            claim_id=claim_id,
            paper_id=sec_paper.paper_id,
            claim_type=raw_claim.claim_type,
            topic_hint=raw_claim.topic_hint.strip(),
            statement=raw_claim.statement.strip(),
            metric_value=raw_claim.metric_value,
            provenance=prov,
        )
        kept_claims.append(extracted_claim)

    if dropped_count > 0:
        warnings.append(
            f"Paper '{final_title}' ({sec_paper.paper_id}): dropped {dropped_count} unverified or hallucinated claim quote(s)."
        )

    if len(kept_claims) < 2:
        warnings.append(
            f"Paper '{final_title}' ({sec_paper.paper_id}) yielded only {len(kept_claims)} verified claim(s)."
        )

    return paper_meta, kept_claims, warnings

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from typing import Any, Protocol
import numpy as np

from app.config import Settings, get_settings


class Embedder(Protocol):
    """Protocol for embedding models producing L2-normalised vector representations."""

    def embed(self, texts: list[str]) -> np.ndarray:
        """Embed a list of strings into an L2-normalised 2D numpy array of shape (N, D)."""
        ...


class FastEmbedEmbedder:
    """FastEmbed-based local embedding using ONNX runtime (CPU-friendly, no PyTorch)."""

    def __init__(self, model_name: str = "BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5") -> None:
        from fastembed import TextEmbedding

        self.model_name = model_name
        self._model = TextEmbedding(model_name)

    def embed(self, texts: list[str]) -> np.ndarray:
        if not texts:
            return np.empty((0, 384), dtype=np.float32)

        raw = list(self._model.embed(texts))
        arr = np.array(raw, dtype=np.float32)
        norms = np.linalg.norm(arr, axis=1, keepdims=True)
        return arr / np.maximum(norms, 1e-12)


class HFApiEmbedder:
    """Hugging Face feature extraction HTTP API fallback when local ONNX cannot run."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        token: str,
        model_name: str = "BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5",
        base_url: str = "https://api-inference.huggingface.co/pipeline/feature-extraction",
    ) -> None:
        self.token = token
        self.model_name = model_name
        self.endpoint = f"{base_url.rstrip('/')}/{model_name}"

    def embed(self, texts: list[str]) -> np.ndarray:
        if not texts:
            return np.empty((0, 384), dtype=np.float32)

        import httpx

        headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {self.token}"} if self.token else {}
        with httpx.Client(timeout=30.0) as client:
            resp = client.post(
                self.endpoint,
                headers=headers,
                json={"inputs": texts, "options": {"wait_for_model": True}},
            )
            resp.raise_for_status()
            data = resp.json()

        arr = np.array(data, dtype=np.float32)
        if arr.ndim == 3:
            arr = arr.mean(axis=1)

        norms = np.linalg.norm(arr, axis=1, keepdims=True)
        return arr / np.maximum(norms, 1e-12)


class DeterministicStubEmbedder:
    """Deterministic embedding stub for fast, offline, and reproducible unit tests."""

    def __init__(self, dim: int = 384) -> None:
        self.dim = dim

    def embed(self, texts: list[str]) -> np.ndarray:
        if not texts:
            return np.empty((0, self.dim), dtype=np.float32)

        vecs = []
        for text in texts:
            digest = hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8")).digest()
            seed = int.from_bytes(digest[:4], "little")
            rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
            v = rng.randn(self.dim).astype(np.float32)
            vecs.append(v)

        arr = np.array(vecs, dtype=np.float32)
        norms = np.linalg.norm(arr, axis=1, keepdims=True)
        return arr / np.maximum(norms, 1e-12)


_EMBEDDER_INSTANCE: Embedder | None = None


def get_embedder(settings: Settings | None = None) -> Embedder:
    """Retrieve or initialize the configured singleton embedder."""
    global _EMBEDDER_INSTANCE
    if _EMBEDDER_INSTANCE is not None:
        return _EMBEDDER_INSTANCE

    if settings is None:
        settings = get_settings()

    backend = settings.embedding_backend.lower()
    if backend == "fastembed":
        _EMBEDDER_INSTANCE = FastEmbedEmbedder(model_name=settings.embedding_model)
    elif backend == "hf_api":
        _EMBEDDER_INSTANCE = HFApiEmbedder(
            token=settings.hf_token,
            model_name=settings.embedding_model,
        )
    elif backend == "stub":
        _EMBEDDER_INSTANCE = DeterministicStubEmbedder()
    else:
        _EMBEDDER_INSTANCE = FastEmbedEmbedder(model_name=settings.embedding_model)

    return _EMBEDDER_INSTANCE


def reset_embedder_cache() -> None:
    """Reset the singleton instance (useful in tests)."""
    global _EMBEDDER_INSTANCE
    _EMBEDDER_INSTANCE = None

from __future__ import annotations

from collections import defaultdict
from typing import Sequence
import numpy as np
from sklearn.cluster import AgglomerativeClustering

from app.schemas import ExtractedClaim, ResearchGap, TopicCluster


def claim_to_embed_text(claim: ExtractedClaim) -> str:
    """Format claim for embedding: '{topic_hint}: {statement}'."""
    return f"{claim.topic_hint.strip()}: {claim.statement.strip()}"


def cosine_matrix(emb: np.ndarray, paper_idx: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Compute N x N cosine similarity matrix, masking same-paper pairs with -1.0.

    Args:
        emb: L2-normalised (N, D) embeddings array.
        paper_idx: 1D array of length N with integer paper identifiers.
    """
    if emb.shape[0] == 0:
        return np.empty((0, 0), dtype=np.float32)

    sim = emb @ emb.T
    # Mask same-paper pairs so they are never considered cross-paper candidates
    sim[paper_idx[:, None] == paper_idx[None, :]] = -1.0
    return sim


def find_gaps(sim: np.ndarray, threshold: float) -> list[int]:
    """Find claim indices with no cross-paper match >= threshold.

    IMPORTANT: Evaluated BEFORE top-k and max-pair caps per contract §5.
    """
    if sim.shape[0] == 0:
        return []

    gaps: list[int] = []
    for i in range(sim.shape[0]):
        max_sim = float(sim[i].max())
        if max_sim < threshold:
            gaps.append(i)
    return gaps


def build_research_gaps(
    gap_indices: Sequence[int],
    claims: Sequence[ExtractedClaim],
    sim: np.ndarray,
) -> list[ResearchGap]:
    """Construct contract-compliant ResearchGap models from gap claim indices."""
    result: list[ResearchGap] = []
    for idx in gap_indices:
        claim = claims[idx]
        claim.is_research_gap = True  # F-02: mutate claim in-place
        max_cross_sim = float(sim[idx].max()) if sim.shape[0] > 0 else 0.0
        best_sim = max(0.0, min(1.0, max_cross_sim))
        result.append(
            ResearchGap(
                signal="RESEARCH_GAP",
                gap_id=f"gap-{claim.claim_id}",
                claim_id=claim.claim_id,
                paper_id=claim.paper_id,
                statement=claim.statement,
                provenance=claim.provenance,
                best_match_similarity=round(best_sim, 4),
                reason=f"No cross-paper claim exceeds similarity threshold (best match: {best_sim:.2f}).",
            )
        )
    return result


def candidate_pairs(
    sim: np.ndarray,
    paper_idx: np.ndarray,
    threshold: float,
    top_k: int,
    max_pairs: int,
) -> tuple[list[tuple[int, int]], int]:
    """Select cross-paper candidate pairs applying top-k per paper and similarity threshold.

    Returns:
        (kept_pairs, total_candidates_before_cap)
    """
    if sim.shape[0] < 2:
        return [], 0

    chosen: set[tuple[int, int]] = set()
    papers = np.unique(paper_idx)

    for i in range(sim.shape[0]):
        for p in papers:
            if p == paper_idx[i]:
                continue
            cols = np.where(paper_idx == p)[0]
            scores = sim[i, cols]
            # Top-k indices for this other paper
            sorted_order = np.argsort(-scores)[:top_k]
            for o in sorted_order:
                if scores[o] >= threshold:
                    j = int(cols[o])
                    chosen.add((min(i, j), max(i, j)))

    # Sort descending by cosine similarity with deterministic tie-breaking
    ordered = sorted(
        chosen,
        key=lambda ij: (-float(sim[ij[0], ij[1]]), ij[0], ij[1]),
    )
    total_candidates = len(ordered)
    return ordered[:max_pairs], total_candidates


def calculate_pair_stats(
    paper_idx: np.ndarray,
    candidate_pairs_count: int,
    evaluated_pairs_count: int,
) -> dict[str, float | int]:
    """Calculate pair reduction statistics per contract §5."""
    n = len(paper_idx)
    cross_paper_total = 0
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(i + 1, n):
            if paper_idx[i] != paper_idx[j]:
                cross_paper_total += 1

    if cross_paper_total > 0:
        reduction_pct = round(100.0 * (1.0 - candidate_pairs_count / cross_paper_total), 1)
    else:
        reduction_pct = 0.0

    return {
        "cross_paper_pairs_total": cross_paper_total,
        "candidate_pairs": candidate_pairs_count,
        "referee_pairs_evaluated": evaluated_pairs_count,
        "pair_reduction_pct": reduction_pct,
    }


def cluster_claims(emb: np.ndarray, distance_threshold: float) -> np.ndarray:
    """Perform average linkage agglomerative clustering using cosine distance."""
    if len(emb) < 2:
        return np.zeros(len(emb), dtype=int)

    clustering = AgglomerativeClustering(
        n_clusters=None,
        metric="cosine",
        linkage="average",
        distance_threshold=distance_threshold,
    )
    return clustering.fit_predict(emb)


def build_clusters(
    emb: np.ndarray,
    claims: Sequence[ExtractedClaim],
    distance_threshold: float,
) -> tuple[list[TopicCluster], dict[str, str | None]]:
    """Cluster claims and apply contract discard rules.

    Discard rules:
    - Clusters containing fewer than 2 claims OR fewer than 2 distinct papers are discarded.
    - Discarded claims get cluster_id = None.

    Cluster ordering:
    - Ordered by descending len(claim_ids), then ascending first claim_id.
    - Assigned cluster_id = 'cl-{k}'.
    - Label is the topic_hint of the claim closest to the cluster centroid.
    - mean_similarity is the mean pairwise cosine similarity within the cluster.
    """
    n = len(claims)
    claim_map = {c.claim_id: c for c in claims}
    for c in claims:
        c.cluster_id = None  # F-02: default in-place mutation

    claim_id_to_cluster: dict[str, str | None] = {c.claim_id: None for c in claims}

    if n < 2 or emb.shape[0] < 2:
        return [], claim_id_to_cluster

    raw_labels = cluster_claims(emb, distance_threshold)
    groups: dict[int, list[int]] = defaultdict(list)
    for idx, lbl in enumerate(raw_labels):
        groups[lbl].append(idx)

    candidate_clusters: list[dict] = []

    for _, indices in groups.items():
        if len(indices) < 2:
            continue

        cluster_claims_list = [claims[i] for i in indices]

        # F-05: sort distinct papers numerically by paper number (e.g. p2 before p10)
        distinct_papers = sorted(
            {c.paper_id for c in cluster_claims_list},
            key=lambda pid: int("".join(ch for ch in pid if ch.isdigit()) or 0),
        )

        # Contract discard rule: cluster must span >= 2 distinct papers
        if len(distinct_papers) < 2:
            continue

        # Compute centroid and find closest claim
        sub_emb = emb[indices]
        centroid = sub_emb.mean(axis=0)
        c_norm = np.linalg.norm(centroid)
        if c_norm > 1e-12:
            centroid = centroid / c_norm

        sims_to_centroid = sub_emb @ centroid
        best_sub_idx = int(np.argmax(sims_to_centroid))
        label = cluster_claims_list[best_sub_idx].topic_hint.strip()

        # Compute mean pairwise cosine similarity
        m = len(indices)
        sub_sim = sub_emb @ sub_emb.T
        off_diag_sum = np.sum(sub_sim) - np.trace(sub_sim)
        mean_sim = float(off_diag_sum / (m * (m - 1)))
        mean_sim = max(0.0, min(1.0, mean_sim))

        claim_ids = [c.claim_id for c in cluster_claims_list]
        candidate_clusters.append(
            {
                "label": label,
                "claim_ids": claim_ids,
                "paper_ids": distinct_papers,
                "mean_sim": mean_sim,
            }
        )

    # Sort clusters: descending len(claim_ids), ascending first claim_id
    candidate_clusters.sort(key=lambda c: (-len(c["claim_ids"]), c["claim_ids"][0]))

    topic_clusters: list[TopicCluster] = []
    for k, item in enumerate(candidate_clusters, start=1):
        c_id = f"cl-{k}"
        topic_cluster = TopicCluster(
            cluster_id=c_id,
            label=item["label"],
            claim_ids=item["claim_ids"],
            paper_ids=item["paper_ids"],
            mean_similarity=round(item["mean_sim"], 4),
        )
        topic_clusters.append(topic_cluster)
        for cid in item["claim_ids"]:
            claim_id_to_cluster[cid] = c_id
            if cid in claim_map:
                claim_map[cid].cluster_id = c_id  # F-02: mutate claim in-place

    return topic_clusters, claim_id_to_cluster
