from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
import logging
from typing import Any, Sequence
import numpy as np
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator

from app.config import Settings, get_settings
from app.errors import KairosError
from app.schemas import (
    ClaimComparisonEdge,
    ExtractedClaim,
    PaperMetadata,
    RelationType,
)
from app.services.llm.client import LLMClient
from app.services.prompts.referee import (
    REFEREE_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
    REFEREE_USER_PROMPT_TEMPLATE,
)

logger = logging.getLogger("kairos.referee")

VALID_RELATIONS = {"AGREE", "CONTRADICT", "METHODOLOGY_DELTA", "UNRELATED"}


class RawRefereeItem(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")

    pair_id: str
    relation: str = ""
    confidence: float | None = None
    explanation: str = ""

    @field_validator("relation", mode="before")
    @classmethod
    def clean_relation(cls, v: Any) -> str:
        if isinstance(v, str):
            return v.strip().upper()
        return ""

    @field_validator("confidence", mode="before")
    @classmethod
    def clean_confidence(cls, v: Any) -> float | None:
        if v is None:
            return None
        try:
            val = float(v)
            return max(0.0, min(1.0, val))
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            return None


class RawRefereeOutput(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")

    results: list[RawRefereeItem] = Field(default_factory=list)


def _get_paper_num(paper_id: str) -> int:
    """Extract numeric suffix from paper_id (e.g. 'p3' -> 3)."""
    digits = "".join(c for c in paper_id if c.isdigit())
    return int(digits) if digits else 0


def format_pair_payload(
    claim_a: ExtractedClaim,
    claim_b: ExtractedClaim,
    paper_map: dict[str, PaperMetadata],
) -> tuple[str, ExtractedClaim, ExtractedClaim, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Format pair payload guaranteeing source is from the lower-numbered paper.

    Returns:
        (edge_id, source_claim, target_claim, payload_dict)
    """
    num_a = _get_paper_num(claim_a.paper_id)
    num_b = _get_paper_num(claim_b.paper_id)

    if num_a < num_b:
        source_claim, target_claim = claim_a, claim_b
    elif num_b < num_a:
        source_claim, target_claim = claim_b, claim_a
    else:
        # Same paper number tie-break by claim_id
        if claim_a.claim_id <= claim_b.claim_id:
            source_claim, target_claim = claim_a, claim_b
        else:
            source_claim, target_claim = claim_b, claim_a

    # Contract §4: e-{source without dash}-{target without dash}
    source_clean = source_claim.claim_id.replace("-", "")
    target_clean = target_claim.claim_id.replace("-", "")
    edge_id = f"e-{source_clean}-{target_clean}"

    p_source = paper_map.get(source_claim.paper_id)
    p_target = paper_map.get(target_claim.paper_id)

    citation_a = p_source.citation_label if p_source else source_claim.paper_id
    dataset_a = p_source.dataset if p_source else "Not reported"
    method_a = p_source.methodology if p_source else "Not reported"

    citation_b = p_target.citation_label if p_target else target_claim.paper_id
    dataset_b = p_target.dataset if p_target else "Not reported"
    method_b = p_target.methodology if p_target else "Not reported"

    payload = {
        "pair_id": edge_id,
        "paper_a": {
            "citation_label": citation_a,
            "dataset": dataset_a,
            "methodology": method_a,
        },
        "claim_a": {
            "statement": source_claim.statement,
            "verbatim_quote": source_claim.provenance.verbatim_quote,
        },
        "paper_b": {
            "citation_label": citation_b,
            "dataset": dataset_b,
            "methodology": method_b,
        },
        "claim_b": {
            "statement": target_claim.statement,
            "verbatim_quote": target_claim.provenance.verbatim_quote,
        },
    }

    return edge_id, source_claim, target_claim, payload


async def evaluate_claim_pairs(
    candidate_indices: Sequence[tuple[int, int]],
    claims: Sequence[ExtractedClaim],
    papers: Sequence[PaperMetadata],
    sim: np.ndarray,
    llm_client: LLMClient,
    batch_size: int | None = None,
    settings: Settings | None = None,
) -> list[ClaimComparisonEdge]:
    """Run Stage 3 strict NLI referee across candidate claim pairs.

    Features:
    - Batches pairs by batch_size (default from settings).
    - Calls LLMClient.complete_json with Stage 3 parameters (temperature=0.0, max_tokens=900).
    - Unpacks (RawRefereeOutput, provider_name, model_id) tuple (F-01).
    - Drops UNRELATED pairs per contract §5.3.
    - Prevents retry storms on provider failure by bubbling KairosError immediately (F-03).
    - Retries missing, malformed, or invalid pairs once as single-pair calls.
    - Produces contract-compliant ClaimComparisonEdge objects ordered by confidence descending.
    """
    if not candidate_indices:
        return []

    if settings is None:
        settings = get_settings()

    if batch_size is None:
        batch_size = settings.referee_pairs_per_call

    paper_map = {p.paper_id: p for p in papers}

    # Prepare pair records
    pair_meta: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    pair_order: list[str] = []

    for i, j in candidate_indices:
        edge_id, src, tgt, payload = format_pair_payload(claims[i], claims[j], paper_map)
        pair_order.append(edge_id)
        pair_meta[edge_id] = {
            "source_claim": src,
            "target_claim": tgt,
            "cosine_similarity": float(sim[i, j]),
            "payload": payload,
        }

    # Group into batches
    batches: list[list[str]] = []
    for k in range(0, len(pair_order), batch_size):
        batches.append(pair_order[k : k + batch_size])

    last_model_id = getattr(llm_client, "last_used_model", "open-weight-referee") or "open-weight-referee"

    async def _run_batch(batch_edge_ids: list[str]) -> tuple[list[str], Any]:
        batch_payloads = [pair_meta[eid]["payload"] for eid in batch_edge_ids]
        user_prompt = REFEREE_USER_PROMPT_TEMPLATE.format(
            pairs_json=json.dumps(batch_payloads, indent=2)
        )
        try:
            # F-01: Call LLMClient.complete_json with keyword-only arguments and Stage 3 parameters
            res, provider_name, model_id = await llm_client.complete_json(
                system=REFEREE_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
                user=user_prompt,
                max_tokens=900,
                temperature=0.0,
                validate=RawRefereeOutput.model_validate,
            )
            return batch_edge_ids, (res, provider_name, model_id)
        except KairosError as ke:
            # F-03: Provider failure, do not hide under generic Exception
            logger.error("Referee batch provider failure: %s", ke)
            return batch_edge_ids, ke
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("Batch referee call failed (%s): %s", batch_edge_ids, exc)
            return batch_edge_ids, exc

    batch_tasks = [_run_batch(b) for b in batches]
    batch_outputs = await asyncio.gather(*batch_tasks)

    # Process first-pass results
    resolved_items: dict[str, RawRefereeItem] = {}
    pairs_to_retry: set[str] = set()

    for batch_edge_ids, result in batch_outputs:
        if isinstance(result, KairosError):
            # F-03: Provider unavailable, bubble up immediately instead of 60 individual retries
            raise result

        if isinstance(result, Exception):
            pairs_to_retry.update(batch_edge_ids)
            continue

        raw_output, provider_name, model_id = result
        if model_id:
            last_model_id = model_id

        returned_map = {item.pair_id: item for item in raw_output.results}

        for eid in batch_edge_ids:
            item = returned_map.get(eid)
            if item is None:
                pairs_to_retry.add(eid)
            elif item.relation not in VALID_RELATIONS or item.confidence is None:
                pairs_to_retry.add(eid)
            else:
                resolved_items[eid] = item

    # Single-pair retry for failed / missing / invalid items
    if pairs_to_retry:
        logger.info("Retrying %d referee pairs individually", len(pairs_to_retry))

        async def _retry_single(eid: str) -> tuple[str, RawRefereeItem | None, str | None]:
            single_payload = [pair_meta[eid]["payload"]]
            user_prompt = REFEREE_USER_PROMPT_TEMPLATE.format(
                pairs_json=json.dumps(single_payload, indent=2)
            )
            try:
                res, provider_name, model_id = await llm_client.complete_json(
                    system=REFEREE_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
                    user=user_prompt,
                    max_tokens=900,
                    temperature=0.0,
                    validate=RawRefereeOutput.model_validate,
                )
                for it in res.results:
                    if it.pair_id == eid and it.relation in VALID_RELATIONS and it.confidence is not None:
                        return eid, it, model_id
                return eid, None, model_id
            except KairosError as ke:
                logger.error("Single-pair retry provider failure for %s: %s", eid, ke)
                raise
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.warning("Single-pair retry failed for %s: %s", eid, exc)
                return eid, None, None

        retry_tasks = [_retry_single(eid) for eid in sorted(pairs_to_retry)]
        retry_results = await asyncio.gather(*retry_tasks)
        for eid, item, model_id in retry_results:
            if model_id:
                last_model_id = model_id
            if item is not None:
                resolved_items[eid] = item

    # Assemble edges
    edges: list[ClaimComparisonEdge] = []

    for eid in pair_order:
        item = resolved_items.get(eid)
        if item is None:
            continue

        # Drop UNRELATED results per contract §5.3
        if item.relation == "UNRELATED":
            continue

        meta = pair_meta[eid]
        src: ExtractedClaim = meta["source_claim"]
        tgt: ExtractedClaim = meta["target_claim"]
        sim_val = meta["cosine_similarity"]

        # Ensure explanation conforms to contract length limits (10..500 chars)
        explanation = item.explanation.strip()
        if len(explanation) > 500:
            explanation = explanation[:500]
        elif len(explanation) < 10:
            p_a = paper_map.get(src.paper_id)
            p_b = paper_map.get(tgt.paper_id)
            lbl_a = p_a.citation_label if p_a else src.paper_id
            lbl_b = p_b.citation_label if p_b else tgt.paper_id
            explanation = f"Referee comparison between {lbl_a} and {lbl_b} regarding empirical claim."[:500]

        # Determine cluster_id if both claims share the same cluster
        edge_cluster_id = src.cluster_id if (src.cluster_id and src.cluster_id == tgt.cluster_id) else None

        edge = ClaimComparisonEdge(
            edge_id=eid,
            source_claim_id=src.claim_id,
            target_claim_id=tgt.claim_id,
            source=src.provenance,
            target=tgt.provenance,
            relation=RelationType(item.relation),
            confidence=round(item.confidence, 4),
            cosine_similarity=round(max(0.0, min(1.0, sim_val)), 4),
            explanation=explanation,
            cluster_id=edge_cluster_id,
            referee_model=last_model_id,
        )
        edges.append(edge)

    # Order edges per contract §4: Descending confidence, with CONTRADICT before
    # METHODOLOGY_DELTA before AGREE on ties
    relation_rank = {
        RelationType.CONTRADICT: 0,
        RelationType.METHODOLOGY_DELTA: 1,
        RelationType.AGREE: 2,
    }
    edges.sort(
        key=lambda e: (
            -e.confidence,
            relation_rank.get(e.relation, 99),
            -e.cosine_similarity,
            e.edge_id,
        )
    )

    return edges

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
from typing import Any, Sequence
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field

from app.schemas import (
    ClaimComparisonEdge,
    ConsensusReport,
    PaperMetadata,
    RelationType,
    ResearchGap,
    TopContradiction,
)
from app.services.llm.client import LLMClient
from app.services.prompts.consensus import (
    CONSENSUS_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
    CONSENSUS_USER_PROMPT_TEMPLATE,
)

logger = logging.getLogger("kairos.consensus")


def compute_consensus_score(edges: Sequence[ClaimComparisonEdge]) -> int:
    """Compute deterministic consensus score per contract §6:

    Let A, C, D be the counts of AGREE, CONTRADICT, METHODOLOGY_DELTA edges.
    consensus_score = 0 if A + C + D == 0
    consensus_score = round(100 * (A + 0.5 * D) / (A + C + D)) otherwise
    """
    a = sum(1 for e in edges if e.relation == RelationType.AGREE)
    c = sum(1 for e in edges if e.relation == RelationType.CONTRADICT)
    d = sum(1 for e in edges if e.relation == RelationType.METHODOLOGY_DELTA)
    total = a + c + d
    if total == 0:
        return 0
    return round(100.0 * (a + 0.5 * d) / total)


class RawContradiction(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")
    edge_id: str
    headline: str = ""


class RawConsensusOutput(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")
    executive_summary: str = ""
    agreed_findings: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    top_contradictions: list[RawContradiction] = Field(default_factory=list)
    recommended_next_steps: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)


def build_fallback_consensus(
    papers: Sequence[PaperMetadata],
    edges: Sequence[ClaimComparisonEdge],
    gaps: Sequence[ResearchGap],
) -> ConsensusReport:
    """Construct a high-quality deterministic fallback consensus report when LLM call fails."""
    score = compute_consensus_score(edges)
    a_edges = [e for e in edges if e.relation == RelationType.AGREE]
    c_edges = [e for e in edges if e.relation == RelationType.CONTRADICT]
    d_edges = [e for e in edges if e.relation == RelationType.METHODOLOGY_DELTA]

    summary = (
        f"Comparative literature synthesis across {len(papers)} papers identified {len(a_edges)} points of agreement, "
        f"{len(c_edges)} empirical contradictions, and {len(d_edges)} methodology-driven divergences. "
        f"Overall consensus score is calculated at {score}%. "
        f"{len(gaps)} isolated research claim(s) represent potential gaps in peer literature."
    )

    agreed = [
        f"{e.explanation}"
        for e in a_edges[:5]
    ]
    if not agreed:
        agreed = ["No direct empirical consensus was established across the analyzed papers."]

    top_contra: list[TopContradiction] = []
    for e in c_edges[:3]:
        headline = f"Conflicting empirical outcomes observed between {e.source_claim_id} and {e.target_claim_id}."
        top_contra.append(TopContradiction(edge_id=e.edge_id, headline=headline[:200]))

    next_steps = [
        "Harmonize evaluation metrics and benchmark datasets to isolate true variance from setup deltas.",
        "Replicate conflicting experiments under standardized environmental and hardware configurations.",
    ]
    if gaps:
        next_steps.append("Address identified research gaps through focused empirical trials.")

    return ConsensusReport(
        consensus_score=score,
        executive_summary=summary[:1200],
        agreed_findings=agreed[:5],
        top_contradictions=top_contra,
        gap_ids=[g.gap_id for g in gaps],
        recommended_next_steps=next_steps[:4],
    )


async def generate_consensus_report(
    papers: Sequence[PaperMetadata],
    edges: Sequence[ClaimComparisonEdge],
    gaps: Sequence[ResearchGap],
    llm_client: LLMClient,
) -> tuple[ConsensusReport, str, str]:
    """Generate Stage 4 consensus report via open-weight model with ID validation and fallback.

    Returns:
        (ConsensusReport, provider_name, model_id)
    """
    score = compute_consensus_score(edges)
    gap_ids = [g.gap_id for g in gaps]
    valid_contradiction_ids = {e.edge_id for e in edges if e.relation == RelationType.CONTRADICT}

    data_payload = {
        "papers": [
            {
                "citation_label": p.citation_label,
                "title": p.title,
                "dataset": p.dataset,
                "methodology": p.methodology,
                "primary_metric": p.primary_metric,
            }
            for p in papers
        ],
        "edges": [
            {
                "edge_id": e.edge_id,
                "relation": e.relation.value,
                "confidence": e.confidence,
                "explanation": e.explanation,
                "source_claim": e.source_claim_id,
                "target_claim": e.target_claim_id,
            }
            for e in edges
        ],
        "gaps": [
            {
                "gap_id": g.gap_id,
                "paper": g.paper_id,
                "statement": g.statement,
            }
            for g in gaps
        ],
    }

    user_prompt = CONSENSUS_USER_PROMPT_TEMPLATE.format(
        data_json=json.dumps(data_payload, indent=2)
    )

    try:
        raw_output, provider_name, model_id = await llm_client.complete_json(
            system=CONSENSUS_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
            user=user_prompt,
            max_tokens=700,
            temperature=0.3,
            validate=RawConsensusOutput.model_validate,
        )

        # Validate top contradictions: must match a real CONTRADICT edge_id
        filtered_contradictions: list[TopContradiction] = []
        for it in raw_output.top_contradictions:
            if it.edge_id in valid_contradiction_ids:
                hl = it.headline.strip() or f"Contradiction identified on edge {it.edge_id}."
                filtered_contradictions.append(TopContradiction(edge_id=it.edge_id, headline=hl[:200]))
                if len(filtered_contradictions) >= 3:
                    break

        # Fallback bullets if LLM returned empty lists
        agreed = [s.strip() for s in raw_output.agreed_findings if s.strip()][:5]
        if not agreed:
            a_edges = [e for e in edges if e.relation == RelationType.AGREE]
            agreed = [e.explanation for e in a_edges[:5]] or ["No uniform consensus emerged from the claim pairs."]

        next_steps = [s.strip() for s in raw_output.recommended_next_steps if s.strip()][:4]
        if not next_steps:
            next_steps = [
                "Harmonize evaluation metrics and benchmark datasets across research groups.",
                "Replicate boundary-condition experiments to resolve methodological differences.",
            ]

        summary = raw_output.executive_summary.strip()
        if not summary:
            summary = (
                f"Multi-paper synthesis across {len(papers)} papers identified {score}% consensus. "
                f"Examined {len(edges)} cross-paper relationships and {len(gaps)} potential literature gaps."
            )

        report = ConsensusReport(
            consensus_score=score,
            executive_summary=summary[:1200],
            agreed_findings=agreed,
            top_contradictions=filtered_contradictions,
            gap_ids=gap_ids,
            recommended_next_steps=next_steps,
        )
        return report, provider_name, model_id

    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning("Stage 4 consensus generation failed (%s), using template fallback", exc)
        fallback = build_fallback_consensus(papers, edges, gaps)
        model = getattr(llm_client, "last_used_model", "fallback-template") or "fallback-template"
        provider = getattr(llm_client, "last_used_provider", "template") or "template"
        return fallback, provider, model

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Sequence

from app.schemas import (
    SCHEMA_VERSION,
    AnalysisMode,
    AnalysisResult,
    AnalysisStats,
    ClaimComparisonEdge,
    ConsensusReport,
    ExtractedClaim,
    FixtureProvenance,
    ModelDisclosure,
    ModelRole,
    PaperMetadata,
    ResearchGap,
    StageLatency,
    TopicCluster,
)


def _resolve_model_metadata(model_id: str, provider: str) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
    """Resolve license, license_url, and parameter_count for standard open-weight models."""
    lowered = model_id.lower()
    if "bge-small" in lowered:
        return "MIT", "https://huggingface.co/BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5/blob/main/LICENSE", "33M"
    if "llama-3.2-3b" in lowered or "llama3.2:3b" in lowered or "3.2-3b" in lowered:
        return "Llama 3.2 Community License", "https://llama.meta.com/llama3/license/", "3B"
    if "qwen" in lowered:
        return "Apache-2.0", "https://huggingface.co/Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct/blob/main/LICENSE", "7B"
    # Default to Llama-3.1-8B metadata
    return "Meta Llama 3.1 Community License", "https://llama.meta.com/llama3/license/", "8B"


def build_model_disclosures(
    extraction_model: str = "llama-3.1-8b-instant",
    extraction_provider: str = "groq",
    embedding_model: str = "BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5",
    embedding_provider: str = "fastembed-local",
    referee_model: str = "llama-3.1-8b-instant",
    referee_provider: str = "groq",
    consensus_model: str = "llama-3.1-8b-instant",
    consensus_provider: str = "groq",
) -> list[ModelDisclosure]:
    """Build ModelDisclosure entries for the 4 pipeline model roles."""
    roles_info = [
        (ModelRole.EXTRACTION, extraction_model, extraction_provider),
        (ModelRole.EMBEDDING, embedding_model, embedding_provider),
        (ModelRole.REFEREE, referee_model, referee_provider),
        (ModelRole.CONSENSUS_WRITER, consensus_model, consensus_provider),
    ]

    disclosures: list[ModelDisclosure] = []
    for role, m_id, prov in roles_info:
        lic, lic_url, params = _resolve_model_metadata(m_id, prov)
        disclosures.append(
            ModelDisclosure(
                role=role,
                model_id=m_id,
                provider=prov,
                license=lic,
                license_url=lic_url,
                parameter_count=params,
            )
        )
    return disclosures


def build_analysis_result(
    analysis_id: str,
    papers: Sequence[PaperMetadata],
    claims: Sequence[ExtractedClaim],
    clusters: Sequence[TopicCluster],
    edges: Sequence[ClaimComparisonEdge],
    gaps: Sequence[ResearchGap],
    consensus: ConsensusReport,
    cross_paper_pairs_total: int,
    candidate_pairs_count: int,
    referee_pairs_evaluated: int,
    referee_calls: int,
    pair_reduction_pct: float,
    stage_latencies: StageLatency,
    models: Sequence[ModelDisclosure],
    warnings: Sequence[str] | None = None,
    mode: AnalysisMode = AnalysisMode.LIVE,
    preset_id: str | None = None,
    fixture_provenance: FixtureProvenance = FixtureProvenance.NOT_APPLICABLE,
) -> AnalysisResult:
    """Construct and validate contract-compliant AnalysisResult."""
    stats = AnalysisStats(
        paper_count=len(papers),
        claim_count=len(claims),
        cross_paper_pairs_total=cross_paper_pairs_total,
        candidate_pairs=candidate_pairs_count,
        referee_pairs_evaluated=referee_pairs_evaluated,
        referee_calls=referee_calls,
        pair_reduction_pct=pair_reduction_pct,
        stage_latency_ms=stage_latencies,
    )

    final_warnings = list(warnings or [])

    return AnalysisResult(
        schema_version=SCHEMA_VERSION,
        analysis_id=analysis_id,
        mode=mode,
        preset_id=preset_id,
        fixture_provenance=fixture_provenance,
        generated_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        papers=list(papers),
        claims=list(claims),
        clusters=list(clusters),
        edges=list(edges),
        gaps=list(gaps),
        consensus=consensus,
        stats=stats,
        models=list(models),
        warnings=final_warnings,
    )

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import logging
from typing import Optional
import uuid

from app.schemas import (
    AnalysisResult,
    ErrorBody,
    JobState,
    JobStatus,
    PipelineStage,
    ProgressEvent,
)

logger = logging.getLogger("kairos.jobs")


class JobRecord:
    def __init__(self, analysis_id: str) -> None:
        self.analysis_id = analysis_id
        self.state = JobState(
            analysis_id=analysis_id,
            status=JobStatus.QUEUED,
            stage=PipelineStage.INGESTION,
            progress_pct=0,
            error=None,
            result=None,
        )
        self.created_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        self.last_event: Optional[ProgressEvent] = None
        self.subscribers: set[asyncio.Queue[ProgressEvent | None]] = set()

    def publish_progress(
        self,
        stage: PipelineStage,
        status: JobStatus,
        progress_pct: int,
        message: str,
    ) -> ProgressEvent:
        """Update job state and broadcast progress event to all subscribers."""
        self.state.stage = stage
        self.state.status = status
        self.state.progress_pct = max(0, min(100, progress_pct))

        event = ProgressEvent(
            analysis_id=self.analysis_id,
            stage=stage,
            status=status,
            progress_pct=self.state.progress_pct,
            message=message,
            timestamp=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        )
        self.last_event = event

        for q in list(self.subscribers):
            try:
                q.put_nowait(event)
            except Exception:
                self.subscribers.discard(q)

        return event

    def complete(self, result: AnalysisResult) -> ProgressEvent:
        """Mark job as completed and broadcast final event."""
        self.state.result = result
        event = self.publish_progress(
            stage=PipelineStage.DONE,
            status=JobStatus.COMPLETED,
            progress_pct=100,
            message="Analysis complete.",
        )
        # Notify subscribers that stream has ended
        for q in list(self.subscribers):
            try:
                q.put_nowait(None)
            except Exception:
                pass
        return event

    def fail(self, error: ErrorBody) -> ProgressEvent:
        """Mark job as failed and broadcast error event."""
        self.state.error = error
        event = self.publish_progress(
            stage=self.state.stage,
            status=JobStatus.FAILED,
            progress_pct=self.state.progress_pct,
            message=error.message,
        )
        for q in list(self.subscribers):
            try:
                q.put_nowait(None)
            except Exception:
                pass
        return event


class JobStore:
    """Thread-safe and async in-memory job store with SSE fanout and TTL cleanup."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._jobs: dict[str, JobRecord] = {}
        self._lock = asyncio.Lock()

    def generate_analysis_id(self) -> str:
        return f"ana-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"

    def create_job(self, analysis_id: str | None = None) -> JobRecord:
        if analysis_id is None:
            analysis_id = self.generate_analysis_id()

        record = JobRecord(analysis_id)
        self._jobs[analysis_id] = record
        # Initial event
        record.publish_progress(
            stage=PipelineStage.INGESTION,
            status=JobStatus.QUEUED,
            progress_pct=0,
            message="Job queued for processing.",
        )
        return record

    def get_job(self, analysis_id: str) -> JobRecord | None:
        return self._jobs.get(analysis_id)

    def subscribe(self, analysis_id: str) -> asyncio.Queue[ProgressEvent | None] | None:
        """Subscribe to job progress events. Replays the latest event if available."""
        record = self._jobs.get(analysis_id)
        if record is None:
            return None

        q: asyncio.Queue[ProgressEvent | None] = asyncio.Queue()
        record.subscribers.add(q)

        # Replay last event if available
        if record.last_event is not None:
            q.put_nowait(record.last_event)

        # If job is already complete or failed, signal stream completion
        if record.state.status in (JobStatus.COMPLETED, JobStatus.FAILED):
            q.put_nowait(None)

        return q

    def unsubscribe(self, analysis_id: str, q: asyncio.Queue[ProgressEvent | None]) -> None:
        record = self._jobs.get(analysis_id)
        if record is not None:
            record.subscribers.discard(q)

    def cleanup_expired(self, ttl_minutes: int = 60) -> int:
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        to_delete = []
        for aid, rec in self._jobs.items():
            age_minutes = (now - rec.created_at).total_seconds() / 60.0
            if age_minutes > ttl_minutes:
                to_delete.append(aid)

        for aid in to_delete:
            del self._jobs[aid]

        return len(to_delete)


# Global singleton instance
_JOB_STORE_INSTANCE: JobStore = JobStore()


def get_job_store() -> JobStore:
    return _JOB_STORE_INSTANCE

FILE: backend/app/services/rate_limit.py (52 lines)

from __future__ import annotations

from collections import defaultdict
import math
import time

from app.config import get_settings
from app.errors import KairosError
from app.schemas import ErrorCode


class RateLimiter:
    """In-memory sliding-window rate limiter per client IP."""

    def __init__(self, limit_per_minute: int | None = None) -> None:
        if limit_per_minute is None:
            limit_per_minute = get_settings().analyze_rate_limit_per_minute
        self.limit = limit_per_minute
        self.window_seconds = 60.0
        self._history: dict[str, list[float]] = defaultdict(list)

    def check_rate_limit(self, client_ip: str) -> None:
        """Verify client IP has not exceeded rate limit. Raises KairosError(429) if exceeded."""
        now = time.monotonic()
        cutoff = now - self.window_seconds

        # Clean history
        records = [t for t in self._history[client_ip] if t > cutoff]
        self._history[client_ip] = records

        if len(records) >= self.limit:
            oldest = records[0]
            retry_after = max(1, math.ceil(self.window_seconds - (now - oldest)))
            raise KairosError(
                ErrorCode.RATE_LIMITED,
                f"Rate limit exceeded: maximum {self.limit} requests per minute.",
                status_code=429,
                details={"retry_after": retry_after},
            )

        self._history[client_ip].append(now)

    def reset(self) -> None:
        """Clear all rate limit history (useful in tests)."""
        self._history.clear()


_RATE_LIMITER_INSTANCE = RateLimiter()


def get_rate_limiter() -> RateLimiter:
    return _RATE_LIMITER_INSTANCE

FILE: backend/app/services/pipeline.py (373 lines)
================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import logging
import time
from typing import Sequence
import numpy as np

from app.config import Settings, get_settings
from app.errors import KairosError
from app.schemas import (
    AnalysisMode,
    AnalysisResult,
    ErrorCode,
    ExtractedClaim,
    JobStatus,
    PaperMetadata,
    PipelineStage,
    StageLatency,
)
from app.services.consensus import generate_consensus_report
from app.services.embeddings import FastEmbedEmbedder, get_embedder
from app.services.extraction import extract_claims_from_paper
from app.services.graph_builder import build_analysis_result, build_model_disclosures
from app.services.ingestion.coordinator import ingest_all
from app.services.jobs import JobRecord, JobStore
from app.services.llm.client import LLMClient
from app.services.referee import evaluate_claim_pairs
from app.services.similarity import (
    build_clusters,
    build_research_gaps,
    calculate_pair_stats,
    candidate_pairs,
    claim_to_embed_text,
    cosine_matrix,
    find_gaps,
)

logger = logging.getLogger("kairos.pipeline")


async def run_pipeline(
    analysis_id: str,
    raw_files: list[tuple[str, bytes]],
    arxiv_ids: list[str],
    job_store: JobStore,
    settings: Settings | None = None,
    llm_client: LLMClient | None = None,
    embedder: FastEmbedEmbedder | None = None,
) -> AnalysisResult:
    """Execute the end-to-end 5-stage synthesis pipeline:

    Stage 0: Ingestion & parsing (0% -> 20%)
    Stage 1: LLM claim extraction & quote verification (20% -> 55%)
    Stage 2: Embeddings, cosine matrix, pre-cap gaps & clustering (55% -> 75%)
    Stage 3: Strict NLI referee (75% -> 90%)
    Stage 4: Consensus report, graph assembly & final validation (90% -> 100%)
    """
    if settings is None:
        settings = get_settings()

    job_record = job_store.get_job(analysis_id)
    if job_record is None:
        raise KairosError(
            ErrorCode.ANALYSIS_NOT_FOUND,
            f"Job {analysis_id} not found in store.",
            status_code=404,
        )

    pipeline_start = time.perf_counter()
    warnings: list[str] = []

    async def _execute() -> AnalysisResult:
        nonlocal llm_client, embedder
        if llm_client is None:
            llm_client = LLMClient(settings)
        if embedder is None:
            embedder = get_embedder(settings)

        # =========================================================================
        # Stage 0: Ingestion (0% -> 20%)
        # =========================================================================
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        job_record.publish_progress(
            stage=PipelineStage.INGESTION,
            status=JobStatus.RUNNING,
            progress_pct=5,
            message="Ingesting paper sources (PDF uploads and arXiv metadata)...",
        )

        ingested_batch = await ingest_all(
            files=raw_files,
            arxiv_ids=arxiv_ids,
            settings=settings,
        )

        sectioned_papers = ingested_batch.papers
        warnings.extend(ingested_batch.warnings)

        job_record.publish_progress(
            stage=PipelineStage.INGESTION,
            status=JobStatus.RUNNING,
            progress_pct=20,
            message=f"Ingested and parsed {len(sectioned_papers)} papers successfully.",
        )
        ingestion_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)

        # =========================================================================
        # Stage 1: LLM Claim Extraction & Quote Verification (20% -> 55%)
        # =========================================================================
        t1 = time.perf_counter()
        job_record.publish_progress(
            stage=PipelineStage.EXTRACTION,
            status=JobStatus.RUNNING,
            progress_pct=25,
            message="Extracting scientific claims and verifying quotes against source text...",
        )

        # Run Stage 1 extractions concurrently (bounded by llm_client.sem)
        extraction_tasks = [
            extract_claims_from_paper(
                sec_paper=sec,
                llm_client=llm_client,
                settings=settings,
            )
            for sec in sectioned_papers
        ]
        extraction_results = await asyncio.gather(*extraction_tasks)

        all_papers: list[PaperMetadata] = []
        all_claims: list[ExtractedClaim] = []

        for paper_meta, claims, paper_warnings in extraction_results:
            warnings.extend(paper_warnings)
            if len(claims) == 0:
                warnings.append(
                    f"Excluded paper '{paper_meta.citation_label}' ({paper_meta.paper_id}): zero verified claims survived quote verification."
                )
                continue
            all_papers.append(paper_meta)
            all_claims.extend(claims)

        # Guard surviving counts per 03-open-weight-models.md §6
        if len(all_papers) < settings.min_papers or len(all_claims) < 2:
            raise KairosError(
                ErrorCode.TOO_FEW_PAPERS,
                f"Insufficient verified claims extracted across papers ({len(all_papers)} papers and {len(all_claims)} claims survived; minimum is {settings.min_papers} papers and 2 claims).",
                status_code=400,
            )

        job_record.publish_progress(
            stage=PipelineStage.EXTRACTION,
            status=JobStatus.RUNNING,
            progress_pct=55,
            message=f"Extracted {len(all_claims)} verified claims across {len(all_papers)} surviving papers.",
        )
        extraction_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - t1) * 1000)

        # Record active extraction model and provider
        ext_model = getattr(llm_client, "last_used_model", settings.groq_model) or settings.groq_model
        ext_prov = getattr(llm_client, "last_used_provider", "groq") or "groq" 

        # =========================================================================
        # Stage 2: Embeddings, Similarity, Gaps & Clustering (55% -> 75%)
        # =========================================================================
        t2_emb = time.perf_counter()
        job_record.publish_progress(
            stage=PipelineStage.EMBEDDING,
            status=JobStatus.RUNNING,
            progress_pct=58,
            message="Computing dense semantic embeddings for claims...",
        )

        claim_texts = [claim_to_embed_text(c) for c in all_claims]
        paper_id_to_int = {p.paper_id: i for i, p in enumerate(all_papers)}
        paper_idx = np.array([paper_id_to_int[c.paper_id] for c in all_claims], dtype=int)

        # Offload synchronous ONNX CPU embedding to a worker thread
        emb = await asyncio.to_thread(embedder.embed, claim_texts)
        embedding_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - t2_emb) * 1000)

        t2_clust = time.perf_counter()
        job_record.publish_progress(
            stage=PipelineStage.CLUSTERING,
            status=JobStatus.RUNNING,
            progress_pct=65,
            message="Building similarity matrix, detecting research gaps, and clustering...",
        )

        sim = cosine_matrix(emb, paper_idx)

        # Gaps evaluated BEFORE top-k and max-pair caps per contract §5
        gap_indices = find_gaps(sim, threshold=settings.similarity_threshold)
        gaps = build_research_gaps(gap_indices, all_claims, sim)

        # Candidate pairs pre-filtered with top_k per paper
        kept_pairs, total_candidates = candidate_pairs(
            sim=sim,
            paper_idx=paper_idx,
            threshold=settings.similarity_threshold,
            top_k=settings.top_k_per_paper,
            max_pairs=settings.max_referee_pairs,
        )

        # Topic clusters with contract discard rules
        clusters, _ = build_clusters(
            emb=emb,
            claims=all_claims,
            distance_threshold=settings.cluster_distance_threshold,
        )

        pair_stats = calculate_pair_stats(
            paper_idx=paper_idx,
            candidate_pairs_count=total_candidates,
            evaluated_pairs_count=len(kept_pairs),
        )

        if total_candidates > settings.max_referee_pairs:
            warnings.append(
                f"Candidate pairs truncated from {total_candidates} to {settings.max_referee_pairs} highest-similarity pairs."
            )

        job_record.publish_progress(
            stage=PipelineStage.CLUSTERING,
            status=JobStatus.RUNNING,
            progress_pct=75,
            message=f"Formed {len(clusters)} topic clusters and selected {len(kept_pairs)} candidate pairs.",
        )
        clustering_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - t2_clust) * 1000)

        # =========================================================================
        # Stage 3: Strict NLI Referee (75% -> 90%)
        # =========================================================================
        t3 = time.perf_counter()
        job_record.publish_progress(
            stage=PipelineStage.REFEREE,
            status=JobStatus.RUNNING,
            progress_pct=78,
            message=f"Evaluating {len(kept_pairs)} claim pairs with academic NLI referee...",
        )

        initial_calls = getattr(llm_client, "call_count", 0)

        edges = await evaluate_claim_pairs(
            candidate_indices=kept_pairs,
            claims=all_claims,
            papers=all_papers,
            sim=sim,
            llm_client=llm_client,
            batch_size=settings.referee_pairs_per_call,
            settings=settings,
        )

        referee_calls_made = max(0, getattr(llm_client, "call_count", 0) - initial_calls)

        job_record.publish_progress(
            stage=PipelineStage.REFEREE,
            status=JobStatus.RUNNING,
            progress_pct=90,
            message=f"Referee evaluated {len(edges)} verified claim relationships.",
        )
        referee_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - t3) * 1000)

        # Record active referee model and provider
        ref_model = getattr(llm_client, "last_used_model", settings.groq_model) or settings.groq_model
        ref_prov = getattr(llm_client, "last_used_provider", "groq") or "groq" 

        # =========================================================================
        # Stage 4: Consensus Writer & Graph Assembly (90% -> 100%)
        # =========================================================================
        t4 = time.perf_counter()
        job_record.publish_progress(
            stage=PipelineStage.CONSENSUS,
            status=JobStatus.RUNNING,
            progress_pct=92,
            message="Synthesizing literature consensus and executive summary...",
        )

        consensus_report, cons_prov, cons_model = await generate_consensus_report(
            papers=all_papers,
            edges=edges,
            gaps=gaps,
            llm_client=llm_client,
        )
        consensus_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - t4) * 1000)

        # Invariant: total_ms equals the exact sum of the 6 stage latencies per contract §3.5
        total_ms = (
            ingestion_ms
            + extraction_ms
            + embedding_ms
            + clustering_ms
            + referee_ms
            + consensus_ms
        )

        stage_latencies = StageLatency(
            ingestion_ms=ingestion_ms,
            extraction_ms=extraction_ms,
            embedding_ms=embedding_ms,
            clustering_ms=clustering_ms,
            referee_ms=referee_ms,
            consensus_ms=consensus_ms,
            total_ms=total_ms,
        )

        # Map embedding provider per contract §4 ("fastembed-local" or "hf-inference-api")
        emb_backend = settings.embedding_backend.lower()
        if "fastembed" in emb_backend:
            emb_prov = "fastembed-local"
        elif "hf" in emb_backend:
            emb_prov = "hf-inference-api"
        else:
            emb_prov = settings.embedding_backend

        models = build_model_disclosures(
            extraction_model=ext_model,
            extraction_provider=ext_prov,
            embedding_model=settings.embedding_model,
            embedding_provider=emb_prov,
            referee_model=ref_model,
            referee_provider=ref_prov,
            consensus_model=cons_model,
            consensus_provider=cons_prov,
        )

        # Assemble final result
        analysis_result = build_analysis_result(
            analysis_id=analysis_id,
            papers=all_papers,
            claims=all_claims,
            clusters=clusters,
            edges=edges,
            gaps=gaps,
            consensus=consensus_report,
            cross_paper_pairs_total=int(pair_stats["cross_paper_pairs_total"]),
            candidate_pairs_count=int(pair_stats["candidate_pairs"]),
            referee_pairs_evaluated=len(kept_pairs),
            referee_calls=referee_calls_made,
            pair_reduction_pct=float(pair_stats["pair_reduction_pct"]),
            stage_latencies=stage_latencies,
            models=models,
            warnings=warnings,
            mode=AnalysisMode.LIVE,
        )

        job_record.complete(analysis_result)
        return analysis_result

    try:
        return await asyncio.wait_for(_execute(), timeout=float(settings.job_timeout_seconds))
    except asyncio.TimeoutError:
        logger.error("Analysis %s exceeded timeout of %ds", analysis_id, settings.job_timeout_seconds)
        err = KairosError(
            ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR,
            f"Analysis timed out after {settings.job_timeout_seconds} seconds.",
            status_code=504,
        )
        job_record.fail(err.to_body())
        raise err
    except KairosError as ke:
        logger.error("Analysis %s failed with KairosError: %s", analysis_id, ke)
        job_record.fail(ke.to_body())
        raise
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception("Unexpected pipeline failure on %s", analysis_id)
        err = KairosError(
            ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR,
            f"An unexpected internal error occurred during synthesis: {exc}",
            status_code=500,
        )
        job_record.fail(err.to_body())
        raise err

FILE: backend/scripts/tune_threshold.py (93 lines)

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np

from app.services.embeddings import FastEmbedEmbedder


def run_tuning(data_path: Path | None = None) -> list[dict]:
    if data_path is None:
        data_path = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "tests" / "data" / "tuning_pairs.json"

    with open(data_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        pairs = json.load(f)

    print(f"Loaded {len(pairs)} pairs from {data_path}")

    # Build texts to embed
    texts_a: list[str] = []
    texts_b: list[str] = []
    labels: list[str] = []

    for item in pairs:
        ca = item["claim_a"]
        cb = item["claim_b"]
        ta = f"{ca['topic_hint'].strip()}: {ca['statement'].strip()}"
        tb = f"{cb['topic_hint'].strip()}: {cb['statement'].strip()}"
        texts_a.append(ta)
        texts_b.append(tb)
        labels.append(item["label"].lower())

    embedder = FastEmbedEmbedder(model_name="BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5")
    emb_a = embedder.embed(texts_a)
    emb_b = embedder.embed(texts_b)

    # Cosine similarities (dot products since vectors are L2-normalised)
    sims = np.sum(emb_a * emb_b, axis=1)

    thresholds = [round(t, 2) for t in np.arange(0.50, 0.86, 0.05)]
    results = []

    print("\n" + "=" * 65)
    print(f"{'Threshold':<12} | {'Precision':<10} | {'Recall':<10} | {'F1':<10} | {'TP/FP/FN/TN':<15}")
    print("-" * 65)

    best_thresh = 0.65
    max_thresh_with_recall = None

    for thresh in thresholds:
        tp = fp = fn = tn = 0
        for sim, label in zip(sims, labels):
            pred_related = sim >= thresh
            is_related = label == "related"

            if pred_related and is_related:
                tp += 1
            elif pred_related and not is_related:
                fp += 1
            elif not pred_related and is_related:
                fn += 1
            else:
                tn += 1

        precision = tp / (tp + fp) if (tp + fp) > 0 else 1.0
        recall = tp / (tp + fn) if (tp + fn) > 0 else 0.0
        f1 = (2 * precision * recall / (precision + recall)) if (precision + recall) > 0 else 0.0

        if recall >= 0.85:
            max_thresh_with_recall = thresh

        row = {
            "threshold": thresh,
            "precision": round(precision, 4),
            "recall": round(recall, 4),
            "f1": round(f1, 4),
            "tp": tp,
            "fp": fp,
            "fn": fn,
            "tn": tn,
        }
        results.append(row)
        print(f"{thresh:<12.2f} | {precision:<10.3f} | {recall:<10.3f} | {f1:<10.3f} | {f'{tp}/{fp}/{fn}/{tn}':<15}")

    print("=" * 65)
    chosen = max_thresh_with_recall if max_thresh_with_recall is not None else 0.65
    print(f"\nRecommended threshold (highest threshold with Recall >= 0.85): {chosen:.2f}")

    return results


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_tuning()

import json, os, sys
from pathlib import Path

# Add backend directory to path

from app import schemas

OUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

MODELS = [
