def make_radiology():
    papers_data = [
        ('p1', 'P1', 'Smith (2024)', 'Placeholder Paper 1: ResNet-50 on Curated Chest Radiographs', ['J. Smith', 'A. Doe'], 2024, 12, 'NIH ChestX-ray14 (curated n=1,200)', 'Supervised fine-tuning of ResNet-50', '94.2% Diagnostic Accuracy (AUC 0.96)', 'Evaluated only on clean, motion-free curated hospital archives.', 'ResNet-50 models surpass human clinician baselines under curated lab conditions.'),
        ('p2', 'P2', 'Adewale (2024)', 'Placeholder Paper 2: DenseNet-121 in Real-World Clinical Deployments', ['T. Adewale', 'O. Bello'], 2024, 14, 'Rural District Hospital Scans (n=3,400)', 'DenseNet-121 with uncalibrated field inputs', '68.4% Accuracy (AUC 0.72)', 'Severe sensitivity drop when encountering portable X-ray artifacts.', 'Deep architectures fail to maintain diagnostic parity on noisy clinical field data.'),
        ('p3', 'P3', 'Chen (2024)', 'Placeholder Paper 3: Vision Transformer Robustness under Motion Blur', ['L. Chen', 'M. Rossi'], 2024, 16, 'Synthetic Motion-Corrupted MIMIC-CXR (n=5,000)', 'ViT-Base with Patch-Level Attention Dropout', '88.1% Accuracy under 15% blur', 'Computationally prohibitive for low-power edge medical carts.', 'Attention mechanisms retain spatial diagnostic features better than convolutional backbones under motion blur.'),
        ('p4', 'P4', 'Kowalski (2025)', 'Placeholder Paper 4: Multi-Center Generalization of Pneumothorax Detectors', ['P. Kowalski', 'S. Lind'], 2025, 11, '4-Center Collaborative Radiography Cohort (n=8,200)', 'Ensemble ResNet + Swin Transformer with domain adversarial training', '91.3% Multi-Site Balanced Accuracy', 'Requires cross-site calibration to counter differing scanner manufacturer profiles.', 'Domain adaptation effectively bridges the performance gap across disparate radiographic hardware.'),
        ('p5', 'P5', 'Nwachukwu (2025)', 'Placeholder Paper 5: Low-Dose CT vs High-kV Radiography in Triage', ['C. Nwachukwu', 'D. Miller'], 2025, 13, 'Acute Pulmonary Admissions (n=950)', 'Comparative prospective diagnostic trial with expert consensus ground truth', 'High-kV Radiography missed 24% of occult lesions vs Low-Dose CT', 'Radiation dose was 4x higher in the CT cohort.', 'Plain radiography remains inadequate for early-stage ground-glass opacity screening compared to low-dose CT.'),
        ('p6', 'P6', 'Dubois (2025)', 'Placeholder Paper 6: Self-Supervised Pretraining for Pediatric Chest Scans', ['E. Dubois', 'V. Moreau'], 2025, 15, 'Pediatric Pneumonia Cohort (n=2,100)', 'DINOv2 self-supervised pretraining with pediatric fine-tuning', '89.7% Specificity on pediatric consolidations', 'Small sample size for rare congenital thoracic malformations.', 'Self-supervised representations learned on adult scans transfer robustly to pediatric radiology with minimal label tuning.'),
        ('p7', 'P7', 'Patel (2025)', 'Placeholder Paper 7: Radiologist-in-the-Loop Diagnostic Calibration', ['R. Patel', 'K. Gomez'], 2025, 10, 'Academic Teaching Hospital Double-Read Trial (n=4,000)', 'Interactive triage queue with confidence-weighted clinician routing', '18% Reduction in reading turnaround time; 96.1% joint accuracy', 'Clinicians displayed automation bias when model confidence was artificially high.', 'Human-AI collaborative triage outperforms either autonomous AI or unassisted human radiologists.'),
        ('p8', 'P8', 'Takahashi (2025)', 'Placeholder Paper 8: Edge Device Quantization for Portable X-Ray Units', ['H. Takahashi', 'Y. Sato'], 2025, 12, 'Field Bedside Portable Imaging Suite (n=1,500)', 'INT8 post-training quantization on Raspberry Pi / Jetson Orin', '0.4% AUC drop vs FP32 with 3.8x inference speedup', 'Battery thermal throttling observed after 45 consecutive scan evaluations.', 'INT8 quantization enables real-time bedside triage without meaningful loss in diagnostic discrimination.')
    ]
    
    papers = []
    claims = []
    
    for i, p in enumerate(papers_data):
        pid, short_lbl, cit_lbl, title, authors, yr, pages, dset, meth, metric, limit, conc = p
        paper = schemas.PaperMetadata(
            paper_id=pid,
            short_label=short_lbl,
            citation_label=cit_lbl,
            palette_index=i,
            source=schemas.PaperSource.DEMO,
            arxiv_id=None,
            filename=f'{pid}_radiology_placeholder.pdf',
            title=title,
            authors=authors,
            year=yr,
            page_count=pages,
            sections_found=[schemas.SectionName.ABSTRACT, schemas.SectionName.METHODOLOGY, schemas.SectionName.RESULTS, schemas.SectionName.CONCLUSION],
            dataset=dset,
            sample_size=f'n={1000 + i*500}',
            methodology=meth,
            primary_metric=metric,
            limitations=limit,
            core_conclusion=conc,
            extraction_model='meta-llama/Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct'
        )
        papers.append(paper)
        
        claim_defs = [
            (schemas.ClaimType.EMPIRICAL_RESULT, 'Diagnostic Accuracy', f'{short_lbl} reports primary diagnostic metric: {metric}.', metric, f'{pid}-c1', False, schemas.SectionName.RESULTS, 4, f'In our primary evaluation, {title} achieved {metric}.'),
            (schemas.ClaimType.METHOD, 'Methodological Design', f'{short_lbl} employs {meth} as its core approach.', None, f'{pid}-c2', False, schemas.SectionName.METHODOLOGY, 3, f'Our pipeline implements {meth} to ensure reproducible evaluation across test subsets.'),
            (schemas.ClaimType.CONCLUSION, 'Clinical Viability', f'{short_lbl} concludes: {conc}', None, f'{pid}-c3', False, schemas.SectionName.CONCLUSION, pages - 1, f'In conclusion, {conc}'),
            (schemas.ClaimType.LIMITATION, 'Operational Constraints', f'{short_lbl} observes key constraint: {limit}', None, f'{pid}-c4', False, schemas.SectionName.LIMITATIONS, pages - 2, f'A critical limitation of this work is: {limit}'),
            (schemas.ClaimType.EMPIRICAL_RESULT, 'Secondary Evaluation', f'{short_lbl} verifies secondary performance on {dset}.', 'Valid baseline', f'{pid}-c5', pid in ['p5', 'p8'], schemas.SectionName.RESULTS, 6, f'Secondary cross-validation confirms baseline consistency across {dset}.')
        ]
        
        for ctype, topic, stmt, mval, cid, is_gap, sec, pg, quote in claim_defs:
            claims.append(schemas.ExtractedClaim(
                claim_id=cid,
                paper_id=pid,
                claim_type=ctype,
                topic_hint=topic,
                statement=stmt,
                metric_value=mval,
                cluster_id='cl-1' if 'Accuracy' in topic or 'Viability' in topic else ('cl-2' if 'Method' in topic else 'cl-3'),
                is_research_gap=is_gap,
                provenance=schemas.Provenance(
                    paper_id=pid,
                    paper_title=title,
                    section=sec,
                    page_number=pg,
                    verbatim_quote=quote
                )
            ))

    def make_edge(src_cid, tgt_cid, rel, conf, sim, expl, cl_id):
        s_claim = next(c for c in claims if c.claim_id == src_cid)
        t_claim = next(c for c in claims if c.claim_id == tgt_cid)
        eid = f"e-{src_cid.replace('-', '')}-{tgt_cid.replace('-', '')}"
        return schemas.ClaimComparisonEdge(
            edge_id=eid,
            source_claim_id=src_cid,
            target_claim_id=tgt_cid,
            source=s_claim.provenance,
            target=t_claim.provenance,
            relation=rel,
            confidence=conf,
            cosine_similarity=sim,
            explanation=expl,
            cluster_id=cl_id,
            referee_model='meta-llama/Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct'
        )

    edges = [
        make_edge('p1-c1', 'p2-c1', schemas.RelationType.CONTRADICT, 0.92, 0.81, 'Smith (2024) asserts 94.2% diagnostic accuracy ready for clinical triage, whereas Adewale (2024) demonstrates an accuracy collapse to 68.4% on actual hospital scans with motion artifacts.', 'cl-1'),
        make_edge('p1-c3', 'p2-c3', schemas.RelationType.CONTRADICT, 0.95, 0.79, 'Smith claims models surpass human clinician baselines, but Adewale proves models fail to maintain diagnostic parity on noisy clinical field data.', 'cl-1'),
        make_edge('p3-c1', 'p4-c1', schemas.RelationType.AGREE, 0.88, 0.74, 'Both Chen (2024) and Kowalski (2025) find that modern architectural adaptations maintain robust accuracy (>88%) under distribution shifts.', 'cl-1'),
        make_edge('p1-c1', 'p6-c1', schemas.RelationType.AGREE, 0.84, 0.71, 'Dubois (2025) confirms Smith (2024) in finding high baseline accuracy (>89%) when leveraging strong pretrained vision representations.', 'cl-1'),
        make_edge('p2-c1', 'p3-c1', schemas.RelationType.METHODOLOGY_DELTA, 0.89, 0.77, 'Adewale tested uncalibrated DenseNet-121 on field artifacts yielding 68.4% accuracy, whereas Chen applied ViT with attention dropout yielding 88.1%, proving architectural choice drives resilience to blur.', 'cl-1'),
        make_edge('p1-c3', 'p7-c3', schemas.RelationType.METHODOLOGY_DELTA, 0.91, 0.73, 'Smith evaluates fully autonomous AI triage (94.2%), whereas Patel evaluates an interactive radiologist-in-the-loop collaborative workflow (96.1%), showing hybrid deployment mitigates false confidence.', 'cl-1'),
        make_edge('p1-c1', 'p8-c1', schemas.RelationType.METHODOLOGY_DELTA, 0.87, 0.69, 'Smith reports FP32 server inference, while Takahashi tests INT8 quantization on edge hardware, demonstrating minimal accuracy loss (0.4%) at the cost of thermal throttling constraints.', 'cl-3'),
        make_edge('p4-c4', 'p7-c4', schemas.RelationType.AGREE, 0.82, 0.68, 'Both Kowalski (2025) and Patel (2025) highlight that deployment success hinges on rigorous cross-scanner and clinician confidence calibration.', 'cl-2')
    ]
    
    gaps = [
        schemas.ResearchGap(
            signal='RESEARCH_GAP',
            gap_id='gap-p5-c5',
            claim_id='p5-c5',
            paper_id='p5',
            statement='Nwachukwu (2025) evaluates high-kV plain radiography against low-dose CT for occult ground-glass lesions, an imaging modality unexamined by any other paper in this set.',
            provenance=next(c.provenance for c in claims if c.claim_id == 'p5-c5'),
            best_match_similarity=0.43,
            reason='No claim in any other paper investigates comparative low-dose CT diagnostic benchmarks for occult thoracic lesions.'
        ),
        schemas.ResearchGap(
            signal='RESEARCH_GAP',
            gap_id='gap-p8-c5',
            claim_id='p8-c5',
            paper_id='p8',
            statement='Takahashi (2025) identifies battery thermal throttling on mobile X-ray edge hardware after 45 consecutive scans, an operational hardware factor unaddressed by algorithm-centric papers.',
            provenance=next(c.provenance for c in claims if c.claim_id == 'p8-c5'),
            best_match_similarity=0.38,
            reason='No claim in any other paper measures physical hardware thermal throttling or sustained edge-device battery drain during portable bedside triage.'
        )
    ]
    
    clusters = [
        schemas.TopicCluster(
            cluster_id='cl-1',
            label='Diagnostic Accuracy & Real-World Generalization',
            claim_ids=['p1-c1', 'p1-c3', 'p2-c1', 'p2-c3', 'p3-c1', 'p4-c1', 'p6-c1'],
            paper_ids=['p1', 'p2', 'p3', 'p4', 'p6'],
            mean_similarity=0.76
        ),
        schemas.TopicCluster(
            cluster_id='cl-2',
            label='Methodological Adaptation & Clinician Calibration',
            claim_ids=['p4-c4', 'p7-c3', 'p7-c4'],
            paper_ids=['p4', 'p7'],
            mean_similarity=0.71
        ),
        schemas.TopicCluster(
            cluster_id='cl-3',
            label='Hardware Constraints & Edge Deployment',
            claim_ids=['p1-c1', 'p8-c1', 'p8-c4'],
            paper_ids=['p1', 'p8'],
            mean_similarity=0.69
        )
    ]
    
    cross_pairs = 700
    candidate_pairs = 32
    referee_evaluated = 8
    pair_reduction = round(100 * (1 - candidate_pairs / cross_pairs), 1)
    
    consensus = schemas.ConsensusReport(
        consensus_score=56,
        executive_summary='Across the 8 analyzed radiology papers, strong empirical consensus exists that deep learning models achieve high diagnostic discrimination (>88%) under curated or domain-adapted settings. However, a major empirical contradiction divides clean lab studies (Smith 2024: 94.2%) from clinical field deployments (Adewale 2024: 68.4%), where uncalibrated scanner artifacts cause severe diagnostic degradation. Multi-center ensembling and radiologist-in-the-loop workflows consistently outperform autonomous triage.',
        agreed_findings=[
            'Modern neural architectures maintain diagnostic accuracy (>88%) when domain adaptation is applied (Chen 2024, Kowalski 2025).',
            'Pretrained self-supervised adult representations transfer effectively to pediatric radiography with minimal fine-tuning (Dubois 2025).'
        ],
        top_contradictions=[
            schemas.TopContradiction(
                edge_id='e-p1c1-p2c1',
                headline='Clinical readiness dispute: Smith (2024) reports 94.2% accuracy in clean archives, while Adewale (2024) finds performance drops to 68.4% on field hospital scans.'
            ),
            schemas.TopContradiction(
                edge_id='e-p1c3-p2c3',
                headline='Human clinician equivalence controversy: Lab benchmark superiority does not hold on noisy field inputs.'
            )
        ],
        gap_ids=['gap-p5-c5', 'gap-p8-c5'],
        recommended_next_steps=[
            'Standardize motion-artifact augmentation benchmarks across prospective radiography trials.',
            'Incorporate physical edge hardware thermal constraints into portable triage models.',
            'Mandate radiologist-in-the-loop confidence calibration before autonomous deployment.'
        ]
    )
    
    stats = schemas.AnalysisStats(
        paper_count=8,
        claim_count=40,
        cross_paper_pairs_total=cross_pairs,
        candidate_pairs=candidate_pairs,
        referee_pairs_evaluated=referee_evaluated,
        referee_calls=2,
        pair_reduction_pct=pair_reduction,
        stage_latency_ms=schemas.StageLatency(
            ingestion_ms=920,
            extraction_ms=6400,
            embedding_ms=85,
            clustering_ms=14,
            referee_ms=2100,
            consensus_ms=1350,
            total_ms=10869
        )
    )
    
    res = schemas.AnalysisResult(
        schema_version='1.0.0',
        analysis_id='demo-radiology',
        mode=schemas.AnalysisMode.DEMO,
        preset_id='radiology',
        fixture_provenance=schemas.FixtureProvenance.SYNTHETIC_PLACEHOLDER,
        generated_at='2026-10-26T21:00:00Z',
        papers=papers,
        claims=claims,
        clusters=clusters,
        edges=edges,
        gaps=gaps,
        consensus=consensus,
        stats=stats,
        models=MODELS,
        warnings=['This dataset is synthetic placeholder data for hackathon development. Honest open-source disclosure.']
    )
    return res

def make_llm_quant():
    papers_data = [
        ('p1', 'P1', 'Zheng (2024)', 'Placeholder Paper 1: GPTQ 4-Bit Instruction Following Evaluation', ['H. Zheng', 'Y. Wang'], 2024, 12, 'MMLU 5-shot & Alpaca-Eval', 'GPTQ 4-bit weight-only quantization', '0.8 point MMLU accuracy drop vs FP16', 'Context length capped at 2,048 tokens.', '4-bit weight-only quantization preserves standard benchmark reasoning within 1 point.'),
        ('p2', 'P2', 'Okonkwo (2024)', 'Placeholder Paper 2: AWQ Degradation under Long-Context Reasoning', ['C. Okonkwo', 'E. Vance'], 2024, 15, 'LongBench 32k context suite', 'Activation-aware Weight Quantization (AWQ) 4-bit', '7.9 point degradation at 32k tokens', 'Tested only dense decoder models (Llama-2/3).', 'Long-context reasoning is disproportionately vulnerable to low-bit quantization errors.'),
        ('p3', 'P3', 'Al-Mansoor (2024)', 'Placeholder Paper 3: Calibration Set Diversity in Weight-Only Quantization', ['K. Al-Mansoor', 'F. Tariq'], 2024, 11, 'RedPajama diverse subsets', 'Systematic calibration corpus variance testing', 'Perplexity variance of 1.4 across domains', 'English-centric calibration corpora only.', 'Calibration domain mismatch causes severe out-of-domain degradation in 4-bit models.'),
        ('p4', 'P4', 'Schneider (2025)', 'Placeholder Paper 4: Low-Rank Adaptation Mixed Precision Quantization', ['M. Schneider', 'G. Braun'], 2025, 14, 'GSM8k and HumanEval', 'QLoRA 4-bit NormalFloat with FP16 adapters', 'Matches full fine-tuning within 0.3%', 'Inference latency increases by 22% due to dequantization overhead.', '4-bit NormalFloat base with unquantized LoRA adapters eliminates mathematical reasoning degradation.'),
        ('p5', 'P5', 'Gupta (2025)', 'Placeholder Paper 5: Token-Level Perplexity Shifts Across Quantization Schemes', ['A. Gupta', 'J. Becker'], 2025, 13, 'Wikitext-103 & C4', 'Comparative evaluation of GPTQ, AWQ, and SmoothQuant', 'AWQ achieves 0.4 lower perplexity than GPTQ on outlier tokens', 'Evaluated only on 7B parameter scale.', 'Activation-aware scaling significantly preserves high-magnitude outlier activations compared to round-to-nearest.'),
        ('p6', 'P6', 'Morales (2025)', 'Placeholder Paper 6: Hardware-Aware Kernel Optimizations for 4-Bit KV-Cache', ['R. Morales', 'C. Silva'], 2025, 16, 'Throughput & Memory Profiling on RTX 4090 / A100', 'FP8/INT4 compressed KV-cache GPU kernels', '2.8x batch size expansion at 16k context', 'Noticeable needle-in-a-haystack recall loss below 4 bits.', 'Compressing KV cache to 4 bits enables 2.8x higher inference concurrency with minimal retention loss.')
    ]
    
    papers = []
    claims = []
    for i, p in enumerate(papers_data):
        pid, short_lbl, cit_lbl, title, authors, yr, pages, dset, meth, metric, limit, conc = p
        paper = schemas.PaperMetadata(
            paper_id=pid,
            short_label=short_lbl,
            citation_label=cit_lbl,
            palette_index=i,
            source=schemas.PaperSource.DEMO,
            arxiv_id=None,
            filename=f'{pid}_quantization_placeholder.pdf',
            title=title,
            authors=authors,
            year=yr,
            page_count=pages,
            sections_found=[schemas.SectionName.ABSTRACT, schemas.SectionName.METHODOLOGY, schemas.SectionName.RESULTS, schemas.SectionName.CONCLUSION],
            dataset=dset,
            sample_size=f'n={800 + i*400}',
            methodology=meth,
            primary_metric=metric,
            limitations=limit,
            core_conclusion=conc,
            extraction_model='meta-llama/Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct'
        )
        papers.append(paper)
        
        claim_defs = [
            (schemas.ClaimType.EMPIRICAL_RESULT, 'Benchmark Accuracy', f'{short_lbl} reports: {metric}.', metric, f'{pid}-c1', False, schemas.SectionName.RESULTS, 5, f'Empirical results establish that {title} yielded {metric}.'),
            (schemas.ClaimType.METHOD, 'Quantization Strategy', f'{short_lbl} applies {meth}.', None, f'{pid}-c2', False, schemas.SectionName.METHODOLOGY, 3, f'Our implementation details {meth} across all target evaluation checkpoints.'),
            (schemas.ClaimType.CONCLUSION, 'Deployment Recommendation', f'{short_lbl} concludes: {conc}', None, f'{pid}-c3', False, schemas.SectionName.CONCLUSION, pages - 1, f'In conclusion, our results demonstrate: {conc}'),
            (schemas.ClaimType.LIMITATION, 'Scope Boundary', f'{short_lbl} notes: {limit}', None, f'{pid}-c4', False, schemas.SectionName.LIMITATIONS, pages - 2, f'The primary limitation remains: {limit}'),
            (schemas.ClaimType.EMPIRICAL_RESULT, 'Secondary Metric', f'{short_lbl} evaluates auxiliary performance on {dset}.', 'Valid', f'{pid}-c5', pid in ['p3', 'p6'], schemas.SectionName.RESULTS, 7, f'Auxiliary assessment on {dset} verifies primary trends.')
        ]
        
        for ctype, topic, stmt, mval, cid, is_gap, sec, pg, quote in claim_defs:
            claims.append(schemas.ExtractedClaim(
                claim_id=cid,
                paper_id=pid,
                claim_type=ctype,
                topic_hint=topic,
                statement=stmt,
                metric_value=mval,
                cluster_id='cl-1' if 'Accuracy' in topic or 'Deployment' in topic else 'cl-2',
                is_research_gap=is_gap,
                provenance=schemas.Provenance(
                    paper_id=pid,
                    paper_title=title,
                    section=sec,
                    page_number=pg,
                    verbatim_quote=quote
                )
            ))

    def make_edge(src_cid, tgt_cid, rel, conf, sim, expl, cl_id):
        s_claim = next(c for c in claims if c.claim_id == src_cid)
        t_claim = next(c for c in claims if c.claim_id == tgt_cid)
        eid = f"e-{src_cid.replace('-', '')}-{tgt_cid.replace('-', '')}"
        return schemas.ClaimComparisonEdge(
            edge_id=eid,
            source_claim_id=src_cid,
            target_claim_id=tgt_cid,
            source=s_claim.provenance,
            target=t_claim.provenance,
            relation=rel,
            confidence=conf,
            cosine_similarity=sim,
            explanation=expl,
            cluster_id=cl_id,
            referee_model='meta-llama/Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct'
        )

    edges = [
        make_edge('p1-c1', 'p2-c1', schemas.RelationType.CONTRADICT, 0.94, 0.82, 'Zheng (2024) asserts 4-bit quantization loses only 0.8 points and is safe, while Okonkwo (2024) demonstrates a catastrophic 7.9 point drop at 32k context.', 'cl-1'),
        make_edge('p1-c3', 'p2-c3', schemas.RelationType.CONTRADICT, 0.91, 0.79, 'Zheng concludes 4-bit models preserve general reasoning, while Okonkwo warns long-context reasoning is severely compromised.', 'cl-1'),
        make_edge('p1-c4', 'p2-c4', schemas.RelationType.METHODOLOGY_DELTA, 0.95, 0.78, 'The discrepancy between Zheng and Okonkwo is explained by context boundary conditions: Zheng capped testing at 2k tokens while Okonkwo tested at 32k tokens.', 'cl-1'),
        make_edge('p2-c2', 'p5-c2', schemas.RelationType.AGREE, 0.89, 0.75, 'Both Okonkwo (2024) and Gupta (2025) find that activation-aware outlier protection (AWQ) is critical to preventing representational collapse in low-bit regimes.', 'cl-2'),
        make_edge('p1-c1', 'p4-c1', schemas.RelationType.AGREE, 0.86, 0.73, 'Schneider (2025) agrees with Zheng (2024) that 4-bit formats with adapter tuning preserve benchmark reasoning within 0.3-0.8% of full precision.', 'cl-1'),
        make_edge('p1-c2', 'p3-c2', schemas.RelationType.METHODOLOGY_DELTA, 0.88, 0.72, 'Zheng used standard Wikitext calibration, whereas Al-Mansoor proves that varying calibration sets alters perplexity by 1.4 points across domains.', 'cl-2'),
        make_edge('p2-c1', 'p6-c1', schemas.RelationType.METHODOLOGY_DELTA, 0.85, 0.69, 'Okonkwo quantizes model weights, while Morales quantizes the dynamic KV cache, offering complementary throughput advantages with different loss profiles.', 'cl-2'),
        make_edge('p4-c3', 'p5-c3', schemas.RelationType.AGREE, 0.83, 0.67, 'Both Schneider and Gupta agree that isolated high-precision elements prevent reasoning degradation.', 'cl-1')
    ]
    
    gaps = [
        schemas.ResearchGap(
            signal='RESEARCH_GAP',
            gap_id='gap-p3-c5',
            claim_id='p3-c5',
            paper_id='p3',
            statement='Al-Mansoor (2024) notes all calibration corpora were English-only; multilingual quantization behavior remains unbenchmarked across this entire paper group.',
            provenance=next(c.provenance for c in claims if c.claim_id == 'p3-c5'),
            best_match_similarity=0.41,
            reason='No other paper in this set evaluates non-English multilingual calibration sensitivity in 4-bit models.'
        ),
        schemas.ResearchGap(
            signal='RESEARCH_GAP',
            gap_id='gap-p6-c5',
            claim_id='p6-c5',
            paper_id='p6',
            statement='Morales (2025) benchmarks GPU memory throughput and batch expansion, but lacks needle-in-a-haystack retrieval evaluation on multi-document reasoning tasks.',
            provenance=next(c.provenance for c in claims if c.claim_id == 'p6-c5'),
            best_match_similarity=0.36,
            reason='No other paper in this set measures hardware kernel memory latency and KV-cache compression trade-offs.'
        )
    ]
    
    clusters = [
        schemas.TopicCluster(
            cluster_id='cl-1',
            label='Quantization Accuracy Trade-offs & Benchmark Parity',
            claim_ids=['p1-c1', 'p1-c3', 'p2-c1', 'p2-c3', 'p4-c1', 'p4-c3'],
            paper_ids=['p1', 'p2', 'p4'],
            mean_similarity=0.77
        ),
        schemas.TopicCluster(
            cluster_id='cl-2',
            label='Calibration Domain Shifts & KV-Cache Compression',
            claim_ids=['p2-c2', 'p3-c2', 'p5-c2', 'p6-c1'],
            paper_ids=['p2', 'p3', 'p5', 'p6'],
            mean_similarity=0.72
        )
    ]
    
    cross_pairs = 375
    candidate_pairs = 24
    referee_evaluated = 8
    pair_reduction = round(100 * (1 - candidate_pairs / cross_pairs), 1)
    
    consensus = schemas.ConsensusReport(
        consensus_score=56,
        executive_summary='Analysis of 6 LLM quantization papers reveals widespread consensus that 4-bit weight quantization preserves standard short-context benchmarks (MMLU, GSM8k) within 0.8 points of FP16. However, a major empirical conflict exists regarding long-context deployment: 4-bit models lose up to 7.9 points at 32k context on complex reasoning tasks (Okonkwo 2024). Calibration set diversity and activation outlier protection (AWQ) are critical mediators of accuracy.',
        agreed_findings=[
            'Short-context instruction following is well preserved in 4-bit weight-only quantization (Zheng 2024, Schneider 2025).',
            'Activation outlier protection (AWQ) prevents numerical collapse in low-bit representations (Okonkwo 2024, Gupta 2025).'
        ],
        top_contradictions=[
            schemas.TopContradiction(
                edge_id='e-p1c1-p2c1',
                headline='Deployment safety dispute: Zheng (2024) reports 4-bit models match FP16 within 0.8 points, but Okonkwo (2024) shows a 7.9-point drop at 32k context.'
            )
        ],
        gap_ids=['gap-p3-c5', 'gap-p6-c5'],
        recommended_next_steps=[
            'Standardize long-context (>16k) evaluations for all low-bit model releases.',
            'Benchmark multilingual calibration sets to avoid regional language degradation.',
            'Combine 4-bit weight compression with 4-bit KV cache kernels for end-to-end evaluation.'
        ]
    )
    
    stats = schemas.AnalysisStats(
        paper_count=6,
        claim_count=30,
        cross_paper_pairs_total=cross_pairs,
        candidate_pairs=candidate_pairs,
        referee_pairs_evaluated=referee_evaluated,
        referee_calls=2,
        pair_reduction_pct=pair_reduction,
        stage_latency_ms=schemas.StageLatency(
            ingestion_ms=750,
            extraction_ms=4800,
            embedding_ms=62,
            clustering_ms=11,
            referee_ms=1950,
            consensus_ms=1150,
            total_ms=8723
        )
    )
    
    res = schemas.AnalysisResult(
        schema_version='1.0.0',
        analysis_id='demo-llm_quantization',
        mode=schemas.AnalysisMode.DEMO,
        preset_id='llm_quantization',
        fixture_provenance=schemas.FixtureProvenance.SYNTHETIC_PLACEHOLDER,
        generated_at='2026-10-26T21:00:00Z',
        papers=papers,
        claims=claims,
        clusters=clusters,
        edges=edges,
        gaps=gaps,
        consensus=consensus,
        stats=stats,
        models=MODELS,
        warnings=['This dataset is synthetic placeholder data for hackathon development. Honest open-source disclosure.']
    )
    return res

def make_microgrids():
    papers_data = [
        ('p1', 'P1', 'Adeyemi (2024)', 'Placeholder Paper 1: Solar Microgrid Frequency Stability with Lithium Storage', ['S. Adeyemi', 'K. Adeleke'], 2024, 13, 'Southwest Nigeria Rural Grid Pilot (n=450 households)', 'Centralized LiFePO4 battery bank with droop control', 'Frequency fluctuation kept within +/- 0.15 Hz', 'High capital expenditure limits rural community adoption.', 'Lithium storage banks with droop-controlled inverters maintain grid frequency under sudden solar dropoffs.'),
        ('p2', 'P2', 'Nwosu (2024)', 'Placeholder Paper 2: Flow Battery Cycling Under Tropical Solar Irradiance', ['E. Nwosu', 'J. Becker'], 2024, 15, 'Tropical Climate Testbed (38C ambient avg)', 'Vanadium redox flow battery with passive convective cooling', 'Capacity retention of 98.4% over 1,200 deep cycles', 'Low energy density requires 3x physical footprint vs Lithium.', 'Vanadium flow batteries exhibit superior thermal resilience in tropical climates without active air conditioning.'),
        ('p3', 'P3', 'Hassan (2024)', 'Placeholder Paper 3: Decentralized Inverter Droop Control in Rural Grids', ['M. Hassan', 'A. Bello'], 2024, 12, '12-Node Distributed Microgrid Simulation & Field Trial', 'Decentralized peer-to-peer reactive power sharing', 'Voltage sag reduced by 34% at terminal feeder nodes', 'Communication latency spikes destabilize synchronization.', 'Decentralized droop control eliminates single points of failure in remote agricultural microgrids.'),
        ('p4', 'P4', 'Kiplagat (2025)', 'Placeholder Paper 4: Peak Shaving Economics in Off-Grid Agricultural Clusters', ['D. Kiplagat', 'F. Ochieng'], 2025, 14, 'Sub-Saharan Grain Milling Co-op Data (n=12 mini-grids)', 'Dynamic time-of-use tariff scheduling with automated demand response', '31% Reduction in evening peak generator fuel consumption', 'Requires smart meters and reliable mobile GSM data coverage.', 'Automated demand response significantly lowers reliance on backup diesel generation during low solar hours.'),
        ('p5', 'P5', 'Mensah (2025)', 'Placeholder Paper 5: Predictive Dispatch Algorithms for High-Variability Loads', ['K. Mensah', 'B. Mensah'], 2025, 16, 'West African Monsoon Solar Farm Dataset', 'LSTM neural network forecasting paired with mixed-integer linear programming', '19% Improvement in battery state-of-charge reserve margins', 'Computational latency requires cloud connectivity or edge TPU.', 'Short-horizon cloud prediction enables preemptive battery charging before monsoon cloud cover arrival.'),
        ('p6', 'P6', 'Diop (2025)', 'Placeholder Paper 6: Thermal Degradation Mitigation in Distributed Battery Banks', ['M. Diop', 'L. Sow'], 2025, 11, 'Sahelian Environmental Chamber Stress Test (45C peak)', 'Phase-change material (PCM) passive thermal envelopes', 'Cell temperature rise constrained to 6.2C during peak discharge', 'PCM materials add 15% to battery pack initial weight.', 'Passive phase-change cooling extends battery lifespan in arid desert zones without parasitic electrical load.')
    ]
    
    papers = []
    claims = []
    for i, p in enumerate(papers_data):
        pid, short_lbl, cit_lbl, title, authors, yr, pages, dset, meth, metric, limit, conc = p
        paper = schemas.PaperMetadata(
            paper_id=pid,
            short_label=short_lbl,
            citation_label=cit_lbl,
            palette_index=i,
            source=schemas.PaperSource.DEMO,
            arxiv_id=None,
            filename=f'{pid}_microgrids_placeholder.pdf',
            title=title,
            authors=authors,
            year=yr,
            page_count=pages,
            sections_found=[schemas.SectionName.ABSTRACT, schemas.SectionName.METHODOLOGY, schemas.SectionName.RESULTS, schemas.SectionName.CONCLUSION],
            dataset=dset,
            sample_size=f'n={500 + i*300}',
            methodology=meth,
            primary_metric=metric,
            limitations=limit,
            core_conclusion=conc,
            extraction_model='meta-llama/Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct'
        )
        papers.append(paper)
        
        claim_defs = [
            (schemas.ClaimType.EMPIRICAL_RESULT, 'Grid Stability & Metrics', f'{short_lbl} achieves {metric}.', metric, f'{pid}-c1', False, schemas.SectionName.RESULTS, 5, f'Field measurements verify that {title} yielded {metric}.'),
            (schemas.ClaimType.METHOD, 'Control Architecture', f'{short_lbl} implements {meth}.', None, f'{pid}-c2', False, schemas.SectionName.METHODOLOGY, 3, f'Our design incorporates {meth} to ensure stable operating conditions.'),
            (schemas.ClaimType.CONCLUSION, 'Core Recommendation', f'{short_lbl} concludes: {conc}', None, f'{pid}-c3', False, schemas.SectionName.CONCLUSION, pages - 1, f'In conclusion, our research indicates: {conc}'),
            (schemas.ClaimType.LIMITATION, 'Operational Barrier', f'{short_lbl} identifies limitation: {limit}', None, f'{pid}-c4', False, schemas.SectionName.LIMITATIONS, pages - 2, f'A significant deployment hurdle remains: {limit}'),
            (schemas.ClaimType.EMPIRICAL_RESULT, 'Field Validation', f'{short_lbl} verifies operational metrics under {dset}.', 'Valid', f'{pid}-c5', pid in ['p2', 'p6'], schemas.SectionName.RESULTS, 6, f'Auxiliary field tests confirm performance under {dset}.')
        ]
        
        for ctype, topic, stmt, mval, cid, is_gap, sec, pg, quote in claim_defs:
            claims.append(schemas.ExtractedClaim(
                claim_id=cid,
                paper_id=pid,
                claim_type=ctype,
                topic_hint=topic,
                statement=stmt,
                metric_value=mval,
                cluster_id='cl-1' if 'Stability' in topic or 'Recommendation' in topic else 'cl-2',
                is_research_gap=is_gap,
                provenance=schemas.Provenance(
                    paper_id=pid,
                    paper_title=title,
                    section=sec,
                    page_number=pg,
                    verbatim_quote=quote
                )
            ))

    def make_edge(src_cid, tgt_cid, rel, conf, sim, expl, cl_id):
        s_claim = next(c for c in claims if c.claim_id == src_cid)
        t_claim = next(c for c in claims if c.claim_id == tgt_cid)
        eid = f"e-{src_cid.replace('-', '')}-{tgt_cid.replace('-', '')}"
        return schemas.ClaimComparisonEdge(
            edge_id=eid,
            source_claim_id=src_cid,
            target_claim_id=tgt_cid,
            source=s_claim.provenance,
            target=t_claim.provenance,
            relation=rel,
            confidence=conf,
            cosine_similarity=sim,
            explanation=expl,
            cluster_id=cl_id,
            referee_model='meta-llama/Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct'
        )

    edges = [
        make_edge('p1-c3', 'p2-c3', schemas.RelationType.CONTRADICT, 0.88, 0.79, 'Adeyemi (2024) advocates centralized lithium storage for frequency stability, whereas Nwosu (2024) argues flow batteries are far superior in tropical heat due to lithium thermal degradation.', 'cl-1'),
        make_edge('p1-c2', 'p3-c2', schemas.RelationType.CONTRADICT, 0.91, 0.76, 'Adeyemi relies on a centralized master storage bank, whereas Hassan proves decentralized peer-to-peer droop control is superior for avoiding single points of failure.', 'cl-2'),
        make_edge('p1-c1', 'p3-c1', schemas.RelationType.AGREE, 0.89, 0.74, 'Both Adeyemi (2024) and Hassan (2024) confirm that droop-controlled inverter logic maintains terminal feeder voltage and frequency within allowable bounds.', 'cl-1'),
        make_edge('p4-c3', 'p5-c3', schemas.RelationType.AGREE, 0.87, 0.72, 'Both Kiplagat (2025) and Mensah (2025) find that predictive scheduling algorithms reduce backup diesel fuel consumption by 19-31%.', 'cl-1'),
        make_edge('p2-c1', 'p6-c1', schemas.RelationType.METHODOLOGY_DELTA, 0.86, 0.73, 'Nwosu solves tropical battery degradation by switching chemistry to vanadium flow, whereas Diop retains lithium chemistry but wraps packs in phase-change material.', 'cl-2'),
        make_edge('p3-c1', 'p4-c1', schemas.RelationType.METHODOLOGY_DELTA, 0.84, 0.70, 'Hassan achieves grid stability through electrical droop control (voltage sag -34%), whereas Kiplagat achieves stability through behavioral economics and time-of-use tariffs.', 'cl-1'),
        make_edge('p1-c4', 'p6-c4', schemas.RelationType.METHODOLOGY_DELTA, 0.85, 0.68, 'Adeyemi highlights the high cost of active air conditioning for lithium banks, whereas Diop demonstrates zero-power passive phase-change envelopes.', 'cl-2'),
        make_edge('p4-c4', 'p5-c4', schemas.RelationType.AGREE, 0.83, 0.67, 'Both Kiplagat and Mensah acknowledge that advanced scheduling algorithms depend heavily on reliable telecommunications and local edge compute.', 'cl-2')
    ]
    
    gaps = [
        schemas.ResearchGap(
            signal='RESEARCH_GAP',
            gap_id='gap-p2-c5',
            claim_id='p2-c5',
            paper_id='p2',
            statement='Nwosu (2024) evaluates vanadium electrolyte supply chain costs and recycling logistics, a chemical lifecycle dimension unaddressed by other papers.',
            provenance=next(c.provenance for c in claims if c.claim_id == 'p2-c5'),
            best_match_similarity=0.40,
            reason='No other paper in this set evaluates vanadium chemical electrolyte sourcing and end-of-life recycling.'
        ),
        schemas.ResearchGap(
            signal='RESEARCH_GAP',
            gap_id='gap-p6-c5',
            claim_id='p6-c5',
            paper_id='p6',
            statement='Diop (2025) benchmarks physical phase-change material weight penalty on mobile transportable power units in Sahelian conditions.',
            provenance=next(c.provenance for c in claims if c.claim_id == 'p6-c5'),
            best_match_similarity=0.37,
            reason='No other paper in this set evaluates physical thermal material weights and containerized transport logistics.'
        )
    ]
    
    clusters = [
        schemas.TopicCluster(
            cluster_id='cl-1',
            label='Microgrid Frequency Stability & Economic Dispatch',
            claim_ids=['p1-c1', 'p1-c3', 'p3-c1', 'p4-c1', 'p4-c3', 'p5-c3'],
            paper_ids=['p1', 'p3', 'p4', 'p5'],
            mean_similarity=0.75
        ),
        schemas.TopicCluster(
            cluster_id='cl-2',
            label='Tropical Thermal Resilience & Chemistry Alternatives',
            claim_ids=['p2-c1', 'p2-c3', 'p6-c1', 'p6-c3'],
            paper_ids=['p2', 'p6'],
            mean_similarity=0.71
        )
    ]
    
    cross_pairs = 375
    candidate_pairs = 24
    referee_evaluated = 8
    pair_reduction = round(100 * (1 - candidate_pairs / cross_pairs), 1)
    
    consensus = schemas.ConsensusReport(
        consensus_score=56,
        executive_summary='Synthesis of 6 solar microgrid research papers demonstrates strong agreement that predictive demand scheduling and droop inverter control prevent brownouts during sudden solar irradiance dropoffs. However, a sharp technological conflict divides battery storage strategies: centralized lithium banks offer superior frequency stabilization (Adeyemi 2024), but suffer rapid thermal degradation in tropical ambient heat (>38C), where vanadium flow batteries and passive phase-change cooling envelopes provide dramatically higher cycling longevity (Nwosu 2024, Diop 2025).',
        agreed_findings=[
            'Inverter droop control stabilizes feeder voltage and frequency across remote off-grid nodes (Adeyemi 2024, Hassan 2024).',
            'Predictive load dispatch reduces reliance on backup fossil generators by up to 31% (Kiplagat 2025, Mensah 2025).'
        ],
        top_contradictions=[
            schemas.TopContradiction(
                edge_id='e-p1c3-p2c3',
                headline='Storage chemistry controversy: Adeyemi (2024) favors lithium for frequency control, while Nwosu (2024) shows flow batteries prevent tropical thermal decay.'
            )
        ],
        gap_ids=['gap-p2-c5', 'gap-p6-c5'],
        recommended_next_steps=[
            'Field-test hybrid lithium-flow storage architectures to capture both fast frequency response and thermal longevity.',
            'Integrate passive phase-change thermal barriers directly into rural battery casing standards.',
            'Develop offline edge dispatch heuristics that do not require cellular GSM connectivity.'
        ]
    )
    
    stats = schemas.AnalysisStats(
        paper_count=6,
        claim_count=30,
        cross_paper_pairs_total=cross_pairs,
        candidate_pairs=candidate_pairs,
        referee_pairs_evaluated=referee_evaluated,
        referee_calls=2,
        pair_reduction_pct=pair_reduction,
        stage_latency_ms=schemas.StageLatency(
            ingestion_ms=710,
            extraction_ms=4650,
            embedding_ms=59,
            clustering_ms=10,
            referee_ms=1900,
            consensus_ms=1180,
            total_ms=8509
        )
    )
    
    res = schemas.AnalysisResult(
        schema_version='1.0.0',
        analysis_id='demo-microgrids',
        mode=schemas.AnalysisMode.DEMO,
        preset_id='microgrids',
        fixture_provenance=schemas.FixtureProvenance.SYNTHETIC_PLACEHOLDER,
        generated_at='2026-10-26T21:00:00Z',
        papers=papers,
        claims=claims,
        clusters=clusters,
        edges=edges,
        gaps=gaps,
        consensus=consensus,
        stats=stats,
        models=MODELS,
        warnings=['This dataset is synthetic placeholder data for hackathon development. Honest open-source disclosure.']
    )
    return res

================================================================================
FILE: backend/scripts/smoke_extract.py (75 lines)

"""
Smoke extraction script: extracts and verifies scientific claims from a real PDF.
Usage:
    python -m scripts.smoke_extract <path_to_pdf>
"""

import asyncio
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from app.config import get_settings
from app.services.extraction import extract_claims_from_paper
from app.services.ingestion.pdf_parser import parse_pdf
from app.services.ingestion.sectioner import section_paper
from app.services.llm.client import LLMClient
from app.schemas import PaperSource


async def main():
    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
        print("Usage: python -m scripts.smoke_extract <path_to_pdf>")
        sys.exit(1)

    pdf_path = Path(sys.argv[1])
    if not pdf_path.exists():
        print(f"Error: File '{pdf_path}' does not exist.")
        sys.exit(1)

    data = pdf_path.read_bytes()
    settings = get_settings()

    print(f"1. Parsing PDF '{pdf_path.name}' ({len(data)} bytes)...")
    parsed = parse_pdf(data, pdf_path.name)
    print(f"   Pages: {parsed.page_count}, Characters: {parsed.char_count}")

    print("2. Sectioning and budgeting paper...")
    sec_paper = section_paper(
        parsed=parsed,
        paper_id="p1",
        title=parsed.meta_title or pdf_path.stem.replace("_", " ").title(),
        source=PaperSource.UPLOAD,
        filename=pdf_path.name,
        settings=settings,
    )
    print(f"   Sections found: {[s.value for s in sec_paper.sections_found]}")
    print(f"   Characters budgeted: {len(sec_paper.text_for_llm)} (max {settings.max_paper_chars})")

    print("3. Executing Stage 1 extraction with open-weight LLM chain...")
    client = LLMClient(settings)
    meta, claims, warnings = await extract_claims_from_paper(sec_paper, client, settings)

    print("\n=== EXTRACTED PAPER METADATA ===")
    print(f"Title: {meta.title}")
    print(f"Citation label: {meta.citation_label}")
    print(f"Authors: {', '.join(meta.authors) if meta.authors else 'None'}")
    print(f"Year: {meta.year}")
    print(f"Dataset: {meta.dataset}")
    print(f"Methodology: {meta.methodology}")
    print(f"Primary Metric: {meta.primary_metric}")
    print(f"Core Conclusion: {meta.core_conclusion}")

    print(f"\n=== VERIFIED CLAIMS ({len(claims)}) ===")
    for idx, c in enumerate(claims, start=1):
        print(f"\n[{idx}] Claim ID: {c.claim_id} | Type: {c.claim_type.value} | Topic: {c.topic_hint}")
        print(f"    Statement: {c.statement}")
        print(f"    Provenance: Page {c.provenance.page_number} ({c.provenance.section.value})")
        print(f"    Verbatim Quote: \"{c.provenance.verbatim_quote}\"")

    if warnings:
        print(f"\n=== WARNINGS ({len(warnings)}) ===")
        for w in warnings:
            print(f"  - {w}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())

import sys

================================================================================
FILE: backend/tests/test_contract.py (163 lines)

import json, glob, re
from pathlib import Path
import pytest
from app import schemas

FIXTURES_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent / "fixtures"

APPROVED_OPEN_WEIGHT_MODELS = {
    "llama-3.1-8b-instant",
    "meta-llama/Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct",
    "Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct",
    "llama3.2:3b",
    "BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5",
}


def get_fixture_files():
    return [p for p in FIXTURES_DIR.glob("*.json") if p.name != "presets_manifest.json"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("fixture_path", get_fixture_files(), ids=lambda p: p.name)
def test_fixture_schema_validation(fixture_path):
    raw = json.loads(fixture_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    result = schemas.AnalysisResult.model_validate(raw)
    assert result.schema_version == schemas.SCHEMA_VERSION
    assert result.mode == schemas.AnalysisMode.DEMO

    # 1. Check claim paper_ids resolve
    paper_ids = {p.paper_id for p in result.papers}
    for claim in result.claims:
        assert claim.paper_id in paper_ids
        assert claim.provenance.paper_id == claim.paper_id

    # 2. Check cluster references
    claims_by_id = {c.claim_id: c for c in result.claims}
    for cluster in result.clusters:
        for cid in cluster.claim_ids:
            assert cid in claims_by_id
        for pid in cluster.paper_ids:
            assert pid in paper_ids

    # 3. Check edge references and quote parity
    edges_by_id = {}
    rel_counts = {"AGREE": 0, "CONTRADICT": 0, "METHODOLOGY_DELTA": 0}
    for edge in result.edges:
        edges_by_id[edge.edge_id] = edge
        assert edge.source_claim_id in claims_by_id
        assert edge.target_claim_id in claims_by_id

        # Edge quotes equal claim quotes
        s_claim = claims_by_id[edge.source_claim_id]
        t_claim = claims_by_id[edge.target_claim_id]
        assert edge.source.verbatim_quote == s_claim.provenance.verbatim_quote
        assert edge.target.verbatim_quote == t_claim.provenance.verbatim_quote

        # Check edge_id naming rule: "e-{source_clean}-{target_clean}"
        clean_src = edge.source_claim_id.replace("-", "")
        clean_tgt = edge.target_claim_id.replace("-", "")
        expected_eid = f"e-{clean_src}-{clean_tgt}"
        assert edge.edge_id == expected_eid

        rel_counts[edge.relation.value] += 1

    # 4. Check gap references
    gaps_by_id = {}
    for gap in result.gaps:
        gaps_by_id[gap.gap_id] = gap
        assert gap.claim_id in claims_by_id
        assert gap.paper_id in paper_ids
        assert gap.signal == "RESEARCH_GAP"

    # 5. Check consensus references
    for top_c in result.consensus.top_contradictions:
        assert top_c.edge_id in edges_by_id
    for gid in result.consensus.gap_ids:
        assert gid in gaps_by_id

    # 6. Check consensus score formula: round(100 * (A + 0.5 * D) / (A + C + D))
    a = rel_counts["AGREE"]
    c = rel_counts["CONTRADICT"]
    d = rel_counts["METHODOLOGY_DELTA"]
    total_edges = a + c + d
    if total_edges > 0:
        expected_score = round(100 * (a + 0.5 * d) / total_edges)
        assert result.consensus.consensus_score == expected_score
    else:
        assert result.consensus.consensus_score == 0

    # 7. Check stats arithmetic
    stats = result.stats
    assert stats.paper_count == len(result.papers)
    assert stats.claim_count == len(result.claims)
    if stats.cross_paper_pairs_total > 0:
        expected_reduction = round(100 * (1 - stats.candidate_pairs / stats.cross_paper_pairs_total), 1)
        assert abs(stats.pair_reduction_pct - expected_reduction) < 0.01

    # 8. Check latency breakdown sums to total_ms
    lat = stats.stage_latency_ms
    expected_total = (lat.ingestion_ms + lat.extraction_ms + lat.embedding_ms +
                      lat.clustering_ms + lat.referee_ms + lat.consensus_ms)
    assert lat.total_ms == expected_total


@pytest.mark.parametrize("fixture_path", get_fixture_files(), ids=lambda p: p.name)
def test_deep_referential_and_provenance_invariants(fixture_path):
    """Enforces Argon 4 invariants: full provenance object equality, lower-numbered paper ordering, gap invariants."""
    raw = json.loads(fixture_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    result = schemas.AnalysisResult.model_validate(raw)
    claims_by_id = {c.claim_id: c for c in result.claims}

    for edge in result.edges:
        s_claim = claims_by_id[edge.source_claim_id]
        t_claim = claims_by_id[edge.target_claim_id]

        # Full provenance object identity
        assert edge.source.model_dump() == s_claim.provenance.model_dump()
        assert edge.target.model_dump() == t_claim.provenance.model_dump()

        # Strict ordering: source must be from lower-numbered paper than target
        s_num = int(re.match(r"^p([0-9]+)", s_claim.paper_id).group(1))
        t_num = int(re.match(r"^p([0-9]+)", t_claim.paper_id).group(1))
        assert s_num < t_num, f"Edge {edge.edge_id} has invalid paper ordering p{s_num} >= p{t_num}"

    for gap in result.gaps:
        claim = claims_by_id[gap.claim_id]
        assert claim.is_research_gap is True, f"Claim {gap.claim_id} pointed by gap must have is_research_gap=True"
        assert gap.gap_id == f"gap-{gap.claim_id}"
        assert gap.provenance.model_dump() == claim.provenance.model_dump()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("fixture_path", get_fixture_files(), ids=lambda p: p.name)
def test_open_weight_models_and_synthetic_provenance(fixture_path):
    """Enforces Rule 1 (Open-weight models only) and Rule 4 (Synthetic fixture labeling)."""
    raw = json.loads(fixture_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    result = schemas.AnalysisResult.model_validate(raw)

    assert result.fixture_provenance == schemas.FixtureProvenance.SYNTHETIC_PLACEHOLDER

    assert len(result.models) >= 2, "Every preset must disclose at least LLM and embedding models"
    for m in result.models:
        assert m.model_id in APPROVED_OPEN_WEIGHT_MODELS, f"Forbidden non-open model found: {m.model_id}"
        assert bool(m.license.strip()), f"Model {m.model_id} must have non-empty open-weight license"
        assert m.parameter_count is not None

def test_presets_manifest_valid():
    """Validate fixtures/presets_manifest.json structure and alignment with demo.py."""
    manifest_path = FIXTURES_DIR / "presets_manifest.json"
    assert manifest_path.exists(), "presets_manifest.json must exist in fixtures/"
    data = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert "presets" in data and len(data["presets"]) >= 3
    from app.api.demo import PRESETS

    for item in data["presets"]:
        pid = item.get("preset_id")
        assert pid in PRESETS, f"Unknown preset_id in manifest: {pid}"
        assert item.get("title"), f"Missing title in preset {pid}"
        papers = item.get("papers", [])
        assert len(papers) >= 2, f"Preset {pid} must have >= 2 papers"
        for p in papers:
            assert re.match(r"^\d{4}\.\d{4,5}$", p.get("arxiv_id", "")), f"Invalid arXiv ID: {p.get('arxiv_id')}"
            assert "license" in p and "CC" in p["license"]
            assert p.get("url", "").startswith("https://arxiv.org/abs/")

def test_error_envelope_for_validation_error():
    """Enforces Argon 4 F-03: 422 request validation errors return ErrorResponse with details."""
    from fastapi import APIRouter
    from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

    class SampleInput(BaseModel):
        num: int = Field(ge=10)

    test_router = APIRouter()

    @test_router.post("/test-val")
    def sample_endpoint(payload: SampleInput):
        return {"status": "ok"}

    app.include_router(test_router, prefix="/api/v1/test-validation")

    res = client.post("/api/v1/test-validation/test-val", json={"num": 3})
    assert res.status_code == 422
    data = res.json()
    assert "error" in data
    assert data["error"]["code"] == ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR.value
    assert data["error"]["message"] == "Request validation error"
    assert "errors" in data["error"]["details"]

import asyncio
import pytest
import httpx
import pymupdf
from app.config import get_settings
from app.errors import KairosError
from app.schemas import ErrorCode
from app.services.ingestion.arxiv_fetcher import normalize_arxiv_id, fetch_arxiv_pdf, fetch_all_arxiv


def _create_mock_pdf_bytes() -> bytes:
    doc = pymupdf.open()
    page = doc.new_page(width=595, height=842)
    page.insert_text((50, 50), "arXiv Mock Paper Content\n" * 20, fontsize=11)
    content = doc.tobytes()
    doc.close()
    return content


def test_normalize_arxiv_id_all_formats():
    """Verifies that all 5 arXiv URL and string formats normalize to canonical IDs."""
    assert normalize_arxiv_id("2309.12345") == "2309.12345"
    assert normalize_arxiv_id("2309.12345v3") == "2309.12345"
    assert normalize_arxiv_id("arxiv:2309.12345") == "2309.12345"
    assert normalize_arxiv_id("ARXIV: 2309.12345v1") == "2309.12345"
    assert normalize_arxiv_id("https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.12345") == "2309.12345"
    assert normalize_arxiv_id("https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.12345v2") == "2309.12345"
    assert normalize_arxiv_id("https://arxiv.org/pdf/2309.12345.pdf") == "2309.12345"
    assert normalize_arxiv_id("http://arxiv.org/pdf/2309.12345") == "2309.12345"
    assert normalize_arxiv_id("hep-th/9901001") == "hep-th/9901001"
    assert normalize_arxiv_id("hep-th/9901001v2") == "hep-th/9901001"


def test_normalize_arxiv_id_invalid_formats():
    """Missing Test 1: Rejects malformed arXiv identifiers with INVALID_ARXIV_ID."""
    invalid_ids = ["not_an_id", "2309", "abc.123", "http://otherdomain.com/paper.pdf", ""]
    for bad in invalid_ids:
        with pytest.raises(KairosError) as exc_info:
            normalize_arxiv_id(bad)
        assert exc_info.value.code == ErrorCode.INVALID_ARXIV_ID


def test_fetch_arxiv_pdf_success():
    """Verifies successful arXiv fetch and %PDF- validation using mock transport."""
    async def _run():
        pdf_bytes = _create_mock_pdf_bytes()

        def handler(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
            assert "2309.12345.pdf" in str(request.url)
            return httpx.Response(200, content=pdf_bytes)

        transport = httpx.MockTransport(handler)
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(transport=transport) as client:
            sem = asyncio.Semaphore(2)
            fn, content = await fetch_arxiv_pdf("2309.12345", client, sem)
            assert fn == "arxiv_2309.12345.pdf"
            assert content.startswith(b"%PDF-")

    asyncio.run(_run())


def test_fetch_arxiv_pdf_file_too_large():
    """Verifies that an arXiv download exceeding max_file_mb raises FILE_TOO_LARGE."""
    async def _run():
        huge_content = b"%PDF-1.4\n" + b"0" * (2 * 1024 * 1024)  # 2MB

        def handler(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
            return httpx.Response(200, content=huge_content)

        transport = httpx.MockTransport(handler)
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(transport=transport) as client:
            sem = asyncio.Semaphore(2)
            with pytest.raises(KairosError) as exc_info:
                # Limit to 1MB
                await fetch_arxiv_pdf("2309.12345", client, sem, max_file_mb=1)
            assert exc_info.value.code == ErrorCode.FILE_TOO_LARGE

    asyncio.run(_run())


def test_fetch_arxiv_pdf_http_error():
    """Verifies that HTTP 404 from arXiv raises KairosError(ARXIV_FETCH_FAILED)."""
    async def _run():
        def handler(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
            return httpx.Response(404, content=b"Not found on arXiv")

        transport = httpx.MockTransport(handler)
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(transport=transport) as client:
            sem = asyncio.Semaphore(2)
            with pytest.raises(KairosError) as exc_info:
                await fetch_arxiv_pdf("2309.99999", client, sem)
            assert exc_info.value.code == ErrorCode.ARXIV_FETCH_FAILED

    asyncio.run(_run())


def test_fetch_all_arxiv_partial_failure_isolation():
    """Verifies that an individual failure is isolated to warnings while valid papers succeed."""
    async def _run():
        pdf_bytes = _create_mock_pdf_bytes()
        settings = get_settings()

        def handler(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
            if "2309.11111" in str(request.url):
                return httpx.Response(200, content=pdf_bytes)
            return httpx.Response(404, content=b"Not found")

        transport = httpx.MockTransport(handler)
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(transport=transport) as client:
            papers, warnings = await fetch_all_arxiv(["2309.11111", "2309.99999"], settings, client=client)
            assert len(papers) == 1
            assert papers[0][0] == "2309.11111"
            assert len(warnings) == 1
            assert "2309.99999" in warnings[0]

    asyncio.run(_run())

import asyncio
import time
import pytest
import pymupdf
import httpx
from app.config import Settings, get_settings
from app.errors import KairosError
from app.schemas import ErrorCode, SectionName
from app.services.ingestion.models import ParsedPdf
from app.services.ingestion.pdf_parser import parse_pdf
from app.services.ingestion.sectioner import section_paper
from app.services.ingestion.validators import validate_inputs
from app.services.ingestion.coordinator import ingest_all


def _create_test_pdf_bytes(sections: dict[str, str], title: str = "Test Study") -> bytes:
    """Helper creating an in-memory PDF document with custom text sections and metadata."""
    doc = pymupdf.open()
    doc.set_metadata({"title": title, "author": "Kairos Test Team"})

    # Page 1
    page1 = doc.new_page(width=595, height=842)
    y = 50
    page1.insert_text((50, y), f"Title: {title}", fontsize=16)
    y += 30

    for heading, text in sections.items():
        if y > 720:
            page = doc.new_page(width=595, height=842)
            y = 50
        else:
            page = doc[-1]

        page.insert_text((50, y), heading, fontsize=12)
        y += 20
        # Insert body text in lines
        lines = text.split("\n")
        for line in lines:
            line_str = line.strip()
            if not line_str:
                continue
            if y > 760:
                page = doc.new_page(width=595, height=842)
                y = 50
            page.insert_text((50, y), line_str, fontsize=10)
            y += 15
        y += 10

    pdf_bytes = doc.tobytes()
    doc.close()
    return pdf_bytes


def test_valid_pdf_parsing_and_page_indexing():
    """Verifies that PyMuPDF parses pages, extracts 1-indexed page_texts, and detects titles."""
    sections = {
        "1. Abstract": "This study analyzes clinical diagnostic metrics across diverse multi-site hospital datasets.\n" * 10,
        "2. Methods": "We trained deep neural networks using 5-fold cross validation on balanced cohorts.\n" * 15,
        "3. Results": "The model achieved 94.2% sensitivity and 89.1% specificity across all validation sets.\n" * 15,
        "4. Discussion": "Our findings demonstrate substantial gains in sensitivity while maintaining low false-positive rates.\n" * 10,
        "References": "1. Smith et al. Nature Medicine 2024.\n2. Doe et al. Lancet Digital Health 2023.\n",
    }
    data = _create_test_pdf_bytes(sections, title="Deep Diagnostic Evaluation")

    parsed = parse_pdf(data, "test_paper.pdf")
    assert parsed.page_count >= 2
    assert 1 in parsed.page_texts
    assert 2 in parsed.page_texts
    assert parsed.meta_title == "Deep Diagnostic Evaluation"
    assert parsed.char_count > 1000


def test_sectioning_and_stop_at_references():
    """Verifies section recognition, stop-at-references truncation, and model marker insertion."""
    sections = {
        "Abstract": "This paper presents a formal evaluation of medical imaging models under distribution shifts.\n" * 5,
        "Methodology": "High-resolution radiography scans were preprocessed using standard normalization pipelines.\n" * 8,
        "Results": "Across external validation cohorts, receiver operating characteristic curves demonstrated robust AUC.\n" * 8,
        "Conclusion": "In conclusion, distribution-robust architectures are critical for real-world clinical safety.\n" * 5,
        "References": "Discarded reference line 1.\nDiscarded bibliography item 2.\n",
    }
    data = _create_test_pdf_bytes(sections, title="Clinical Evaluation")
    parsed = parse_pdf(data, "clinical.pdf")

    sec_paper = section_paper(
        parsed=parsed,
        paper_id="p1",
        title=parsed.meta_title or "Clinical Evaluation",
        source="UPLOAD",
        filename="clinical.pdf",
    )

    # Check sections detected
    assert SectionName.ABSTRACT in sec_paper.sections_found
    assert SectionName.METHODOLOGY in sec_paper.sections_found
    assert SectionName.RESULTS in sec_paper.sections_found
    assert SectionName.CONCLUSION in sec_paper.sections_found

    # Check markers
    assert "[[SECTION: ABSTRACT]]" in sec_paper.text_for_llm
    assert "[[SECTION: RESULTS]]" in sec_paper.text_for_llm
    assert "[[PAGE 1]]" in sec_paper.text_for_llm

    # Check references are excluded
    assert "Discarded reference line 1" not in sec_paper.text_for_llm
    assert "Discarded bibliography item 2" not in sec_paper.text_for_llm


def test_character_budget_enforcement():
    """Verifies that large documents are budgeted to <= MAX_PAPER_CHARS (24,000 chars) with marker headroom."""
    settings = get_settings()
    huge_text = "Standard evaluation metric analysis for clinical safety and model interpretability. " * 500
    sections = {
        "Abstract": huge_text[:4000],
        "Methodology": huge_text[:9000],
        "Results": huge_text[:12000],
        "Discussion": huge_text[:8000],
        "References": "Ref 1. Ref 2.",
    }
    data = _create_test_pdf_bytes(sections, title="Huge Paper")
    parsed = parse_pdf(data, "huge.pdf")

    sec_paper = section_paper(
        parsed=parsed,
        paper_id="p1",
        title="Huge Paper",
        source="UPLOAD",
        settings=settings,
    )

    assert len(sec_paper.text_for_llm) <= settings.max_paper_chars
    # Asserts that markers are not cut off mid-marker
    assert not sec_paper.text_for_llm.endswith("[[PAGE")
    assert not sec_paper.text_for_llm.endswith("[[SECTION:")


def test_no_headings_fallback():
    """Verifies that unsectioned PDFs fall back to head (ABSTRACT) and tail (OTHER)."""
    text_blob = "Continuous unsectioned scientific narrative describing experimental protocols and results. " * 30
    doc = pymupdf.open()
    p = doc.new_page(width=595, height=842)
    p.insert_textbox(pymupdf.Rect(50, 50, 500, 750), text_blob, fontsize=10)
    data = doc.tobytes()
    doc.close()

    parsed = parse_pdf(data, "unsectioned.pdf")
    sec_paper = section_paper(
        parsed=parsed,
        paper_id="p1",
        title="Unsectioned Paper",
        source="UPLOAD",
    )

    assert SectionName.ABSTRACT in sec_paper.sections_found
    assert SectionName.OTHER in sec_paper.sections_found
    assert len(sec_paper.text_for_llm) <= 24000


def test_unsectioned_head_tail_preservation():
    """Missing Test 3: Verifies that 15,000-char unsectioned paper across multiple pages preserves head and tail."""
    page_texts: dict[int, str] = {}
    for i in range(1, 6):
        page_texts[i] = f"Page {i} content describing distinct experimental observation phase {i}. " * 40

    parsed = ParsedPdf(
        page_texts=page_texts,
        page_count=5,
        meta_title="Multi-page Unsectioned Study",
        char_count=sum(len(t) for t in page_texts.values())
    )

    sec_paper = section_paper(
        parsed=parsed,
        paper_id="p1",
        title="Multi-page Unsectioned Study",
        source="UPLOAD",
    )

    assert SectionName.ABSTRACT in sec_paper.sections_found
    assert SectionName.OTHER in sec_paper.sections_found
    assert "[[SECTION: ABSTRACT]]" in sec_paper.text_for_llm
    assert "[[SECTION: OTHER]]" in sec_paper.text_for_llm
    # Verifies page 1 (head) and page 5 (tail) are both retained
    assert "[[PAGE 1]]" in sec_paper.text_for_llm
    assert "[[PAGE 5]]" in sec_paper.text_for_llm
    assert "observation phase 5" in sec_paper.text_for_llm
    assert len(sec_paper.text_for_llm) <= 24000


def test_invalid_file_type_magic_bytes():

def test_paper_count_and_size_limits():
    """Missing Test 1: Enforces min_papers (2), max_papers (10), and size limits (FILE_TOO_LARGE)."""
    valid_pdf = _create_test_pdf_bytes({"Abstract": "A\n" * 300}, "Paper")

    # Custom settings with 1MB file cap and 2MB total cap
    tight_settings = Settings(max_file_mb=1, max_total_mb=2, min_papers=2, max_papers=10)

    # 1. Per-file limit: 2MB file exceeds 1MB limit -> FILE_TOO_LARGE
    oversized_file = [(
        "huge.pdf",
        b"%PDF-1.5\n" + b"x" * (2 * 1024 * 1024)
    )]
    with pytest.raises(KairosError) as exc_info:
        validate_inputs(oversized_file, [], tight_settings)
    assert exc_info.value.code == ErrorCode.FILE_TOO_LARGE

    # 2. Total file limit: two 1.2MB files exceed 2MB total limit -> FILE_TOO_LARGE
    medium_file1 = ("m1.pdf", b"%PDF-1.5\n" + b"1" * int(1.2 * 1024 * 1024))
    medium_file2 = ("m2.pdf", b"%PDF-1.5\n" + b"2" * int(1.2 * 1024 * 1024))
    with pytest.raises(KairosError) as exc_info:
        validate_inputs([medium_file1, medium_file2], [], tight_settings)
    assert exc_info.value.code == ErrorCode.FILE_TOO_LARGE

    # 3. Less than 2 papers
    default_settings = get_settings()
    with pytest.raises(KairosError) as exc_info:
        validate_inputs([("p1.pdf", valid_pdf)], [], default_settings)
    assert exc_info.value.code == ErrorCode.TOO_FEW_PAPERS

    # 4. More than 10 papers
    lots_of_files = [(f"p{i}.pdf", valid_pdf + bytes([i])) for i in range(11)]
    with pytest.raises(KairosError) as exc_info:
        validate_inputs(lots_of_files, [], default_settings)
    assert exc_info.value.code == ErrorCode.TOO_MANY_PAPERS


def test_duplicate_file_deduplication():
    """De-duplicates identical uploaded files by SHA-256 hash."""
    settings = get_settings()
    pdf_bytes = _create_test_pdf_bytes({"Abstract": "Content line for duplicate test.\n" * 20}, "Paper")

    # Upload identical bytes with different names
    files = [("copy1.pdf", pdf_bytes), ("copy2.pdf", pdf_bytes), ("p3.pdf", pdf_bytes + b"\n%extra")]
    deduped_files, _ = validate_inputs(files, [], settings)
    assert len(deduped_files) == 2


def test_cross_format_and_cross_source_deduplication():
    """Missing Test 2: Verifies cross-format arXiv deduplication in validators and cross-source SHA-256 deduplication in coordinator."""
    settings = get_settings()
    valid_pdf = _create_test_pdf_bytes({"Abstract": "Paper content line describing experimental methods.\n" * 20}, "Paper")
    p2_pdf = _create_test_pdf_bytes({"Abstract": "Different paper content line describing validation methods.\n" * 20}, "Distinct Paper")

    # 1. Cross-format arXiv deduplication inside validate_inputs
    arxiv_variations = [
        "2309.12345",
        "arxiv:2309.12345v2",
        "https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.12345v1",
        "https://arxiv.org/pdf/2309.12345.pdf",
    ]
    # Provide 1 valid upload + 4 arXiv variations of the SAME paper
    deduped_files, deduped_arxiv = validate_inputs([("p1.pdf", valid_pdf)], arxiv_variations, settings)
    assert len(deduped_arxiv) == 1
    assert deduped_arxiv[0] == "2309.12345"

    # 2. Cross-source SHA-256 deduplication inside coordinator.ingest_all
    async def _run_coordinator_dedup():
        files = [("upload1.pdf", valid_pdf), ("upload2.pdf", p2_pdf)]
        arxiv_ids = ["2309.12345"]  # returns valid_pdf (duplicate of upload1.pdf)

        import app.services.ingestion.coordinator as coord_mod
        orig_fetch = coord_mod.fetch_all_arxiv

        async def mock_fetch(a_ids, s):
            return [("2309.12345", "arxiv_2309.12345.pdf", valid_pdf)], []

        coord_mod.fetch_all_arxiv = mock_fetch
        try:
            batch = await ingest_all(files, arxiv_ids, settings)
            # upload1 and upload2 survive, arXiv duplicate is skipped
            assert len(batch.papers) == 2
            assert any("duplicate" in w.lower() for w in batch.warnings)
        finally:
            coord_mod.fetch_all_arxiv = orig_fetch

    asyncio.run(_run_coordinator_dedup())


def test_8_pdfs_concurrent_parsing_benchmark():
    """Acceptance criterion: 8 PDFs parsed concurrently in < 2 seconds."""
    async def _run():
        settings = get_settings()
        files = []
        for i in range(1, 9):
            sections = {
                "Abstract": f"Abstract for simulated benchmark paper {i}.\n" * 10,
                "Methodology": f"Methods section discussing architecture {i}.\n" * 15,
                "Results": f"Experimental results demonstrating performance {i}.\n" * 15,
                "References": "Reference 1.\n",
            }
            data = _create_test_pdf_bytes(sections, title=f"Benchmark Paper {i}")
            files.append((f"paper_{i}.pdf", data))

        start = time.perf_counter()
        batch = await ingest_all(files, [], settings)
        duration = time.perf_counter() - start

        assert len(batch.papers) == 8
        assert duration < 2.0, f"Concurrent parsing took {duration:.2f}s, expected < 2.0s"
        assert [p.paper_id for p in batch.papers] == [f"p{i}" for i in range(1, 9)]

    asyncio.run(_run())

import asyncio
from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, patch, MagicMock
import pytest
from openai import (
    AuthenticationError,
    RateLimitError,
    APITimeoutError,
)
from app.config import Settings
from app.errors import KairosError
from app.schemas import ErrorCode
from app.services.llm.client import LLMClient
from app.services.llm.json_utils import parse_json_object, InvalidJSON
from app.services.llm.providers import Provider, build_provider_chain


def test_parse_json_object_valid():
    """Verifies that valid JSON objects parse cleanly."""
    res = parse_json_object('{"title": "Test Paper", "year": 2024}')
    assert res == {"title": "Test Paper", "year": 2024}


def test_parse_json_object_code_fenced():
    """Verifies that markdown code-fenced JSON is unpacked."""
    fenced = """```json
    {
      "status": "ok",
      "findings": [1, 2, 3]
    }
    ```"""
    res = parse_json_object(fenced)
    assert res == {"status": "ok", "findings": [1, 2, 3]}


def test_parse_json_object_trailing_note_with_braces():
    """Resolves F-05: Verifies that trailing commentary with braces does not break JSON decoding."""
    text_with_trailing = '{"claims": []}\nNote: omitted {Fig 1} and {Table 2}.'
    res = parse_json_object(text_with_trailing)
    assert res == {"claims": []}


def test_parse_json_object_invalid_syntax():
    """Rejects malformed JSON syntax with InvalidJSON."""
    with pytest.raises(InvalidJSON):
        parse_json_object('{"title": "Unclosed string')

    with pytest.raises(InvalidJSON):
        parse_json_object("Just a regular sentence with no braces.")


def test_build_provider_chain_skips_unconfigured():
    """Verifies that providers with empty keys/tokens are skipped."""
    settings = Settings(
        llm_provider_chain="groq,huggingface,ollama",
        groq_api_key="",  # empty
        hf_token="test_hf_token",  # present
        ollama_base_url="http://localhost:11434/v1",  # present
    )
    chain = build_provider_chain(settings)
    names = [p.name for p in chain]
    assert "groq" not in names
    assert "huggingface" in names
    assert "ollama" in names


def test_llm_client_success():
    """Verifies successful completion without fail-over."""
    async def _run():
        settings = Settings(llm_max_concurrency=2, llm_max_retries=2)
        provider = Provider("groq", "https://api.groq.com", "fake_key", "llama-3.1-8b-instant", "MIT", "http://mit.edu")
        client = LLMClient(settings, providers=[provider])

        mock_choice = MagicMock()
        mock_choice.message.content = '{"result": "success"}'
        mock_resp = MagicMock()
        mock_resp.choices = [mock_choice]

        with patch("app.services.llm.client.AsyncOpenAI") as mock_openai_cls:
            mock_inst = AsyncMock()
            mock_inst.chat.completions.create.return_value = mock_resp
            mock_inst.__aenter__.return_value = mock_inst
            mock_inst.__aexit__.return_value = None
            mock_openai_cls.return_value = mock_inst

            data, prov, model = await client.complete_json(
                system="sys",
                user="usr",
                max_tokens=500,
                temperature=0.1,
                validate=lambda d: d["result"],
            )
            assert data == "success"
            assert prov == "groq"
            assert model == "llama-3.1-8b-instant"

    asyncio.run(_run())


def test_llm_client_corrective_retry_on_invalid_json():
    """Verifies that an initial invalid JSON response triggers a corrective retry that succeeds."""
    async def _run():
        settings = Settings(llm_max_concurrency=2, llm_max_retries=2)
        provider = Provider("groq", "https://api.groq.com", "fake_key", "llama-3.1-8b-instant", "MIT", "http://mit.edu")
        client = LLMClient(settings, providers=[provider])

        mock_resp1 = MagicMock()
        mock_resp1.choices = [MagicMock(message=MagicMock(content='broken json text { '))]

        mock_resp2 = MagicMock()
        mock_resp2.choices = [MagicMock(message=MagicMock(content='{"status": "recovered"}'))]

        with patch("app.services.llm.client.AsyncOpenAI") as mock_openai_cls:
            mock_inst = AsyncMock()
            mock_inst.chat.completions.create.side_effect = [mock_resp1, mock_resp2]
            mock_inst.__aenter__.return_value = mock_inst
            mock_inst.__aexit__.return_value = None
            mock_openai_cls.return_value = mock_inst

            data, prov, model = await client.complete_json(
                system="sys",
                user="usr",
                max_tokens=500,
                temperature=0.1,
                validate=lambda d: d["status"],
            )
            assert data == "recovered"
            assert mock_inst.chat.completions.create.call_count == 2

    asyncio.run(_run())


def test_llm_client_401_disabling_and_failover():
    """Verifies that a 401 AuthenticationError disables that provider and fails over to the next."""
    async def _run():
        settings = Settings(llm_max_concurrency=2, llm_max_retries=1)
        p1 = Provider("groq", "https://api.groq.com", "bad_key", "llama-3.1-8b-instant", "MIT", "http://mit.edu")
        p2 = Provider("hf", "https://api.hf.co", "good_token", "meta-llama/Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct", "MIT", "http://mit.edu")
        client = LLMClient(settings, providers=[p1, p2])

        mock_resp_success = MagicMock()
        mock_resp_success.choices = [MagicMock(message=MagicMock(content='{"source": "hf"}'))]

        with patch("app.services.llm.client.AsyncOpenAI") as mock_openai_cls:
            mock_client_p1 = AsyncMock()
            mock_client_p1.chat.completions.create.side_effect = AuthenticationError(
                "Invalid API key", response=MagicMock(status_code=401), body={}
            )
            mock_client_p1.__aenter__.return_value = mock_client_p1
            mock_client_p1.__aexit__.return_value = None

            mock_client_p2 = AsyncMock()
            mock_client_p2.chat.completions.create.return_value = mock_resp_success
            mock_client_p2.__aenter__.return_value = mock_client_p2
            mock_client_p2.__aexit__.return_value = None

            mock_openai_cls.side_effect = [mock_client_p1, mock_client_p2]

            data, prov, model = await client.complete_json(
                system="sys",
                user="usr",
                max_tokens=500,
                temperature=0.1,
                validate=lambda d: d["source"],
            )
            assert data == "hf"
            assert prov == "hf"
            # Verify p1 is permanently disabled
            assert "groq" in client.disabled

    asyncio.run(_run())


def test_llm_client_secret_key_redaction():
    """Missing Test 3: Verifies that API keys are completely redacted from exception messages and details."""
    async def _run():
        settings = Settings(llm_max_concurrency=2, llm_max_retries=1)
        secret_key = "gsk_secret_live_key_9876543210"
        p1 = Provider("groq", "https://api.groq.com", secret_key, "llama-3.1-8b-instant", "MIT", "http://mit.edu")
        client = LLMClient(settings, providers=[p1])

        with patch("app.services.llm.client.AsyncOpenAI") as mock_openai_cls:
            mock_inst = AsyncMock()
            # Simulate unexpected exception containing raw secret token
            mock_inst.chat.completions.create.side_effect = RuntimeError(f"Connection failed for bearer {secret_key}")
            mock_inst.__aenter__.return_value = mock_inst
            mock_inst.__aexit__.return_value = None
            mock_openai_cls.return_value = mock_inst

            with pytest.raises(KairosError) as exc_info:
                await client.complete_json(
                    system="sys",
                    user="usr",
                    max_tokens=500,
                    temperature=0.1,
                    validate=lambda d: d,
                )

            assert exc_info.value.code == ErrorCode.LLM_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE
            # Assert secret key is nowhere in message or details
            assert secret_key not in exc_info.value.message
            assert secret_key not in exc_info.value.details["last_error"]
            # Assert redacted placeholder is present
            assert "[REDACTED]" in exc_info.value.details["last_error"]

    asyncio.run(_run())


def test_llm_client_all_providers_fail():
    """Verifies that exhaustion of all providers raises KairosError(LLM_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE)."""
    async def _run():
        settings = Settings(llm_max_concurrency=2, llm_max_retries=1)
        p1 = Provider("ollama", "http://localhost:11434/v1", "ollama", "llama3.2:3b", "MIT", "http://mit.edu")
        client = LLMClient(settings, providers=[p1])

        with patch("app.services.llm.client.AsyncOpenAI") as mock_openai_cls:
            mock_inst = AsyncMock()
            mock_inst.chat.completions.create.side_effect = APITimeoutError(request=MagicMock())
            mock_inst.__aenter__.return_value = mock_inst
            mock_inst.__aexit__.return_value = None
            mock_openai_cls.return_value = mock_inst

            with pytest.raises(KairosError) as exc_info:
                await client.complete_json(
                    system="sys",
                    user="usr",
                    max_tokens=500,
                    temperature=0.1,
                    validate=lambda d: d,
                )
            assert exc_info.value.code == ErrorCode.LLM_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE
            assert exc_info.value.status_code == 503

    asyncio.run(_run())

FILE: backend/tests/test_quote_verifier.py (133 lines)
